|| śrīgaṇeśāya namaḥ ||

{atha kāmaratnam} 

{hindīvyākhyāvibhūṣitam} 

[prathamopadeśaḥ]

[maṅgalācaraṇam] 

yasyeśvarasya vimalaṃ caraṇāravindaṃ saṃsevyate vibudhaviddhamadhuvratena | 
nirvāṇasūcakaguṇāṣṭakavarga pūrṇaṃ taṃ śaṃṅkaraṃ sakaladuḥkhaharaṃ namāmi || 1 ||


devyuvāca 

jñātaṃ tava prasādena yathā kālasya bandhanam |
mantrasya sārasaṃbhūtamidānīmauṣadhaṃ vada ||

auṣadhānyapyanekāni manujānāṃ hitāya vai |
pūrvaṃ tu yattvayā proktaṃ pratyakṣaṃ kathayasva me || 1 ||

īśvara uvāca 

kāmaratnamidaṃ citraṃ nānātantrārṇavānmayā |
vaśyādiyakṣiṇīmaṃtrasādhanāntaṃ samuddhṛtam || 1 ||

tithinakṣatravāreṇa ṛtubhedaiḥ parigrahaḥ |
khananotpāṭanaṃ mantraiḥ kārayedvai cikitsakaḥ || 2 ||

auṣadhaṃ kālayogena gṛhṇāti paramaṃ balam |
śaraddhemāntike devi tvaco mūlaparigrahaḥ || 3 ||

śiśire ca phalaṃ samyaṅmūlaṃ sārasamanvitam |
vasante puṣpapatre ca grīṣme ca phalabījake || 4 ||

svakāle balavanto'pi varṣāsu taravaḥ sadā |
mūle śuṣke balaṃ cārddhaṃ malādau bhiṣaje tathā || 5 ||

grīṣmavārṣikayoretaccharatsaṃpūrṇatā bhavet |
vṛkṣādīnāṃ phalaṃ bījaṃ svakīye cārtave tathā || 6 ||

phalapuṣpalatā hyete svakāle balinastathā |
niśāyāṃ vanajā vīryā jalajā balino divā || 7 ||


ṣaṭkarmāṇi 

śāntivaśyastambhanāni dveṣaṇoccāṭane tathā |
māraṇāntāni śaṃsanti ṣaṭkarmāṇi manīṣiṇaḥ || 1 ||

vaśyākarṣaṇakarmmāṇi vasante yojayetpriye |
grīṣme vidveṣaṇaṃ kuryātprāvṛṣi stambhanaṃ tathā || 2 ||

śiśire māraṇañcaiva śāntikaṃ śaradi smṛtam |
hemante pauṣṭikaṃ kuryāduktakarmaviśāradaiḥ || 3 ||

vasante caiva pūrvāhṇe grīṣme madhyāhna ucyate |
varṣā jñeyā'parāhṇe tu pradoṣe śiśirastathā |
arddharātre śaratkāle uṣā hemanta ucyate || 4 ||

ṛtavaḥ kathitā hyete sarve hyeva krameṇa tu |
tadvihīnā na siddhyanti prayatnenāpi kurvataḥ ||
ananyakaraṇātte hi dhruvaṃ siddhyanti nānyathā || 5 ||

iti ṛtunirṇayaḥ 

atha tithinirṇayaḥ 

vaśīkaraṇakarmāṇi saptamyāṃ sādhayed budhaḥ |
tṛtīyāyāṃ trayodaśyāṃ tathākarṣaṇakarma vai || 6 ||

uccāṭanaṃ dvitīyāyāṃ ṣaṣṭhyāñcaiva prakārayet |
stambhanañca caturdaśyāṃ caturthyāṃ pratipadyapi || 7 ||

mohanantu navamyāñca tathāṣṭamyāṃ prayojayet |
dvādaśyāṃ māraṇañcaivamekādaśyāṃ tathaiva ca || 8 ||

pañcamyāṃ paurṇamāsyāñca yojayecchāntikādikam |
sarvavidyāprasiddhyarthaṃ tithayaḥ kathitāḥ kramāt || 9 ||

iti tithinirṇayaḥ |

atha vārāḥ 

śukre lakṣmīḥ śanau vaśyaṃ ravau māraṇakarma ca |
uccāṭanaṃ budhe bhaume vidveṣādi śubhaṃ bhavet || 1 ||

stambhanaṃ mohanañcaiva vaśīkaraṇamuttamam |
māhendre vāruṇe caiva karttavyamiha viddhidam ||
vidveṣoccāṭanaṃ vahnivāyuyogena kārayet || 2 ||

jyeṣṭhā caivottarāṣāḍhā hyanurādhā ca rohiṇī |
māhendramaṇḍalāsthāśca proktāḥ karmaprasiddhidāḥ || 3 ||

syāduttarābhādrapadā mūlā śatabhiṣā tathā |
pūrvābhādrapadāśleṣā jñeyā vāruṇamadhyagāḥ |
pūrvāṣāḍhā ca tatkarmmasiddhidā śambhunā smṛtāḥ || 4 ||

svātī hasto mṛgaśirāścitrā cottaraphālgunī |
puṣya punarvasurvahnimaṇḍalasthāḥ prakīrtitāḥ || 5 ||

aśvinī bharaṇī cārdrā dhaniṣṭhā śravaṇaṃ maghā |
viśākhā kṛttikā pūrvāphālgunī revatī tathā |
vāyumaṇḍalamadhyasthāstattatkarmaprasiddhidāḥ || 6 ||

iti māhendrādinirṇayaḥ |

śāntikaṃ pauṣṭikañcaiva hyabhicārika karmma ca || 7 ||

tarjanyādisamārūḍhaṃ kuryādyatnātkramaṃ sudhīḥ |
tatrāṅguṣṭhāsamārūḍhā sarvakarma hyubhe tathā || 8 ||

iti aṅgulinirṇayaḥ |

atha auṣadhīnāṃbalābalavicāraḥ 

vidhimantrasamāyuktamauṣadhaṃ saphalaṃ bhavet |
vidhimantravihīnaṃ tu kāṣṭhavadbheṣajaṃ bhavet || 9 ||

ekānte tu śubhāraṇye tiṣṭhatyeva yadauṣadham |
kāryaviddhirbhavettena vīryamasti ca tatra vai || 10 ||

valmīkakūparathyātarutaladevālayaśmaśāneṣu |
jātā vidhinā vihitā oṣadhayaḥ siddhidā na syuḥ || 11 ||

jalajīrṇamagnikavalitamakālajātaṃ kṛmikṣataśarīram |
nyūnaṃ tathādhikaṃ vā dravyamadravyaṃ jagurbhiṣajaḥ || 12 ||

iti auṣadhīnāṃbalāvalavicāraḥ |

atha mūlikāgrahaṇavidhiḥ |

bhūtādiyuktamabhyarcya girīśaṃ prātarutthitaiḥ |
śrāddhairupāsitairvāpi saṃgrāhyaṃ sarvamauṣadham || 1 ||

ityevaṃ sarvamūlānāṃ vidhirmantraśca kathyate |
ādau vṛkṣasya mūlañca gatvā tamabhimaṃtrayet || 2 ||

oṃ vetālāśca piśācāśca rākṣasāśca sarīsṛpāḥ |
apasarpantu te sarve vṛkṣādasmācchivājñayā || 3 ||

tato namaskāraḥ

oṃ namaste'mṛtasambhūte balavīryavivarddhini |
balamāyuśca me dehi pāpānme trāhi dūrataḥ || 4 ||

tataḥ khananam 

yena tvāṃ khanate brahmā yena tvāṃ khanate bhṛguḥ |
yena hīndro'tha varuṇo yena tvāmapacakrame || 5 ||

tenāhaṃ khanayiṣyāmi mantrapūtena pāṇinā |
mā pātemāni patite mā te tejonyathā bhavet || 6 ||

atraiva tiṣṭha kalyāṇi mama kāryakarī bhava |
mama kārye kṛte siddhe tataḥ svargaṃ gamiṣyasi || 7 ||

utkhanana maṃtra anena mantreṇādityavāre puṣyanakṣatre vā puṣyārkayoge vā sarvā auṣadhīrutpāṭaye | oṃ hrīṃ kṣauṃ phaṭ svāhā anena mūlikāṃ chedayet | iti mūlikāgrahaṇavidhih |

oṃ hrīṃ caṇḍe hūṁ phaṭ svāhā |

oṃ hrīṃ kṣauṃ phaṭ svāhā |

oṃ hrīṃ caṇḍe hūṃ phaṭ svāhā 

iti mūlikāgrahaṇavidhiḥ |

atha bandāgrahaṇavidhiḥ 

mantraḥ 

oṃ vanadaṇḍe mahādaṇḍāya svāhā |

oṃ śūdrī (sūtrī)kapālamālinī svāhā 

pratyekaṃ saptadhā japtvā bandā grāhyā | tataḥ kāryasiddhiḥ |

iti bandāgrahaṇavidhiḥ |

ityevaṃ sarvavidyānāṃ siddhaye kālanirṇayaḥ |
kathitaṃ cātra yatnena mūlikāgrahaṇādikam || 8 ||

atha vaśīkaraṇam 

tatra sarvajana vaśīkaraṇam 

varṇānāmuttamaṃ varṇaṃ mantrasthānaṃ tathaiva ca |
oṅkāraśirasaṃ cāpi oṅkāraśirasaṃ tataḥ || 1 ||

adhobhāge ca rephañca dattvā mantraṃ samuddharet |
nirāmiṣānnabhoktā ca japtavyo mantra eva ca || 2 ||

kroṃ proṃ vroṃ anena mantreṇa(-)

asādhyāścāpi rājānaḥ putramitrāśca bāndhavāḥ |
ye me gotrasamutpannāḥ paśavo ye ca sarvataḥ || 3 ||

te sarve vaśatāṃ yānti sahasrārddhasya jāpanāt |
pṛṣṭvā dṛṣṭvā ca ye sādhyā gṛhītvā nāma tatra vai || 4 ||

ityādikāḥ sarvamantrā grāhyā bhaktyā gurostadā |
siddhyanti sarvakāryāṇi nānyathā siddhibhāgbhavet || 5 ||

mantraḥ

oṃ namaḥ kaṭavikaṭaghorarūpiṇīsvāhā || anena mantreṇa saptābhimantritaṃ bhakta piḍaṃ yasya nāmnā saptāhaṃ khādyate sa dhruvameva vaśyo bhavati ||

oṃ namaḥ kaṭavikaṭaghorarūpiṇī svāhā |

mantraḥ

oṃ vaśyamukhī rājamukhī svāhā || anena mukhaprakṣālanātsarve vaśyā bhavanti ||

mantra śloka 

oṃ rājamukhi vaśyamukhi svāhā || vāmahastetailaṃ saṃsthāpya anāmikayā tridhā āmantrya punarmūlamantraṃ tridhā paṭhitvā prātaḥkāle śayyāyāṃ sthitvā mukhakeśādau vilepayet | tadā sarvejanā vaśyā bhavanti vyāghropi na khādati ||

mantraḥ

oṃ cāmuṇḍe jaya jaya stambhaya stambhaya jaṃbhaya jaṃbhaya mohaya mohaya sarvasattvānnamaḥ svāhā anena puṣpāṇyabhimaṃtrya yasmai dīyate sa vaśyo bhavati ||

oṃ cāmuṇḍe jaya jaya stambhaya stambhaya jambhaya jambhaya mohaya mohaya sarva sattvānamah savāhā |

ekacittasthito mantrīmantra japtvāyutatrayam |
tataḥ kṣobhayate lokān darśanādeva sādhakaḥ || 6 ||

bhūtākhyavaṭamūlaṃ ca jalena saha gharṣayet |
vibhūtyā saṃyutaṃ mantraṃ tilakaṃ lokavaśyakṛt || 7 ||

puṣye punarnavāmūlaṃ kare saptābhimantritam || baddhvā sarvatra pūjyaḥ syānmantrastavatraiva kathyate || aiṃ rauṃ ḍaṃ kṣobhaya bhagavati tvaṃ svāhā | mantrabhimamuktayogasya pūrvamayutadvayaṃ japettataḥ || 8 ||

apāmārgasya mūlantu peṣayedrocanena ca |
lalāṭe tilakaṃ kuryādvaśī kuryājjagatrayam || 9 ||

oṃ aiṃ rauṃ ḍaṃ kṣobhaya bhagavati tvaṃ svāhā |

oṃ namaḥ kandarpaśaravijālinimālini sarvalokavaśaṃkari svāhā || iti mantrayuktayogasyāṣṭottarasahasraṃ japettataḥ siddhiḥ ||

oṃ namaḥ kandarpaśara vijālinimālini sarvalokavaśaṃkari svāhā |

kṛṣṇapakṣe caturdaśyāmaṣṭamyāṃ vāhyupoṣitaḥ |
baliṃ dattvā samuddhṛtya sahadevīṃ sacūrṇayet ||

tāmbūlena tu taccūrṇaṃ dattaṃ vaśyakaraṃ dhruvam |
snāne lepe ca taccūrṇaṃ yojyaṃ vaśyakaraṃ bhavet || 10 ||

rocanāsahadevībhyāṃ tilakaṃ lokavaśyakṛt |
śirasā dhārettacca cūrṇaṃ sarvatra vaśyakṛt || 11 ||

mukhe kṣiptvā ca tanmūlaṃ kaṭyāṃ baddhvā ca kāmayet |
yāṃ nārīṃ sā bhavedvaśyā mantrayogena kathyate ||

oṃ namo bhagavatimātaṅgeśvarī sarvamukharañjini sarveṣāṃ mahāmāye mātaṅgi kumārike lahalahajihve sarvalokavaśaṃkari svāhā || sahasraṃ japtvā uktayogānāṃ siddhiḥ || 12 ||

śvetaparājitāmūlaṃ candragraste samuddhṛtam |
rañjitākṣo narastena vaśīkuryājjagatrayam |
tanmūlaṃ rocanāyuktaṃ tilakena jagadvaśam || 13 ||

oṃ namo bhagavatimātaṅgeśvari sarvamukharañjini sarveṣāṃ mahāmāye mātaṅgi kumārike lahalahajihve sarvaloka vaśaṃkari svāhā 

grāhyaṃ kṛṣṇatrayodaśyāṃ śvetaguñjīyamūlakam |
tāmbūlena pradātavyaṃ sarvalokavaśaṃkaram || 14 ||

śilārocanātāmbūlaṃ vāriṇā tilake kṛte |
saṃbhāṣaṇena sarveṣāṃ vaśīkaraṇamuttamam |
svarṇena veṣṭitaṃ kṛtvā tenaiva tilake kṛte || 15 ||

dṛṣṭamātre vaśaṃ yāti nārī vā puruṣo'pi vā |

oṃ vajrakiraṇeśiverakṣabhavemamādya amṛtaṃ kuru kuru svāhā |
imaṃ maṃtramuktayogena sahasraṃ japettataḥ siddhiḥ || 16 ||

oṃ vajrakiraṇe śive rakṣa bhavemamādya amṛtaṃ kuru kuru svāhā |

hṛtpādacakṣurnāsānāṃ malaṃ pūge pradāpayet |
tatpūgaṃ khādyate yena yāvajjīvaṃ vaśībhavet || 17 ||

maṃtrābhimaṃtritaṃ kṛtvā daṇḍendīvaramūlakam |
rocanābhistāmrapātre mṛṣṭvā netradvayāñjanāt ||
priyo bhavati sarveṣāṃ dṛṣṭamātre na saṃśayaḥ || 18 ||

tanmūlaṃ madhusaṃyuktaṃ lalāṭe tilake kṛte |
tāmbūle vā pradātavyaṃ vaśīkaraṇamuttamam || 19 ||

tanmūlaṃ rañjanotthaṃ vā mūlaṃ piṣṭvā prayojayet |
tāmbūlena tu tadbhukte dhruvaṃ vaśyaṃ samānayet |
oṃ piṅgalāyai namaḥ anena maṃtreṇābhimantryoktayogān sādhayet || 20 ||

oṃ piṅgalāyai namaḥ 

raktagṛdhrobhayaṃ netraṃ netraṃ vā kṛṣṇapecakam |
kṛṣṇapecikamāhṛtya tattailena pradīpakam || 21 ||

kṛtvā ca madhunā liptvā varti kajjalapātane |
tena netrāñjanaṃ kṛtvā trailokyaṃ vaśamānayet || 22 ||

devadālī ca siddhārthaṃ guṭikāṃ kārayed budhaḥ |
mukhe niḥkṣipya sarveṣāṃ priyo bhavati nānyathā || 23 ||

bhṛṅgamūlaṃ mukhe kṣiptvā sarvaiḥ saṃpūjito bhavet |
rohiṇyāṃ vaṭavandākaṃ saṅgrāhya dhārayetkare |
vaśyaṃ karoti sakalaṃ viśvāmitreṇa bhāṣitam || 24 ||

kuṅkumaṃ tagaraṃ kuṣṭhaṃ haritālaṃ samaṃ trayam |
anāmikāyā raktena tilakaṃ lokavaśyakṛt || 25 ||

viṣṇukrāntā śubhā bhṛṅgī śvadraṃṣṭrā mūlarocanām |
piṣṭvā tu vaṭikāṃ kṛtvā tilakaṃ vaśyakṛtparam || 26 ||

puṣyoddhṛtaṃ śvetabhānumūlaṃ mūtrairajābhavaiḥ |
vaṭikāṃ kārayetprājñastilakena jagadvaśam || 27 ||

ajāraktena tanmūlaṃ puṣyārke peṣayed budhaḥ |
kajjalaṃ pātayitvā tu cakṣuṣī rañjayannaraḥ ||
trailokyaṃ vaśatāṃ yāṃti dṛṣṭamātre na saṃśayaḥ || 28 ||

mūlantu śravaṇā(-)ṛkṣe piṇḍītagarasaṃbhavam |
saṃgrāhya dhārayedvaśyaṃ kurute sakalaṃ jagat || 29 ||

kṛṣṇāparājitāmūlaṃ puṣyeṇoddhṛtya cūrṇayet |
goghṛtena samāloḍya kajjalaṃ dhārayed budhaḥ ||
tenaivāñjitamātreṇa vaśīkuryājjagattrayam || 30 ||

putrajīvakapatraṃ ca tilakaṃ rocanāyutam |
priyo bhavati sarveṣāṃ naraḥ kṛtvā lalāṭake || 31 ||

śvetāparājitāmūlaṃ tathā śvetajavāgrajā |
nāsāgre tilakaṃ kṛtvā vaśīkuryyānna saṃśayaḥ || 32 ||

mañjiṣṭhayadavacāśitasūryamūlaiḥ svīyāṅgaśoṇitayuttaiḥ samakuṣṭhakaiśca |
kṛtvā lalāṭaphalake tilakaṃ kṛtajño lokatrayaṃ vaśayati kṣaṇamātrakeṇa || 33 ||

śambhorjjalaṃ ca madhukaṃ ca kṛtāñjaliñca 
havyaṃ samaṃ nijaśarīramalena miśram |
ālepabhakṣaṇavidhau tilake kṛte vā 
yogo'yamevabhuvanāni vaśīkaroti || 34 ||

mūlaṃ jaṭā tagarameṣaviṣāṇikānāṃ 
pañcāṅgajaṃ nijaśarīramalaṃ tathaiva |
ekīkṛtāni madhunā divase kujasya 
kurvanti vakratilakena vaśaṃ jaganti || 35 ||

bhṛṅgasya pakṣayugalaṃ śukamāṃsayuktaṃ 
svānāmikārudhirakarṇamalaṃ svabījam | 
etāni lepavidhināpyatha bhakṣaṇādvā 
kurvanti vaśyamakhilaṃ jagadapyakasmāt || 36 ||

tālīśakuṣṭhatagareḥ parilipya varti 
siddhārthatailasahitāṃ dṛḍhapaṭṭavastrām |
puṃsaḥ kapālaphalake vinipātitena 
tenāñjanena vaśatāṃ kila yāti lokaḥ || 37 ||

gorocanā padmapatraṃ priyaṅgu raktacandanam |
ekīkṛtyāñjayennetraṃ yaṃ paśyati vaśī bhavet || 38 ||

nāsānetramalaṃ pādamalaṃ guvākamiśritam |
maṃtritaṃ dīyate yasmai vaśībhavati niścitam || 39 ||

oṃ piṅgalāyai namaḥ 

atha rājavaśīkaraṇam

kuṃkumaṃ candanañcaiva rocanaṃ śaśimiśritam |
gavāṃ kṣīreṇa tilakaṃ rājavaśyakaraṃ dhruvam || 1 ||

oṃ hrīṃ saḥ amukaṃ me vaśamānaya svāhā ||
pūrvameva sahasraṃ japtvā'nena maṃtreṇa saptābhimaṃtritatilakaṃ kāryam ||

oṃ hrīṃ saḥ amukaṃ me vaśamānaya svāhā |

caṃpakasya tu vandākaṃ kare baddhvā prayatnataḥ |
saṃgṛhya bharaṇī(-)ṛkṣe puṣyarkṣe vā vidhānataḥ || 2 ||

rājānaṃ tatkṣaṇādeva manuṣyo vaśamānayet |
kare sudarśanāmūlaṃ vaddhvā rājapriyo bhavet || 3 ||

iti rājavaśīkaraṇam |

atha strīvaśīkaraṇam 

puṣye puṣpaṃ ca saṃgṛhya bharaṇyāṃ tu phalaṃ tathā |
śākhāṃ caiva viśākhāyāṃ haste patraṃ tathaiva ca || 1 ||

mūle mūlaṃ samuddhṛtya kṛṣṇonmattasya tatkramāt |
piṣṭvā karpūrasaṃyuktaṃ kuṃkumaṃ rocanāsamam || 
tilākāt strī vaśaṃ yāti yadi sākṣādarundhatī || 2 ||

kākajaṅghā vacā kuṣṭhaṃ śukraśoṇitamiśritam |
taddatte bhojane bālā śmaśāne roditi sadā |
oṃ namo bhagavate rudrāya cāmuṇḍe amukīṃ me vaśamānaya svāhā | uktayogānāmayameva maṃtraḥ || 3 ||

oṃ namo bhagavate rudrāya oṃ cāmuṇḍe amukīṃ me vaśamānaya svāhā |

prātarmukhantu prakṣālya saptāvārābhimaṃtritam |
yasyā nāmnā pibettoyaṃ sā strī vaśyā bhaved dhruvam || 4 ||

oṃ namaḥ kṣiprakāmini amukīṃ me vaśamānaya svāhā |

oṃ namaḥ kṣiprakāminī amukīṃ me vaśamānaya svāhā || 4 ||

kṛṣṇāparājitāmūlaṃ tāmbūlena samāyutam |
avaśyāyai striyai dadyādvaśyā bhavati nānyathā ||
oṃ hūṃ svāhā | anenābhimantrya dadyāt || 5 ||

sādhyasādhakanāmnā tu kṛtvā saptābhimantritam |
dīyate kusumaṃ yasyai sā vaśyā bhavati dhruvam || 6 ||

susādhito hyayaṃ mantraṃ avaśyaṃ phaladāyakaḥ |
tasmādimaṃ prayatnena sādhayemantramuttamam || 7 ||

oṃ hūṃ svāhā 

viśākhāyāntu vandākaṃ maṅgale ca samāharet |
haste baddhvā tu kurute vaśagāṃ varayoṣitam || 8 ||

oṃ pāte vajrāya svāhā 

kṛṣṇotpalaṃ madhukarasya ca pakṣayugmaṃ mūlaṃ tathā tagarajaṃ sitakākajaṅghā |
yasyāḥ śirogatamidaṃ vihitaṃ vicūrṇaṃ dāsī bhavejjhaṭiti sā taruṇī vicitram || 9 ||

savyena pāṇikamalena ratāvasāne yā retasā nijabhavena vilāsinīnām |
vāmaṃ vilimpati padaṃ sahasaiva yasyā vasyaiva sā bhavanti nātra vikalpabhāvaḥ || 10 ||

sindhūtthamākṣikapottamalāni piṣṭvā liṅgaṃ vilipya taruṇīṃ ramate navoḍhām | 
sā'nyaṃ na yāti puruṣaṃ manasāpi nūnaṃ dāsī bhavediti manoharadivyamūrttiḥ || 11 ||

gorocanāśiśiradīdhitiśaṃbhubījaiḥ kāśmīracandanayutaiḥ kanakadravaiśca |
liptvā dhvajaṃ pariramatyabalāṃ naro yāṃ tasyā sa eva hṛdaye mukuṭatvameti || 12 ||

puṣye rudrajaṭāmūlaṃ mukhasthaṃ kārayed budhaḥ |
tāmbūlādau pradātavyaṃ vaśyā bhavati niścitam || 13 ||

tathaiva pāṭalāmūlaṃ tāmbūlena tu vaśyakṛt |
tripatrabhaṇṭikāmūlaṃ piṣṭvā gātre tu saṃkṣipet |
yasyāḥ sā vaśatāṃ yāti bindumātreṇa tatkṣaṇāt || 14 ||

svakīyakāmamādāya kāmadevaṃ smaretpunaḥ |
taruṇyā hṛdaye dattaṃ tatkṣaṇātstrī vaśā bhavet || 15 ||

gilitvā pāradaṃ kiṃcidramyate nāyikā yadi |
prāṇānte'pi ca sā nārī taṃ naraṃ na vimuñcati || 16 ||

kāmākrāntena cittena māsārddhaṃ japate niśi |
avaśyaṃ kurute vaśyaṃ prasanno viśvaceṭakaḥ ||

aiṃ piṃ sthāṃ klīṃ kāmapiśācinī śīghraṃ 
amukīṃ grāhaya grāhaya kāmena mama rūpeṇa nakhairvidāraya 
nakhairvidāraya vidrāvaya vidrāvaya snehena bandhaya bandhaya śrīṃ phaṭ | 
ayuta dvayena siddhiḥ || 17 ||

nāgāpuṣpaṃ priyaṅguñca tagaraṃ padmakeśaram |
jaṭāmāṃsīṃ samaṃ nimbaṃ cūrṇayenmaṃtravittamaḥ || 18 ||

aiṃ piṃ sthāṃ klīṃ kāmapiśācinī śīghraṃ amukīṃ grāhaya grāhaya kāmena mama rūpeṇa nakhairvidāraya nakhairvidāraya vidrāvaya vidrāvaya snehena bandhaya bandhaya śrīṃ phaṭ |

svāṅgaṃ tu dhūpayettena bhajate kāmavatstriyaḥ |

dhūpamantraḥ 

oṃ mūlī mūlī mahāmūlī sarva saṃkṣobhaya saṃkṣobhaya ebhya upadravebhyaḥ svāhā | iti ||

pānīyasyāñjalīn sapta kṛtvā vidyāmimāṃ japet |
sālaṅkārāṃ naraḥ kanyāṃ labhate māsamātrataḥ || 19 ||

oṃ viśvāvasurnāmagandharvaḥ kanyānāmadhipatissurūpāṃ 
sālaṃkṛtāṃ kanyāṃ dehi namastasmai viśvāvasave svāhā 
kanyā gṛhe śālakāṣṭhaṃ kṣipedekādaśāṃgulam |
ṛkṣe ca pūrvaphālgunyāṃ yastāṃ kanyāṃ prayacchati || 20 ||

atha pativaśīkaraṇam 

khañjarīṭasya māṃsaṃ tu madhunā saha peṣayet |
anena yonilepena patirdāso bhaved dhruvam ||

pañcāṅgaṃ dāḍimaṃ piṣṭvā śveta sarṣapasaṃyutam |
yonilepātpatiṃ dāsaṃ karotyapi ca durbhagā || 1 ||

karpūraṃ devadāruṃ ca sakṣaudraṃ pūrvavatphalam |
oṃ kāmakāmamālini patiṃ me vaśamānaya ṭhaḥ ṭhaḥ |
ukta yogānāṃ saptābhimaṃtrite siddhiḥ || 2 ||

oṃ kāmakāmamālini patiṃ me vaśamānaya ṭhaḥ ṭhaḥ |

rocanāṃ matsyapittaṃ ca piṣṭvāpi tilake kṛte |
vāmahastakaniṣṭhāyāṃ patirdāso bhaved dhruvam |
svaśoṇitaṃ rocanayā tilakaṃ pativaśyakṛt || 3 ||

citrakasya tu puṣpāṇi madhuyuktāni kārayet |
khāne pāne pradātavyaṃ pativaśyakaraṃ bhavet |
bhūrjapatraṃ ca madhunā yonilepe patirvaśaḥ || 4 ||

jalaukasāṃ mukhe deyaṃ śambūśaṃkhādicūrṇakam |
taccūrṇaṃ tu samāgṛhya tāmbūlena samāyutam |
dātavyaṃ svāmine bhoktuṃ vaśyo bhavati nānyathā || 5 ||

gorocanānaladakuṃkumabhāvitāyāstasyāḥ sadaiva kurute tilakaṃ vaśitvam | 
vātsyāyanena bahudhā pramadājanānāṃ saubhāgyakṛtyasamaye prakaṭīkṛto'sau || 6 ||

sambhogaśeṣasamaye nijakāntameḍhraṃ yā kāminī spṛśati vāmapadāmbujena | 
tasyāḥ patissapadi vindati dāsabhāvaṃ goṇīsutena kathitaḥ kila yogarājaḥ || 7 ||


iti pativaśīkaraṇam

[iti śrīnityanāthaviracite kāmaratne vaśīkaraṇaṃ nāma prathamopadeśaḥ] || 1 ||


[dvitīyopadeśaḥ] 

athākarṣaṇam 

caturthavarṇamākṛṣya dvitīyavargasaṃsthitam |
kṛtvā trividhahāṃ hāṃtaṃ tadantaṃ he dvitīyakam || 1 ||

aṃkāraśirasaṃ kṛtvā pratyakṣaraprajāpanam |

mantram 

jhāṃ jhāṃ jhāṃ hāṃ hāṃ hāṃ heṃ heṃ ||
sahasrārddhasya jāpena phalaṃ bhavati śāśvatam || 2 ||

mānuṣāsuradevāśca sayakṣoragarākṣasāḥ |
sthāvarā jaṅgamāścaiva ākṛṣṭāste varāṅgane || 3 ||

oṃ jhāṃ jhāṃ jhāṃ hāṃ hāṃ hāṃ heṃ heṃ |

hānte rephaṃ samādāya yakārastu viśeṣataḥ |
akṣaratritayaṃ tacca dvidhā kṛtvā prajāpayet |
bhakṣyaṃ dravyaṃ svahastena kṛtvā maṃtravibhāvanam || 4 ||

dīyate yasya bhakṣyaṃ tatsarveṣāṃ prāṇināṃ śubhe |
te sarve yatra nīyante tatra gacchanti tatkṣaṇāt || 5 ||

hrīṃkāre mantrayetpāśaṃ hūṃkāreṇāṅkuśaṃ tathā |
triphalaṃ vāmagaṃ pāśaṃ dakṣiṇe jvalitāṅkuśam |
saṃdhārya svakare maṃtrī tato mantramimaṃ japet || 6 ||

maṃtra 

oṃ hrīṃ rakṣa rakṣa cāmuṇḍe turu turu amukīmākarṣaya amukīmākarṣaya hrī asya maṃtrasya pūrvamevāyutaṃ japettataḥ siddhiḥ | oṃ cāmuṇḍe jvala jvala prajvala prajvala svāhā | amuṃ maṃtraṃ striyaṃ dṛṣṭvā japettatkṣaṇāt sā strī pṛṣṭhataḥ samāgacchati pūrvamayutaṃ japettatassiddhiḥ || 7 ||

oṃ rakṣa rakṣa cāmuṇḍe turu turu amukīmākarṣaya amukīmākarṣaya hrī |

oṃ cāmuṇḍe jvala jvala prajvala prajvala svāhā |

āśleṣāyāṃ samādāya arjjunasya tu bṛndakam |
ajāmūtreṇa saṃpiṣya strīṇāṃ śirasi dāpayet |
puruṣasya paśūnāṃ vā kṣipedākarṣaṇaṃ bhavet || 8 ||

sādhyāvāmapadasthāṃ tāṃ mṛttikāmāharettataḥ |
kṛkalāsasya raktena pratimāṃ kārayettataḥ || 9 ||

sādhyānāmākṣaraṃ tasyāstadraktaivilikheddhṛdi || 10 ||

mūtrasthāne ca nikhanetsadā tatraiva mūtrayet |
ākarṣayettu tāṃ nārīṃ śatayojanasaṃsthitām || 11 ||

sūryāvartasya mūlaṃ tu pañcamyāṃ grāhayed budhaḥ |
tāmbūlena samaṃ dadyātsvayamāyāti tatkṣaṇāt || 12 ||

ratikarmmakarau grāhyau bhramarau yatnato budhaiḥ |
bhinnau kṛtvā dahettau tu citākāṣṭhaistayoḥ punaḥ || 13 ||

vastreṇa bandhayedbhasma pṛthagvai puṭṭalīdvayam |
tayorekāmajāśṛṅge dṛḍhaṃ baddhvā parīkṣayet || 14 ||

yāṃ yāṃ yāti ca sā meṣī sā pṛthaggṛhyate budhaiḥ |
tadbhasma śirasi nyastaṃ kṣaṇādākarṣayetstriyaḥ |
aparaṃ rakṣayedvastre nocennāyāti kāminī || 15 ||

oṃ kṛṣṇavarṇāya svāhā | imaṃ mantraṃ pūrvamekāyutaṃ japet | tataḥ uktayogamabhimantrya siddhiḥ || 16 ||


[iti śrīnityanāthaviracite kāmaratne ākarṣaṇaṃ nāma dvitīyopadeśaḥ] || 2 ||



[tṛtīyopadeśaḥ] 

atha jayaḥ 

hakāraṃ svarasaṃyuktamukāreṇa supūjitam |
oṃkāreṇa ca saṃpūjya agre phaṭ viniyojayet |
oṃ huṃ phaṭ jeyāgre śatajāpena jito bhavati nānyathā || 1 ||

jeyanāma hṛdi nyasya cakṣuṣā tannimīlya ca |
spṛṣṭvā ca mantrajāpena tatkṣaṇājjitavānasau || 2 ||

gojihvāśikhimūlī vā mukhe śirasi saṃsthitā |
kurute sarvavādeṣu jayaṃ puṣye samuddhṛtā || 3 ||

mārgaśīrṣasya pūrṇāyāṃ śikhimūlaṃ samuddharet |
bāhau śirasi vā dhāryaṃ vivāde vijayī bhavet || 4 ||

girikarṇīṃ śamīṃ guñjāṃ śvetavarṇāṃ samāharet |
candanenānvitaṃ sarvaṃ tilakena jayī bhavet || 5 ||

kanakārkavaṭo vahnirvidrumaḥ pañcamastathā |
tilakaṃ kurute yastu paśyettaṃ pañcadhā ripuḥ || 6 ||

kṛṣṇasarpakapāle tu vasāmṛttikayānvite |
sitaguñjāṃ vapettatra tasyā mūlaṃ samāharet || 7 ||

kṛtatilakaṃ tadā dṛṣṭyā paśyetsvaṃ sambhṛtaṃ ripuḥ || 8 ||

śvagaṇairbhakṣyamāṇaṃ ca patitaṃ ca tato bhuvi |
auṣadhī siṃhikānāma tayā ghṛṣṭo mahārasaḥ || 9 ||

siṃhīkaparddikāmadhye kṣepyastanmūlasaṃyutaḥ |
pidhāya vadanaṃ tasyā sikthakena samanvitaḥ || 10 ||

tasyāṃ vakrasthitāyāṃ tu siṃhavajjñāyate naraḥ |
raṇe rājakule dyūte vivāde cāparājitaḥ |
madonmatto gajastasya darśanena parāṅmukhaḥ || 11 ||

vyāghrīrasena saṃghṛṣṭaḥ pārado mūlasaṃyutaḥ |
pūrvavatsādhayedvyāghrīphalaṃ caiva tathāvidham || 12 ||

kare sudarśanāmūlaṃ baddhvā rājakule jayī |
jayāmūlaṃ rājakule mukhe saṃsthaṃ jayapradam || 13 ||

ārdrāyāṃ vaṭavandākaṃ haste baddhvāparājitaḥ |
tadṛkṣe cūtavandākaṃ gṛhītvā dhārayetkare ||
saṃgrāme jayamāpnoti jayāṃ smṛtvā jayī bhavet || 14 ||

kokāyā nayanaṃ vāmaṃ guḍalohena veṣṭayet |
mukhe prakṣipya ca naraḥ sarvavāde jayī bhavet || 15 ||

kṛttikā ca viśākhā ca bhaumavāreṇa saṃyutā |
taddine ghaṭitaṃ śastraṃ saṃgrāme jayadāyakam || 16 ||

apāmārgarasenaiva yāni śastrāṇi lipyate |
jāyante tāni saṃgrāme vajrasārāṇi niścitam || 17 ||

pūrvoktamaṃtrarājena tāni sarvāṇi maṃtrayet |
sarveṣāmukta yogānāṃ siddhirbhavati te dhruvam || 18 ||

haste'rkalāṅgalīmūlaṃ mūlamantrābhimaṃtritam |
taccūrṇodvarttanānmallo mallānmohayate bahūn || 

mantaḥ 

oṃ namo mahābalaparākrama śastravidyāviśārada śārada amukasya bhujabalaṃ bandhaya bandhaya dṛṣṭiṃ stambhaya stambhaya aṅgāni dhūnaya dhūnaya pātaya pātaya mahītale hūṃ || 19 ||

oṃ namo mahābalaparākrama śastravidyāviśārada śārada amukasya bhuja(-)balaṃ bandhaya bandhaya dṛṣṭiṃ stambhaya stambhaya aṅgāni dhūnaya dhūnaya pātaya pātaya mahītale hūṃ || 18-19 ||

iti jayaprakaraṇam |


atha saubhāgyakaraṇam 

puṣyoddhṛtaṃ sitārkasya mūlaṃ vāmetare bhuje |
baddhvā saubhāgyamāpnoti svāmino durbhagāpi sā || 20 ||

rakta citrakamūlantu somagraste samuddhṛtam |
kṣaudraiḥ piṣṭvā vaṭīḥ kuryāttilakaissubhagāṅganā || 21 ||

kuṅkuma gorocanālaktena yasya nāma saṃlikhya | 
madhumadhye sthāpayet saubhāgyaṃ bhavati || 22 ||

kuṅkumālaktakābhyāṃ bilvapatre yasya nāma saṃlikhya |
madhumadhye sthāpayet saptāhāt sa saubhāgyavān bhavati || 23 ||

iti saubhāgyakaraṇam |


atheśvarādīnāṃ krodhopaśamanam 

(īśvarīya kopaśāntikaraṇam)

oṃ śānte praśānte sarvakruddhopaśamani svāhā 
anena mantreṇa trisaptadhā japtena mukhamārjanāt 
krodhopaśamanaṃ bhavati | prasādaparo bhavati || 24 ||

oṃ śānte praśānte sarvakruddhopaśamani svāhā |

kaṇṭakena tālapatre nāmābhilikhya kardamesthāpayet |
kupitaḥ prasannā bhavati || 25 ||

gorocanā bhūrje yasya nāma samālikhya payomadhye sthāpayet kupitaḥ prasanno bhavati || 26 ||

iti krodhopaśamanam |

atha gajanivāraṇam 

gṛhītvā śubhanakṣatre cūrṇayettāṃ chuchundarīm |
tallepena gajo yāti dūreṇa khalu sammukham || 27 ||

bilvapuṣpasya cūrṇaṃ tu chuchundaryāśca tatsamam |
talliptāṅgaṃ naraṃ dṛṣṭvā dūre gacchati kuñjaraḥ || 28 ||

mūlaṃ markaṭavalyāśca bāhau baddhaṃ ca mūrddhani |
duṣṭadantiharaṃ dūraṃ citraṃ saṃyāti jāyate || 29 ||

śvetāparājitāmūlaṃ hastasthaṃ vārayed gajam |
mūlaṃ triśūlyā vakrasthaṃ gajavaśyakaraṃ dhruvam || 30 ||

iti gajanivāraṇam |

atha vyāghranivāraṇam 

mukhasthaṃ bṛhatīmūlaṃ hastasthaṃ vyāghrabhītijit || 31 ||


mantram 

hrīṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ drauṃ drauṃ hi eti athavā krīṃ hrīṃ oṃ hrīṃ hrīṃ || ityaṣṭākṣaramantreṇaloṣṭaṃ paṭhitvā kṣipet tadā mukhaṃ na cālayati gantumaśaktāḥ ||

hrīṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ drauṃ drauṃ hi eti athavā 
krīṃ hrīṃ oṃ hrīṃ hrīṃ | 

mūlaṃ kṛṣṇacaturdaśyāṃ grāhayellāṅgalībhavam |
hastasthaṃ vyāghrasiṃhādibhayahṛtparikīrtitam || 32 ||


[iti śrīnityanāthaviracite kāmaratne vijayādivyāghranivāraṇaṃ nāma tṛtīyopadeśaḥ] || 3 || 



[caturthopadeśaḥ] 

atha śatrūṇāṃ mukhastambhanam 

meghanādasya mūlaṃ tu mukhasthaṃ tāraveṣṭitam |
paravādī bhavenmūko'thavā yāti digantaram || 1 ||

śvetaguñjotthitaṃ mūlaṃ mukhasthaṃ puṣṭatuṇḍajit ||

oṃ hrīṃ rakṣa cāmuṇḍe turu turu amukaṃ me vaśamānaya svāhā |
ayaṃ cāmuṇḍāmantra uktayogayoḥ siddhikaraḥ || 2 ||

oṃ hrīṃ rakṣa cāmuṇḍe turu turu amukaṃ me vaśamānaya svāhā |

puṣyārke madhuvandākaṃ gṛhītvā prakṣiped budhaḥ |
sabhāmadhye ca sarveṣāṃ mukhastambhaḥ prajāyate || 3 ||

arkapatre haritālarasena yasya nāmābhilikhya udyānamadhye 
īśānakoṇe sthāpayet tasya mukhabandhana stambhanaṃ bhavati || 4 ||

iti mukhastambhanam |


atha nidrāstambhanam 

mūlaṃ bṛhatyā madhukaṃ piṣṭvā nasyaṃ samācaret |
nidrāstambhanametaddhi mūladevena bhāṣitam || 5 ||

iti nidrāstambhanam |

atha naukāstambhanam 

bharaṇyāṃ kṣīrikāṣṭhasya kīlaṃ pañcāṅgulaṃ kṣipet |
naukāmadhye tadā naukāstambhanaṃ jāyate dhruvam || 6 ||

iti naukāstambhanam 

atha śastrastambhanam 

aṅkulī ca jaṭā pāṭhā viṣṇukrāntā ca pāṭalī |
śvetāparājitā puṃsā sa (ha) devī kākajaṅghikā || 7 ||

puṣyarkṣeṇa samuddhṛtya vakre śirasi saṃsthitā |
ekaikaṃ vārayatyeva śastrasaṅghaṭṭane nṛṇām || 8 ||

baddhvā tu vyāghrabhūpālacauraśatrubhayaṃ jayet |
jātīmūlaṃ mukhe kṣiptaṃ śatrustambhanamuttamam || 9 ||

sūryasya grahaṇe cendormūlaṃ cottarago haret |
puṅkhāyā pāṭalāyā vā mukhasthaṃ kāṇḍaśastrahṛt || 10 ||

kapitthasya ca vandākaṃ kṛttikāyāṃ samuddharet |
vakrasaṃsthaṃ tadeva syātkhaḍgastambhakaraṃ param || 11 ||

kare sudarśanāmūlaṃ baddhvāstrastambhanaṃ bhavet |
ketakīmastakaṃ netre tālamūlaṃ mukhe sthitam ||
kharjjūraṃ ca raṇe haste khaḍgastambhaḥ prajāyate || 12 ||

etāni trīṇi mūlāni cūrṇitāni ghṛtaiḥ pibet |
tryahaṃ rātrau tatassarvairyāvajjīvaṃ na bādhyate || 13 ||

sarveṣāmuktayogānāṃ kumbhakarṇaḥ prasīdati |
āyāntaṃ sainyakaṃ śastrasamūhaṃ vinivārayet |

oṃ aho (?) kumbhakarṇa mahārākṣasa kaikasīgarbha(-)saṃbhūta parasainyaṃ stambhaya mahābhagavān rudraḥ ājñāpayati svāhā | sarvayogānāmaṣṭottaraśataṃ japetsiddhiḥ || 14 ||

oṃ aho kumbhakarṇa mahārākṣasa kaikasī (nikaṣā) garbhasambhūtaṃ para(-)sainyaṃ stambhaya mahābhagavān rudraḥ ājñāpayati svāhā || 13-14 ||

vakrī cakrī tathā vajrī triśūlī muśalī tathā |
dehasthā samare puṃsāṃ sarvāyudhanivāriṇī || 15 ||

gṛhītaṃ śubhanakṣatre hyapāmārgasya mūlakam |
lepamātreṇa vīrāṇāṃ sarvaśastranivāraṇam || 16 ||

kharjūrī mukhamadhyasthā kaṭyāṃ baddhvā ca ketakīm |
bhujadaṇḍa sthitaścārkassarvaśastranivāraṇam || 17 ||

puṣyarkṣe śvetaguñjā yā mūlamuddhṛtya dhārayet |
haste kāṇḍaṃ bhayaṃ nāsti saṃgrāme ca kadācana || 18 ||

trilohaveṣṭitaṃ kṛtvā rasaṃ vajrābhrasaṃyutam |
vakrasthañcakarasthañca sarvāyudhanivāraṇam || 19 ||

brahmadaṇḍī ca kaumārī īśvarī vaiṣṇavī tathā |
vārāhī vajriṇī cāndrī mahālakṣmīstathaiva ca || 20 ||

etāścauṣadhayo divyāstathaitā mātaraḥ smṛtāḥ |
smṛtvā caiva kare baddhvā sarvaśastra nivāraṇī || 21 ||

iti śastrastambhanam |

atha agnistambhanam 

oṃ śaṅkara harahara agni stambhaya stambhaya 
anenāgnau phūtkāraṃ dattvā agniṃ stambhayati || 

oṃ śaṃkara harahara agniṃ stambhaya stambhaya |

japtvā jaṭīṃ naro devīṃ tārāṃ mahiṣamardinīm |
khadirāṅgāramadhye tu praviṣṭo'sau na dahyate || 22 ||

oṃ matkaṭiṃ tacchayaghaneśekaṭīya manīya śrī(-)alipyaprāyambudīye vaśanarakīrya mantrī krīṃ phaṭ || 1 ||

oṃ krīṃ mahiṣavāhinī jambhaya jambhaya mohaya mohaya bhedaya bhedaya agniṃ stambhaya stambhaya ṭhaḥ ṭhaḥ || 2 ||

kumārīśūraṇaṃ piṣṭvā liptahasto naro bhavet |
dīptāṅgaraistato loharmantrayukterna dahyate || 23 ||

kumārīrasakaṃ piṣṭvā liptahasto naro bhavet |
dīptāṅgāraistaptalohairmantrayukterna dahyati || 
mantrastu pūrvavat || 24 ||

kare sudarśanāmūlaṃ baddhvāgnistambhanaṃ bhavet |
atra maṃtralekhanaṃ pūrvavat || 25 ||

iti agnistambhanam |

atha jalastambhanam 

padmakaṃ nāma yaddravyaṃ sūkṣmacūrṇantu kārayet |
vāpīkūpataḍāgeṣu niḥkṣipedvandhayejjalam || 26 ||

oṃ namo bhagavate rudrāya jalaṃ stambhaya stambhaya ṭhaḥ ṭhaḥ ṭhaḥ |

kṣaṇārddhena ghaṭaṃ bhidyājjalaṃ tatraiva tiṣṭhati || 27 ||

oṃ namo bhagavate rudrāya jalaṃ stambhaya stambhaya ṭhaḥ ṭhaḥ ṭhaḥ |

devadālīyamūlaṃ tu maṇḍūkarasayojitam |
lepayeddhastapādau tu jalastambhanamuttamam || 28 ||

śleṣmāntakasya piṣṭena karttavyaṃ pādukādvayam |
godhācarmamayaṃ baddhaṃ kṛtvārūḍho bhavejjale || 29 ||

śleṣmāntakaphalaṃ cūrṇa marddayitvā liped ghaṭam |
ghanamaṅgulamātraṃ tu śoṣayetpūrayejjalaiḥ || 30 ||

śirīṣamūlamādāya ravivāre tu pūrvajam |
udakena sahāghṛṣṭaṃ lalāṭe tilake kṛte || 31 ||

iti jalastambhanam |

atha divyastambhanam 

taptadivye tathā sarvakṛtadoṣo vimucyate |
uttarābhimukhaṃ grāhyaṃ meghanādasya mūlakam |
bhakṣayeddhārayedvastrairdivyastambhakaraṃ param || 32 ||

śvetaguñjotthitaṃ mūlamṛkṣe uttarabhādrake |
uttarābhimukhaṃ grāhyaṃ divyastambhakaraṃ mukhe || 33 ||

bhṛṅgīmūlaṃ rocanāṃ ca piṣṭvā pāṇau pralepayet |
lalāṭe tilakaṃ kṛtvā taptadivyajayī bhavet || 34 ||

marīcaṃ māgadhī cailā carvitā gilitā satī |
ravitaṇḍulajairdivyaiḥ kṛtadoṣo vimucyate || 35 ||

ājyaṃ śarkarayā pītvā carvitvā nāgaraṃ vacām |
taptalohaṃ lihetpaścātkṛtadoṣo vimucyate || 36 ||

maṇḍūkarasasaṃpiṣṭairlajjāmūlairvanaktakaiḥ |
liptapāṇirnaraḥ satye taptadivye viśuddhyati || 37 ||

lauha dravyastambhana mantraḥ

oṃ agnīdahaṃtī kodharai maidharo jātīnābhāvāchuchi 
nidadodivyapatitastambhe īśvaro maheśaḥ ||

etena stambhanam | śrīmahādevakī ājñā | amuṃ mantra(-)
mayutaṃ japeddivyasiddhirbhavati ||

oṃ agnīdahantī kodharai maidharo jātīnābhāvāchuchi nidado divyapatitastambhe īśvaro maheśaḥ |

oṃ lohā prajvala ko(-)ilāke bhānuhau caṇḍakedārakāpaḍī lohāpaḍautuṣāra |

iti divyastambhanam |

atha gajagomahiṣyādistambhanam 

uṣṭrāsyasthi caturdikṣu nikhanedbhūtale dhruvam |
govājimeṣīmahiṣīḥ stambhayetkariṇīmapi || 38 ||

iti gajagomahiṣyādistambhanam |

atha manuṣyastambhanam 

kālī karālī amukaṃ stambhaya svāhā |
anena maṃtreṇa sādhyanāma hṛdi dhṛtvā spṛṣṭvā vā 
darśanājjapataḥ stambhito bhavati kṣipram || 39 ||

manuṣyastambhanayantrāṇi 

kālī kaṃkālī amukaṃ stambhaya svāhā |

iti manuṣyastambhanam |

atha manaḥstambhanam 

carmakārasya kuṇḍāni rajakasya tathaiva ca |
kuṇḍānmalaṃ samuddhṛtya cāṇḍālyā ṛtuvāsasam ||

bandhayetpoṭalīṃ prājño yasyāgre tāṃ viniḥkṣipet |
tasyotthāne bhavetstambhaḥ siddhayoga udāhṛtaḥ || 40 ||

iti manaḥstambhanam |

atha āsanastambhanam 

śvetaguñjāphalaṃ vāpyaṃ nṛkapāle'pi mṛtsaha |
niśi kṛṣṇa caturdaśyāṃ tridine tatra jāgaret || 41 ||

nityaṃ siñcejjalenaiva mantra pūjāṃ ca kārayet |
tasyāḥ śākhā latā grāhyā śubha(-)ṛkṣe svamantrataḥ || 42 ||

kṣipedyasyāsane tāṃ taṃ stambhayettatkṣaṇād dhruvam || 43 ||

oṃ rudrebhyo namaḥ | oṃ vajrarūpāya vajrakiraṇāya śive rakṣabhave samāmṛtaṃ kuru kuru svāhā | ayaṃ pūjāmantraḥ ||

ityāsanastambhanam |

sarvabhūtabuddhistambhanam 

bhṛṅgarājo hyapāmārgasiddhārthaṃ sahadevikām |
tulyaṃ tulyaṃ vacāśvetādravyameṣāṃ samāharet || 44 ||

lohapātre vinikṣipya dvidināntaṃ samuddharet |
tilakaiḥ sarvabhūtānāṃ buddhistambhakaro bhavet || 45 ||

iti sarvabhūtabuddhistambhanam |

atha śatrubuddhistambhanam 

oṃ namo bhagavate viśvāmitrāya namaḥ | sarvamukhībhyāṃ viśvāmitrāya viśāmitra ājñāpayati śaktyā āgaccha āgaccha svāhā || uktayogasyāyaṃ mantraḥ | 

oṃ namo bhagavate viśvāmitrāya namaḥ | sarvamukhībhyāṃ viśvāmitrāya viśvāmitra ājñāpayati śaktyā āgaccha āgaccha svāhā |

oṃ aṅgulīlātridhā āśaya āgala svāhā

anena mantreṇa nadīṃ praviśya aṣṭottaraśatāñjalīṃstarpayet | śatrūṇāṃ buddhistambho bhavati | karpūreṇa citāṅgāre nāma likhitvā tadupari mṛttikāṃ dadyāt tatra śatrormukhabandho bhavati |

iti śatrubuddhistambhanam |

atha cauragatistambhanam 

oṃ namo brahmadveṣiṇī śive rakṣa rakṣa ṭhaḥ ṭhaḥ | anena mantreṇa 

saptapāṣāṇakhaṇḍāni śmaśānād gṛhītvā trīṇi kaṭyāṃ baddhvā parāṇi muṣṭibhyāṃ dhārayeccaurāṇāṃ gatistambho bhavati | iti ||

oṃ namo brahmadveṣiṇī śive rakṣa rakṣa ṭhaḥ ṭhaḥ |

iti cauragatistambhanam 

atha garbhastambhanam 

gṛhya kṛṣṇa caturdaśyāṃ dhattūrasya tu mūlakam |
kaṭyāṃ baddhvā rametkāntāṃ na garbhaṃ dhārayetkvacit ||

muktena labhate garbhaṃ purā nāgārjjunoditam |
tanmūlacūrṇa yonisthaṃ na garbhaṃ sambhavetkvacit || 46 ||

siddhārthamūlaṃ śirasi baddhvā kāntaṃ ramettu yā |
na garbhaṃ dhārayetsā strī muktena labhate punaḥ || 47 ||

iti garbhastambhanam |

atha śukrastambhanam 

indravāruṇikāmūlaṃ puṣye nagnaḥ samuddharet |
kaṭutrayairgavāṃ kṣīraiḥ sammiṣya golakīkṛtam |
chāyāśuṣkaṃ sthitaṃ cāsye vīryastambhakaraṃ nṛṇām || 48 ||

nīlīmūlaṃ śmaśānasthaṃ kaṭyāṃ baddhvā tu vīryadhṛk || 49 ||

kṛṣṇonmattavacāmūlaṃ madhupiṣṭaṃ pralepayet |
liṅge tadā rametkāntāṃ svabhāvāddviguṇaṃ naraḥ || 50 ||

bhṛṅgīviṣaṃ pāradaṃ ca pratyekaṃ tu dviguñjakam |
varāṭākṣaṃ kṣipedvinduḥ sthiraḥ syācchirasā dhṛtam || 51 ||

raktāpāmārgamūlaṃ tu somavāre nimantrayet |
bhaume prātaḥ samuddhṛtya kaṭyāṃ baddhvā tu vīryadhṛk || 52 ||

dundubhīnāmakaṃ sarpaṃ kṛṣṇavarṇaṃ samāharet |
tasyāsthi dhārayetkaṭyāṃ naro vīryyaṃ na muñcati |
vimuñcati vimuktena siddhiyoga udāhṛtaḥ || 53 ||

nakhāsthīni samādāya mārjārasya sitasya ca |
kṛkalāsasya pucchāgramudrikāpreta tantubhiḥ || 54 ||

veṣṭyā kaniṣṭhikā dhāryā naro vīryaṃ na muñcati || 55 ||

puṣyoddhṛtaṃ śvetapikākṣabījaṃ kaṭītaṭe lohitasūtra baddham |
bījacyutiṃ dhārayati prasaṅge khyātaḥ sadāyaṃ kila yogarājaḥ || 56 ||

śvetānyupuṣṭākhyataroḥ phalāni kṣīreṇa piṣṭvā vaṭapādapasya |
karañjabījodaramadhyagāni stambhanti vīryaṃ vadane dhṛtāni ||

ādityavāroddhṛtasaptaparṇavṛkṣasya bījaṃ vinidhāya vakre |
jayedakāṇḍaṃ suratāvatāre pumān purandhrīḥ smaratīvravegāḥ || 57 ||

nāgakeśarakarṣaṃ tu goghṛte pātayed budhaḥ |
bhuktvā ramecca ramaṇīṃ tadā binduḥ sthiro bhavet || 58 ||

śveteṣupuṅkhācaraṇaṃ gṛhītvā puṣyārkayoge puruṣasya kaṭyām |
kumārikākartitasūtrakeṇa baddhaṃ jayatyāśu manojabījam || 59 ||

bījamīśvaraliṅgasya sūtaṃ vṛścikakaṇṭakam |
kṣipetpūgaphalaṃ cāsmiṃstrilohaistaṃ ca veṣṭayet |
jihvopari sthite tasminnaro vīryaṃ na muñcati || 60 ||

sahadevībījamūlaṃ saṃmiśryaṃ padmakesaraiḥ |
madhvājyasahitāllepānnaro vīryaṃ na muñcati || 61 ||

nīlotpalasitāmbhojakesaraṃ madhuśarkaram || 62 ||

amībhirnābhilepena ciraṃ ramati kāmukaḥ || 63 ||

ādāya kṛṣṇetarakākajaṅghāmūlaṃ sitāmbhoruhakesarañca |
kṣaudreṇa piṣṭvā parilipya nābhiṃ stambhaṃ prapadyet puruṣasya bījam || 64 ||

chāgyeḍakādugdhapiṣṭaṃ lajjāmūlaṃ pralepayet |
hṛdaye pādayorvīryaṃ dravate na kadācana || 65 ||

mūlaṃ vā śvetapuṅkhāyāḥ sakṣaudraṃ nābhilepanāt |
madhunā padmabījasya tadvallepena vīryadhṛk || 66 ||

indravāruṇikāmūlamunmattājasya mūtrataḥ |
bhāvayetsaptavāraṃ taṃ liṅgalepena vīryadhṛk || 67 ||

unmattājasya mūtreṇa peṣayedvānarīśiphām |
liptvā liṅgaṃ naro vīryaṃ cirakālaṃ na muñcati || 68 ||

karpūraṃ ṭaṅkaṇaṃ sūtaṃ tulyaṃ munirasaṃ madhu |
mardayitvā lipelliṅgaṃ sthitvā yāmaṃ tathaiva ca || 69 ||

tataḥ prakṣālayelliṅgaṃ ramedrāmāṃ yathocitam |
vīryastambhakaraṃ puṃsāṃ samyaṅnāgārjunoditam || 70 ||

kausumbhatailena vilipya pādau yadṛcchayā dīvyati vṛddhavīryaḥ |
punarnavācūrṇavilepanācca jahāti bījaṃ na kadācidetat || 71 ||

karpūrasaṃpākamaheśabījaiḥ sārdhaṃ tu bījaṃ puruṣasya nābhau |
vilipya kāntājaghane ca kānte na labhyate śukramadhaḥ kadāpi || 72 ||

bhūlatāsikthakaṃ caiva kusumbhasya ca tailakam |
vīryadhṛkpādalepena taṭakāṇḍasya lepanāt || 73 ||

navanītena yuktābhyāṃ śayyāṃ padbhayāñca na spṛśet |
śleṣmāntasya kuraṇṭasya bījaṃ kārañjakasya ca |
bheḍīkṣīreṇa taṃ piṣṭvā karṣaṃ bhuktvā tu vīryadhṛk || 74 ||

sudarśanābhavaṃ mūlaṃ tāmbūlaissaha peṣayet |
bhakṣatyājyaṃ prayatnena śukrastambhanamuttamam || 75 ||

śvetārkatūlakairvartīdīpaḥ sūkaramedasā |
yāvajjvalati dīpo'yaṃ tāvadvīryaṃ na muñcati || 76 ||

mūlaṃ varāhakrāntāyā ajākṣīreṇa peṣayet |
liṅgalepena cānena vīryastambhakaraṃ bhavet || 77 ||

puṣyoddhṛtaṃ śvetahayamāramūlaṃ kaṭītaṭe |
baddhaṃ vindusthirakaraṃ mukte tu cyavate punaḥ || 78 ||

vakrīrasaṃ tadūrdhvagena pātreṇa grāhyayedravau |
saṃpiṣya vaṭikāṃ kṛtvā saṃśoṣya ca vinikṣipet |
ātmamūtreṇa tallipyaṃ liṅgamūlaṃ tu vīryyadhṛk || 79 ||

pūtīkarañjabījaṃ ca śvetabandhūkamaṇḍayoḥ |
mūlaṃ piṣṭvā tu liṅgāgre lepayeccandanaissaha || 80 ||
tathā karatale dadyādvāme binduḥ sthiro bhavet || 81 ||

lajjālumūlaṃ bhaṭikāṃ piṣṭvā tāmrasya bhājane |
kṛtvāñjanaṃ ca tenāñjajyādarddharātraṃ sthiro bhavet || 82 ||

kṛṣṇadhūrttaṃ mahākālaṃ śanivāre nimantrayet |
ravivāre samānīya cādattāramaṇīkṛtaiḥ || 83 ||

sūtrairguṇatrayairbaddhaṃ kare vāme prayatnataḥ |
upaviśyāsane khaṇḍatrayairyāmatrayaṃ tataḥ |
binduḥ sthiratvamāpnoti mukte skhalati tatkṣaṇāt || 84 ||

hemahvālocanaṃ tulyaṃ kṛṣṇadhattūrabījakam || 85 ||

vaṭakṣīreṇa saṃmardya kuberākṣasya bījakaiḥ |
kṣiptvā taddhārayedvakre vīryastambhakaraṃ bhavet || 86 ||


[iti śrīnityanāthaviracite kāmaratne śatrumukhastambhanādivarṇanaṃ nāma caturthopadeśaḥ] || 4 ||



[pañcamopadeśaḥ]  

atha mohanam 

kanyāvare yuvatisaṃgamane narāṇāmālokane narapateḥ krayavikrayādau |
prajñāvidhau sakalakarmaṇi kautuke vā dhūpaḥ sadaiva kṛtibhirviniyojanīyaḥ || 1 ||

śṛṅgīvacānaladasarjarasaṃ samānaṃ kṛtvā truṭiṃ malayaje ca ṣaḍeva nityam |
yā dhūpayennijagṛhaṃ vasanaṃ śarīraṃ tasyāstu dāsa iva mohamupaiti lokaḥ || 2 ||

bhṛṅgarājaḥ keśarājo lajjā ca sahadevikā |
ebhistu tilakaṃ kṛtvā trailokyaṃ mohayennaraḥ || 3 ||

tridalaṃ kusumaṃ yasya dhattūrasya kṛtāñjaliḥ |
bhṛṅgarājena sājyena tilakaṃ mohayennaraḥ || 4 ||

tālakaṃ kunaṭīṃ caiva bhṛṅgapakṣaṃ samaṃsamam |
kṛṣṇonmattasya kusumaṃ vaṭikāṃ kārayed budhaḥ |
tenaiva tilakaṃ kṛtvā trailokyaṃ mohayennaraḥ || 5 ||

ādau saptasvarā grāhyo ante huṃkārasaṃyutāḥ |
oṃkāraṃ śirasi kṛtvā hūṃ ante phaḍiti nyaset || 6 ||

mantraḥ

oṃ aṃ āṃ iṃ īṃ uṃ ūṃ ṛṃ hūṃ phaṭ || 5-6 ||

anenaiva tu mantreṇa kṛtvā tāmbūlabhāvanam |
mukhe sādhyasya niḥkṣipte mohamāyāti tatkṣaṇāt || 7 ||

oṃ bhīṃ kṣāṃ bhīṃ mohaya mohaya || oṃ namo bhagavatīpādapaṃkajaparāgebhyaḥ || asya maṃtrasya vāratrayajapānmohamāpnuvanti janāḥ || 8 ||

oṃ bhīṃ kṣāṃ bhīṃ mohaya mohaya |
oṃ namo bhagavatī pādapaṃkajaparāgebhyaḥ || 7(-)8 ||

śubhāmūlaṃ tathā bījaṃ raktacandanasaṃbhavam |
truṭibījaṃ samaṃ piṣṭvā tāmbūlādau prayojayet || 9 ||

bhoktuṃ deyaṃ svahastena mohamāpnoti ceśvaraḥ || 10 ||

vṛścikodbhavacūrṇena dhūpo mohayate nṛṇām || 11 ||

garalaṃ dhūrttapañcāṅgaṃ mahiṣīśoṇitaṃ kaṇā |
niśāyāṃ kurute mohaṃ dhūpo guggulusaṃyutaḥ || 12 ||

halinī viṣadhattūraśikhiviṣṭhābhiranvitam |
tathā dhūpaḥ samaṃ bhāgaṃ mohayatyeva niścitam || 13 ||

chuchundarī sarpamuṇḍaṃ vṛścikasya tu kaṇṭakam |
haritālaṃ samaṃ dhūpo mohayet sakalānnarām || 14 ||

atha śatrumohanam 

adhaḥ puṣpīśikhā caiva śvetaṃ ca girikarṇikā |
gorocanasamāyoge tilakaṃ śatrumohanam || 15 ||

tālakonmattabījāni pāne śatrośca dāpayet |
tatkṣaṇānmohamāpnoti conmatto jāyate naraḥ || 16 ||

mohaśamanam 

samākṣikaiḥ sitāmbhojeḥ svasthaḥ pānādbhavennaraḥ || 17 ||

iti śatrumohanam 

atha rañjanam 

tatra deharañjanam 

atrāṅgarāgaḥ puruṣeṇa kāryaḥ striyāśca saṃbhogasukhāya gātre |
tasmādahaṃ gandhavidhānamādau vilāsinaḥ sarvamudīrayāmi || 18 ||

harītakīlodhra mariṣṭapatraṃ saptacchadaṃ dāḍimavalkalaṃ ca |
eṣo'ṅganāyāḥ kathitaḥ kavīndraiḥ śarīradaurgandhyaharaḥ pralepaḥ || 19 ||

harītakī śrīphalamustaciñcāphalatrikaṃ pūtikarañjabījam |
kakṣādidaurgandhyamapi prabhūtaṃ vināśayatyāśu nidāghakāle || 20 ||

harītakīcandanamustanāgairuśīralodhrāmayarātritulyaiḥ |
strīpuṃsayorgharmajagātragandhaṃ vināśayatyāśu vilepanena || 21 ||

kadambapatraṃ lodhraṃ ca arjunasya tu puṣpakam || 22 ||

piṣṭvā gātrodvartanācca durgandha ca vināśanam || 23 ||

sacandanośīrakarañjapatreḥ kolākṣamajjāgurunāgayuktaiḥ |
liptvā śarīraṃ pramadā tu tena ciraprasūtaṃ vinihanti gandham || 24 ||

sadāḍimatvaṅmadhulodhrapadmaiḥ piṣṭassamānaiḥ picumardaputraḥ |
vilipya gātraṃ taruṇī nidhāghe durgandhagharmāmbucayaṃ nihanti || 25 ||

sakeśarośīraśirīṣalodhraiścūrṇīkṛtairaṅgavilepanena |
grīṣme narāṇāṃ na kadāpi dehe gharmmacyutiḥ syāditi bhojarājaḥ || 26 ||

tilasarṣaparajanīdvayadūrvāgorocanakuṣṭhaiḥ |
ajamūtratakrapiṣṭairudvartitamaṅgamanupamaṃ bhavati || 27 ||

harītakītoyadatulyabhāgairvaneruhaṃsyāpi caturthabhāgaḥ |
tadardhabhāgaḥ kathito nakhasya syādeṣa gandho madanaprakāśaḥ || 28 ||

elāśaṭīpatrakacandanāni toyābhayāśigrughanāmayāni |
sasaurabho'yaṃ surarājayogyaḥ khyātaḥ sa gandho naramohanākhyaḥ || 29 ||

dhattūrakāśmīraghanāmbulohaniśākarośīrasamāni piṣṭvā |
lepaḥ priyo'yaṃ suramānavānāmudīritaḥ pūrvakavīndradhīraiḥ || 30 ||

uśīrakṛṣṇāgurucandanāni patrāmbutulyāni samāni piṣṭvā |
etāni gātreṣu vilāsinīnāṃ śrīkhaṇḍa tulyaṃ prakaroti gandham || 31 ||

iti deharañjanam |

atha mukharañjanam 

rasālajambūphalagarbhasāraḥ sakarkaṭo mākṣikasaṃyutaśca |
sthito mukhānte puruṣasya rātrau karoti puṃsāṃ mukhavāsamiṣṭam || 32 ||

guḍatvagelānakhajātiśihlaiḥ svarṇānvitaiḥ kṣudravaṭī vidheyā |
tāmbūlagarbhā divase ca rātrau karoti puṃsāṃ mukhavāsamiṣṭam || 33 ||

cūrṇaṃ murākeśarakuṣṭhakānāṃ prātardinānte parileḍhi yā strī |
apyarddhamāsena mukhasya gandhaḥ karpuratulyo bhavati prakāmam || 34 ||

yaḥ kuṣṭhacūrṇaṃ madhunā ghṛtena pikākṣabījānvitamatti nityam |
māsaikamātreṇa mukhaṃ tadīyaṃ gandhāyate ketakagandhatulyam || 35 ||

gojalaiḥ kvathitā pathyā miśikuṣṭhakaṇānvitā |
vadanasya durāmodaṃ nihanti pariśīlitā || 36 ||

tilaṃ jātīphalaṃ pūgaṃ bhakṣayitvā pibedanu |
śītatoyaṃ palārddhaṃ tu āsyadurgandhanāśanam || 37 ||

ghṛtakāñjikagaṇḍūṣaṃ parito bhakṣayecchaṭīm |
tathākṛte bhavedāsye durāmodavināśanam || 38 ||

gomūtraiḥ kvāthayetkuṣṭhaṃ bālakaṃ saharītakīm |
sarvaṃ piṣṭvā vaṭīṃ kuryānmukhadurgandhanāśanam || 39 ||

kaṭutiktakaṣāyeṇa dantakāṣṭhena nityaśaḥ |
durgandhaṃ hanti no citraṃ kṣaudrairvā kuṣṭhacūrṇakam || 40 ||

sindhūtthasiddhārthakasārivānāṃ tvacā yutānāṃ parilepanena |
strīpuṃsayoryauvanayorhaṭhena vināśamāyāti mukhasthagandhaḥ || 41 ||

piṣṭvā masūraṃ ghanasāvareṇa muhurmuhustena vilipya vaktram |
naśyanti gaṇḍāḥ piṭakāḥ prarūḍhā māsārddhamātreṇa vilāsinīnām || 42 ||

yaḥ kaṇṭakān śālmalipādapasya kṣīreṇa piṣṭvāṣṭadinaṃ vilipya |
gaṇḍapradehāḥ piṭakāstryaheṇa prayānti nāśaṃ puruṣasya tanvyāḥ || 43 ||

dhānyaṃ vacā sāvaratulyabhāgaṃ piṣṭvā lipettena mukhaṃ nitāntam |
mukhodbhavā yauvanajā narāṇāṃ naśyanti nūnaṃ piṭakāḥ krameṇa || 44 ||

siddhārthabījadvitayaṃ tilaṃ ca kṣāreṇa piṣṭvā parilipya vaktram |
saptāhamātreṇa mukhasthanīlīṃ nihanti kṛṣṇāmiti rantidevaḥ || 45 ||

niśādvayaṃ gairikaśoṇayaṣṭirambhāmbumāhendrayavottamāni |
nighnanti nīliṃ trayavāramātrāccandreṇa tulyaṃ vadanaṃ bhavecca || 46 ||

maricaṃ rocanāyuktaṃ saṃpeṣya mukhamālipet |
taruṇyāḥ piṭakāḥ sarvā naśyanti mukhasambhavāḥ || 47 ||

vyaṅgeṣu cārjunatvagvā mañjiṣṭhā vā samākṣikā |
etā liptāstryahaṃ yāvadbhavetpadmopamaṃ mukham || 48 ||

mātuluṅgajaṭā sarpiḥ śilā gośakṛto rasaḥ |
mukhakāntikaro lepaḥ piṭakātilakālajit || 49 ||

raktacandanamañjiṣṭhākuṣṭhalodhrapriyaṅgavaḥ |
vaṭāṅkuramasūrāśca vyaṅgaghnā mukhakāntidāḥ || 50 ||

mañjiṣṭhā madhukaṃ lākṣā mātuluṅgaṃ sapiṣṭakam |
karṣapramāṇairetaistu tailasya kuḍavaṃ pacet || 51 ||

ajāpayastaddviguṇaṃ śanairmudvagninā pacet || 52 ||

nīlikā pīṭikā vyaṅgā abhyaṅgādeva nāśayet |
mukhaprasanne'pi hitaṃ nīlakarkaśavarjitam || 53 ||

saptarātraprayogeṇa bhavetkāñcanasaṃnibham |
atra dviruktatvādyaṣṭīmadhukarṣadvayaṃ tathā || 54 ||

manaḥśilā tathā lodhraṃ dviniśāsarṣapāḥ samāḥ |
vāripiṣṭo hito lepo vadane śyāmikāṃ haret || 55 ||

mahiṣīkṣīrasaṃyuktaṃ rajanīraktacandanam |
kṛte lepe nihaṃtyāśu śyāmikāṃ vadanāśritām || 56 ||

ubhe haridre mañjiṣṭhā ghṛtaṃ gaurāśca sarṣapāḥ |
peṣyaṃ gairikasaṃyuktamajākṣīreṇa pācitam || 57 ||

etenaiva bhavedvaktramudayādityavarcasam |
vacā lodhramuśīraṃ ca sarpissarjarasaḥ payaḥ || 58 ||

pītāni vaṭapatrāṇi rajanyā saha peṣayet |
lepo'yaṃ mukhapādābhyāṃ padmavatkurute bhṛśam || 59 ||

masūrān madhunā sārdhaṃ piṣṭvā tairmardayenmukham |
saptarātraprayogeṇa puṇḍarīkadalaprabham || 60 ||

iti mukharañjanam |

atha keśarañjanam 

sraggandhadhūpāmbarabhūṣaṇānāṃ na śobhateśuklaśiroruhāṇām |
yasmādato mūrddhajarāgasevāṃ kuryādyathaivāñjanabhūṣaṇānām || 61 ||

āmrāṇḍenotthitaṃ tailaṃ kāntapāṣāṇacūrṇitam |
kākatuṇḍīphalaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ cūrṇayitvā prayatnataḥ || 62 ||

dhānyarāśau viniḥkṣipya māsorddhvaṃ śirasi kṣipet |
nasyaṃ dinatrayaṃ tena keśarañjanakaṃ bhavet |
varṣārddhaṃ tiṣṭhate kṛṣṇabhramarāñjanasannibham || 63 ||

triphalālohacūrṇaṃ tu nīlī bhṛṅgīsamūlakam || 64 ||

etaccūrṇamiḍāmūtre dinamekaṃ vibhāvayet |
tenaiva mardayecchīrṣe rañjate bhramaropamam || 65 ||

guñjābījasya cūrṇantu kuṣṭhailā devadārukam |
tulyāṃśaṃ bhāvayeccūrṇaṃ dinaikaṃ bhṛṅgajadravaiḥ || 66 ||

cūrṇāccaturguṇe taile pācayenmṛduvahninā | 
tenābhyaṅgādghanāḥ keśā rañjanaṃ bhramaropamam || 67 ||

hastidantasya dagdhasya samāṃśena rasaṃ samam |
ajākṣīreṇa taṃ piṣṭvā lepanātkeśarañjanam || 68 ||

triphalālohacūrṇantu ikṣubhṛṅgarasastathā |
kṛṣṇamṛttikayā sārdhaṃ bhāṇḍe māsaṃ nirodhayet |
tallepādrañjayetkeśaścaturmāsaṃ sthiro bhavet || 69 ||

lohakiṭṭaṃ japāpuṣpaṃ piṣṭvā dhātrīphalaṃ samam |
tridinaṃ lepayecchīrṣaṃ dvimāsaṃ keśarañjanam || 70 ||

bhṛṅgarājarasaprasthaṃ tailaṃ kṛṣṇatilātsamam || 71 ||

mardayetpraharaikaṃ tu tailāptaṃ nīlikārasam |
tallepaṃ tridinaṃ kuryyātkeśarañjanakaṃ bhavet || 72 ||

cūrṇaṃ sarjayavakṣāraṃ siddhārthaṃ cāranālakaiḥ |
nāgapuṣpaṃ dine madhye tallepātkeśarañjanam || 73 ||

kākamācīyabījāni samaṃ kṛṣṇatilāṃstataḥ |
tattailaṃ grāhayedyantre tannyasyetkeśarañjanam || 74 ||

goghṛtaṃ bhṛṅgajadrāvaṃ mayūraśikhayā saha |
agninā mṛdunā pācyaṃ taṃ nyasyetkeśarañjanam || 75 ||

kākamācīyabījāni samaṃ kṛṣṇatilāṃstataḥ |
japāpuṣparasaṃ kṣaudraṃ karṣaikaṃ nyasyamācaret ||
saptāhaṃ rañjayetkeśān sarvanyasye tvayaṃ vidhiḥ || 76 ||

triphalālohacūrṇaṃ tu vāriṇā peṣayetsamam |
dvayostulyena tailena pacenmṛdvagninā kṣaṇam || 77 ||

tailatulyaṃ bhṛṅgarasaṃ yāvattailaṃ ca pācayet |
snigdhaṃ bhāṇḍagataṃ bhūmau sthitaṃ māsātsamuddharet || 78 ||

saptāhaṃ lepayelliptvā kadalyāśca dale śiraḥ |
nirvāte kṣīrabhojī syātkṣālayetriphalājalaiḥ || 79 ||

nityamevaṃ hi karttavyaṃ saptāhe rañjanaṃ bhavet |
yāvajjīvaṃ na sandehaḥ keśāḥ syurbhramaropamāḥ || 80 ||

mahākālasya bījāni vākucībījatatsamam || 81 ||

cūrṇaṃ dravyaiśca nirguṇḍyāṃ bhāvayettu caturdinam |
japāpuṣpadravairevaṃ tataḥ pātālayantrake || 82 ||

tailaṃ grāhyaṃ tu tallepe patraireraṇḍajaiśśiraḥ |
veṣṭayetkṣīrabhojī syādvātātapavivarjitaḥ || 83 ||

evaṃ saptadinaṃ lepyaṃ taṇḍulān dhārayenmukhe |
kṛṣṇavarṇāḥ prajāyante tathā bhavati pratyaham || 84 ||

mohograṃ karddame kṣiptvā ṣaṇmāsāttu samuddharet |
taddrutaṃ jāyate kṛṣṇaṃ karṣaikaṃ śirasi kṣipet || 85 ||

keśāḥ kṛṣṇāḥ prajāyante bhramarīkulasannibhāḥ |
kapālarañjanaṃ khyātaṃ yāvajjīvaṃ na saṃśayaḥ || 86 ||

sanīlikāṃ saindhavapippalībhighṛtānuyuktairupalipya keśam |
krameṇa śaṃkhopamamāśu kṛṣṇaṃ śiroruhaṃ mecakatāmupaiti || 87 ||

phalānvitaṃ cūtataroḥ prasūnaṃ piṇḍārakaṃ pāṇḍuranīlike vā |
prasthapramāṇaṃ ca tilasya tailaṃ pacedamobhirviditopadeśaḥ || 88 ||

tasmiṃśca madhye yadi haṃsapakṣaḥ kṣipto bhavenmecakavarṇayuktaḥ |
pākastadaivāsya narairvidheyaḥ khyātaṃ pṛthivyāmapi nīlatailam || 89 ||

etanniliptaṃ palitaṃ narāṇāṃ śaṃkhāvadātaṃ sakalaṃ trirātram |
puṣpaṃ na śreyāṃsi samānayuktaṃ ciraṃbhavedṛṣṭa iti prayogaḥ || 90 ||

śatāvarī kṛṣṇatilena yuktā gorocanā kākamukhābhidhā ca |
amībhirālipya punaḥ sukeśān karoti śuklānapi kṛṣṇavarṇān || 91 ||

madantikāyā rasakalkasiddhaṃ tilodbhavaṃ tailamidaṃ narāṇām |
akālajātaṃ palitaṃ saraukṣyaṃ keśasya kāruṣyamalaṃ nihanti || 92 ||

mutthaṃ ca sarṣapaṃ caiva hyuśīraṃ ca tathaiva ca |
harītakyāśca kvāthena āmalakyāssamaṃ tataḥ || 93 ||

keśamūlaṃ samālepya meghatulyo bhavetkacaḥ || 94 ||

iti keśarañjanam |

atha snānārthasugandhidravyam 

sūkṣmailājīmūtanakhaiḥ sacūtaiḥ svarṇāmasīpatrakasaṃyutaiśca |
saurabhyakāntī kila mūrddhajānāṃ snātvā naro vindati sarvadaiva || 95 ||

svarṇāmbudośīranakhīyutānāṃ pathyānvitānāṃ ca vilepane ca |
snātvā naraḥ saurabhamarddhamāsaṃ vaikalpamāpnoti śiroruhasya || 96 ||

pathyāvasānāmalakīphalānāmajastu jīmūtarasāmayānām |
māṃsīyutānāṃ parilepanena snātvā naraḥ saurabhakāntimeti || 97 ||

iti snānārthasugandhidravyam |

atha yūkalikhyādinivāraṇam 

viḍaṅgagandhotpalakalkayogādgomūtrasiddhaṃ kaṭutailametat |
abhyaṅgayogena śiroruhāṇāṃ yūkādilikṣāpracayaṃ nihanti || 98 ||

gomūtrasārivāmūlaṃ lepādyūkānivāraṇam |
pāradaṃ mardayenniṣkaṃ kṛṣṇadhattūrajairdravaiḥ || 99 ||

nāgavallīdravairvāpi vastrakhaṇḍaṃ vilepayet |
tadvastraṃ veṣṭayenmaulau dhāryaṃ yāmatrayaṃ tathā |
yūkāḥ patanti niśśeṣaṃ mastakānnātra saṃśayaḥ || 100 ||

dviniśānavanītena lepānmauleḥ prakaṇḍunut || 101 ||

nīlotpalaṃ tilaṃ yaṣṭisarṣapā nāgakeśaram |
dhātrīphalaṃ samaṃ piṣṭvā lepo yūkāvināśaka || 102 ||

niśāgandhakagomūtraṃ viḍaṅgaṃ kaṭutailakam |
pāradena samaṃ mardya lepo yūkāvināśakaḥ || 103 ||

lākṣā bhallātakaṃ mustā kūṭaguggulusarṣapāḥ |
viḍaṅgena samaṃ lepāddhūmo yūkānivārakaḥ || 104 ||

bilvamūlaṃ sagomūtraṃ lepādyūkāvināśanam || 105 ||

iti yūkalikhyādinivāraṇam |

atha keśasyendraluptādinivāraṇam 

guñjāphalaiḥ kṣaudrayutairvilipya śiraḥ pradeśe sakalendraluptam |
anena yogena sadaiva keśā rohanti kṛṣṇāḥ kuṭilā viśālāḥ || 106 ||

mātaṅgadantasya masīṃ vidhāya śvetāñjanaṃtulyatayā supiṣṭam |
lipyedanenaiva mahendraluptaṃ keśāḥ prarohantyapi hastamadhye || 107 ||

kuṅkumaṃ maricaissārddhaṃ piṣṭvā tailena lepayet |
indraluptaṃ nihantyāśu kiṃvā jambīrajadravaiḥ || 108 ||

sudagdhaṃ hastidantaṃ tu chāgadugdhaṃ rasāñjanam |
piṣṭvā lepātprajāyante keśāḥ karataleṣvapi || 109 ||

jātīpuṣpaṃ dalaṃ mūlaṃ kṛṣṇāgomūtrapeṣitam |
lepo'yaṃ saptarātreṇa dṛḍhakeśakaraḥ paraḥ || 110 ||

śṛṅgāṭa triphalābhṛṅgīnīlotpalamayorajaḥ |
sūkṣmaṃ cūrṇaṃ samaṃ kṛtvā pacettaile caturguṇe |
tallepena dṛḍhāḥ keśāḥ kuṭilāḥ saralā api || 111 ||

kīṭabhakṣitakeśaṃ tu sthānaṃ svarṇena gharṣayet |
yāvatsutaptatāṃ yāti tāvallepamimaṃ kuru || 112 ||

bhallātakaṃ ca bṛhatī guñjāmūlaphalaṃ tathā |
madhunā saha lepena vāyukṣīroṭakapraṇut || 113 ||

bhallātakaṃ kṛṣṇatilaṃ kaṇṭakārīphalaṃ samam |
piṣṭaṃ taṇḍulatoyena lepo'yaṃ taṃ vināśayet || 114 ||

japāpuṣpaiśca taṃ hanyātkṛṣṇāgomūtralepanāt || 115 ||

tilaprasūnaṃ saha gokṣureṇa salāvaṇaṃ gavyaghṛtena piṣṭam |
saptāhamātreṇa śiraḥ pralepādbhavanti dīrghāḥ pracurāśca keśāḥ || 116 ||

śālmalītālamūlyośca mūlaṃ padmasamudbhavam |
chāgadugdhe samaṃ piṣṭaṃ lepayenmuṇḍitaṃ śiraḥ ||
tridinaṃ bhakṣayettacca keśavardhanamuttamam || 117 ||

iti keśasyendraluptādinivāraṇam |

atha keśaśuklīkaraṇam 

vajrīkṣīreṇa saptāhaṃ taccheṣaṃ bhāvayettilam |
tattailaliptāḥ keśāśca śuklāḥ syurnnātra saṃśayaḥ || 118 ||

rogāmalakacūrṇaṃ tu vajrīkṣīreṇa saptadhā |
bhāvayettasya lepena śuklatāṃ yānti mūrddhajāḥ || 119 ||

ajākṣīreṇa saptāhaṃ bhāvayedabhayāphalam |
taccūrṇaṃ saha tailena lepācchuklā bhavanti hi || 120 ||

[iti śrīnityanāthaviracite kāmaratne mohanādivarṇanaṃ nāma pañcamopadeśaḥ] || 5 ||



[ṣaṣṭhopadeśaḥ] 

atha vājīkaraṇam 

balena nārī paritoṣameti na hīnavīryasya kadāpi saukhyam |
ato balārthaṃ ratilampaṭasya vājīvidhānaṃ prathamaṃ vidadhye || 1 ||

aśvinyāṃ vaṭavṛndākaṃ kṣīraiḥ pītvā mahābalaḥ |
puṣyoddhṛtaṃ pibenmūlaṃ śvetārkasya prayatnataḥ |
saptarātraṃ tu gokṣīrairvṛddho'pi taruṇāyate || 2 ||

cūrṇaṃ vidāryāḥ svarasena tasyā vibhāvitaṃ bhāskararaśmijālaiḥ |
madhvājyasaṃmiśritameva līḍhvā daśa striyo gacchati nirviśaṅkaḥ || 3 ||

bhūyo vibhāvyāmalakasya cūrṇaṃ rasena tasyaiva sitājyamiśram |
sakṣaudramāleḍhi niśāmukhe yo nūnaṃ sa vṛddhastaruṇatvameti || 4 ||

karṣapramāṇaṃ madhukasya cūrṇaṃ kṣaudrājyasaṃmiśritameva līḍhvā |
kṣīrānupānādramate tu tāvadyāvannarāṇāmudarasthametat || 5 ||

sitapikatarubījaṃ taṇḍulā yaṣṭikānāṃ saghṛtamadhusametaṃ pratyahaṃ yo'valeḍhi | 
jaṭharakuharamadhye yāti pākaṃ na yāvadramayati kṛśadehopyaṅganānāṃ samaham || 6 ||

vṛddhaśālmalimūlasya rasaṃ śarkarayā pibet |
etatprayogātsaptāhājjāyate retaso'mbuddhiḥ || 7 ||

laghuśālmalimūlena tālamūlīsacūrṇitam 
sarpiṣā payasā pītvā ratau caṭakavadbhavet || 8 ||

ghṛtena māsaṃ masṛtāni bhūyo subhāvayitvā raviśoṣitāni |
kṣīreṇa saṃsādhya ca bhakṣayitvā sa yāti nārīśatamugraretāḥ || 9 ||

yo varttakīlāvakapiñjalānāṃ māṃsaṃ tathā veśmavihaṅgamasya |
havyena siddhaṃ saha saindhavena mahābalaḥ syādupayujyamānaḥ || 10 ||

vidārīkandakalkaṃ tu ghṛtena payasā naraḥ |
udumbarasamaṃ khādedvṛddho'pi taruṇāyate || 11 ||

pippalīnāṃ rasopetau bastāṇḍau kṣīrasarpiṣā |
sādhitau bhakṣayedyastu sa gacchetpramadāśatam || 12 ||

bastāṇḍasiddhe payasi bhāvitānasakṛttilān |
yaḥ khādetsa naro gacchetstrīnāṃ śatamapūrvavat || 13 ||

gokṣurakaḥ kṣurakaḥ śatamūlī vānarināgabalātibalā ca |
cūrṇamidaṃ payasā niśi peyaṃ yasya gṛhe pramadāśatamasti || 14 ||

aśvatthaphalamūlatvak chuṅgāsiddhaṃ payo naraḥ |
sa pītvā śarkarākṣaudraṃ kaliṅga iva hṛṣyate || 15 ||

ghṛtaṃ śatāvarīgarbhaṃ kṣīre caturguṇe pacet |
śarkarāpippalīkṣaudrayuktaṃ tadvṛṣya mucyate || 16 ||

nṛsiṃhacūrṇam 

śatāvarīrajaḥ prasthaṃ prasthaṃ gokṣurakasya ca |
vārāhyā viṃśatipalaṃ guḍūcyāḥ pañcaviṃśatiḥ || 17 ||

bhallātakānāṃ dvātriṃśaccitrakāṇāṃ daśaiva tu |
tilānāṃ nistuṣāṇāṃ tu prasthaṃ dadyātsucūrṇitam || 18 ||

triphalasya palānyaṣṭau śarkarāyāśca saptatiḥ |
mākṣikaṃ śarkarārddhena mākṣikārddhena vai ghṛtam || 19 ||

śatāvarīsamaṃ deyaṃ vidārīkandajaṃ rajaḥ |
etadekīkṛtaṃ cūrṇa snigdhabhāṇḍe nidhāpayet || 20 ||

palārddhamupamuñjīta yatheṣṭaṃ cāsya bhojanam |
māsaikamupayogena jarāṃ hanti rujāmapi || 21 ||

valīpalitakhālityahemapāṇḍvādyapīnasān |
hantyaṣṭādaśakuṣṭhāni tathāṣṭārbudarāṇi ca || 22 ||

bhagandaraṃ mūtrakṛcchraṃ gṛdhrasīṃ sahalīmakam |
kṣaye caiva mahāvyādhiṃ paṃcakāsān sudāruṇān || 23 ||

aśītivātajānnāgāṃścatvāriṃśacca paittikān |
viṃśatisūkṣmarogāṃśca saṃsṛṣṭānsānnipātikān || 24 ||

sarvānarśogadānhanyādvṛkṣamindrāśaniryathā || 25 ||

sakāñcanābho mṛgarājavikramasturaṅgamaṃ cāpyanuyāti vegataḥ |
strīṇāṃ śataṃ gacchati sātirekaṃ prahṛṣṭapuṣṭaṃ ca yathā vihaṅgam || 26 ||

putrānsaṃjanayeddhīmānnarasiṃhanibhāṃstathā |
nārasiṃhamidaṃ cūrṇaṃ sarvarogaharaṃ nṛṇām || 27 ||

iti nṛsiṃhacūrṇam |

madanamodakaḥ 

trailokyavijayāpatraṃ sabījaṃ ghṛtabharjitam |
trikaṭu triphalā kuṣṭhaṃbhṛṅgī saiṃdhavadhānyakam || 28 ||

cavyaṃ tālīśapatraṃ ca kaṭphalaṃ nāgakeśaram |
ajamodā yavānī ca yaṣṭī madhukameva ca || 29 ||

methī jīrakayugmañca gṛhītā samabhāgataḥ |
cyavantyetāni cūrṇāni tāvadeva tadauṣadham || 30 ||

sama śilātale piṣṭvā cūrṇayedaticikkaṇam |
tāvadeva sitā deyā yāvadāyāti bandhanam || 31 ||

ghṛtena madhunā miśraṃ modakaṃ parikalpayet |
ghṛtabharjitatilacūrṇaṃ modakopari vinyaset || 

trisugandhisamāyuktaṃ karpūreṇādhivāsitam |
sthāpayedghṛtabhāṇḍe tu śrīmanmadanamodakam || 32 ||

bhakṣayetprātarutthāya vātaśleṣmabhayāpaham |
pravṛddhamagniṃ kurute mandamagniṃ pradīpayet || 33 ||

kṛśānāmatirūkṣāṇāṃ snehanaṃ sthaulyakāraṇam || 34 ||

kāsaghnaṃ sarvaśūlaghnamāmavātavināśanam |
sarvarogaharaṃ hyetatsaṃgrahagrahaṇīharam || 35 ||

etasya satatābhyāsād vṛddho'pi taruṇāyate || 36 ||

brahmaṇaśca mukhācchrutvā vāsudeve jagatpatau |
eṣa kāmasya vṛddhyarthaṃ nāradena prakāśitaḥ |
yena lakṣairvarastrīṇāmaraṃsta yadunandanaḥ || 37 ||

iti madanamodakaḥ |

kāmakalārasaḥ 

mṛtasūtābhrakaṃ svarṇavājigandhāvacārasaiḥ |
muśalī kadalīkandadravaiśca mardayeddinam || 38 ||

lākṣālaghupuṭaiḥ pacyānmardayetpūrvavaddravaiḥ |
puṭaṃ deyaṃ punarmardyamevamaṣṭapuṭaiḥ pacet || 39 ||

śālmalījātaniryāmaiścaturmāsāṃstu bhakṣayet |
godugdhaṃ markaṭībījaiḥ palārddhaṃ pācayedanu || 40 ||

rasaḥ kāmakalākhyo'yaṃ ramate strīsahasrakaiḥ |
sarvāṅgodvarttanaṃ kuryātsvarasaiḥ śālmalīrasaiḥ || 41 ||

iti kāmakalārasaḥ |

anaṅgasundarīvaṭikā 

śuddhasūtasamaṃ gandhaṃ tryahaṃ kahlārajairdravaiḥ |
marditaṃ vālukāyantre yāmaṃ saṃpuṭagaṃ pacet || 42 ||

raktāgastyadravairbhāvyaṃ dinamekamiva drutam |
yatheṣṭaṃ bhakṣayeccānnaṃ kāmayetkāminīśatam || 43 ||

paladvayaṃ dvayaṃ śuddhaṃ pāradaṃ gandhakaṃ tathā |
mṛtahemnastu karṣaikaṃ palaikaṃ ca mṛtābhrakam || 44 ||

mṛttatāmraṃ caturniṣkaṃ sarva paṃcāmṛtairdinam |
rudrairgajapuṭaiḥ pacyāddinaikānte samuddharet || 45 ||

piṣṭvā pañcāmṛtaiḥ kuryād vaṭikāṃ badarākṛtim |
anaṅgasundarīṃ khādedramedvāmāśatatrayam || 46 ||

śālmalīmūlaṃ cūrṇaṃ tu bhṛṅgarājasya mūlakam |
palaikaṃ sitayā cānnaṃ bhakṣayetkāmayecchatam || 47 ||

ityanaṅgasundarī vaṭī |

atha mahākāmeśvararasaḥ 

sūtapādaṃ tāmracūrṇaṃ khalve piṣṭaṃ prakārayet |
nikṣipya kadalīkande punarlepyaṃ ca gomayaiḥ || 48 ||

śuṣkaṃ gajapuṭaiḥ pacyāttathā kande punaḥ kṣipet |
evaṃ saptapuṭaiḥ pacyātkandaiḥ kandaṃ pṛthakpṛthak || 49 ||

dattvā tatra ghṛtaṃ cūrṇaṃ vastre baddhvā tu pācayet |
dolāyaṃtre ca saṃyuktaṃ chāgīdugdhe punaḥ pacet || 50 ||

gūḍūcyātha śatāvaryā vānaryā gokṣuraistathā | 
gajapippalikā lājā kadalyā kokilākṣakaiḥ || 51 ||

sitaya pācayedeva dviguṇaṃ laghuvahninā |
uddhṛtya cūrṇayetsvacchaṃ bhakṣayed guñjācatuṣṭayam || 52 ||

sitāyuktaṃ sadā sevyaṃ mahākāmeśvaro rasaḥ |
kāminīnāṃ sahasraikaṃ kṣobhayennimiṣāntare || 53 ||

gokṣuraṃ vānaraṃ bījaṃ guḍūcī gajapippalī |
kokilākṣasya bījāni phalaṃ guṇaśatāvarī || 54 ||

karkaśābījamajjā ca sarvaṃ tulyaṃ vicūrṇayet |
cūrṇatulyā sitā yojyā madhunā piṇḍitaṃ lihet |
palārddhamanupānaṃ syātkiṃcitpeyaṃ gavāṃ payaḥ || 55 ||

iti mahākāmeśvarasaḥ |

atha madanodayarasaḥ 

śuddhasūtasamaṃ gandhaṃ raktotpaladaladravaiḥ |
yāmamekaṃ punargandhaṃ pūrvādarddhaṃ vinikṣipet || 56 ||

taddravairmarddayeccāṅgaṃ pūrvagandhaṃ ca marddanam |
pūrvadrāvairdinaikaṃ tu kācakupyā nirudhya ca || 57 ||

dinaikaṃ vālukāyantraṃ pakvamuddhṛtya bhakṣayet |
pañcaguñjāsitāsārddhaṃ raso'yaṃ madanodayaḥ || 58 ||

kokilākṣasya bījaṃ ca musalī śarkarāsamam |
gavāṃ kṣīreṇa tatpeyaḥ palārddhamanupānakam || 59 ||

iti madanodayarasaḥ |

atha kāmāṅganāyakacūrṇam 

vānarīkokilākṣasya bījaṃ śyāmatilaṃ samam |
mūlaṃ gokṣuramāsābhyāṃ śuṣkacūrṇaṃ prakalpayet || 60 ||

cūrṇatulyaṃ mṛtaṃ cābhraṃ sarvatulyā sitā bhavet |
karṣamekaṃ gavāṃ kṣīraiḥ peyaṃ kāmāṅganāyakam || 61 ||

tailena pakvaṃ caṭakaṃ khādedbhojanapūrvataḥ |
bhojanānte pibetkṣīraṃ rāmāḥ kāmayate śatam || 62 ||

iti kāmāṅganāyakacūrṇam

atha kāmāmṛtayogaḥ 

aśvagandhā vahnimūlaṃ śālmalī ca śatāvarī |
vidārī muśalīkandaṃ kokilākṣasya bījakam || 63 ||

tattulyaṃ vānarībījaṃ suṣṭu cūrṇaṃ tu kārayet |
cūrṇatulyaṃ mṛtaṃ cābhraṃ sarvatulyā sitā bhavet |
gavāṃ kṣīraiḥ pibetkarṣaṃ ramayet kāminīśatam || 64 ||

aśvakarkaṭamāṃsaṃ tu bhakṣayecca pibetpayaḥ |
yogaḥ kāmāmṛtaḥ khyāto balāyurvīryavarddhakaḥ || 65 ||

iti kāmāmṛtayogaḥ |

atha dhātrīloham 

dhātrīphalasya cūrṇaṃ tu bhāvayettatphaladravaiḥ || 66 ||

ekaviṃśativāraṃ tu śoṣyaṃ peṣyaṃ punaḥ punaḥ |
cūrṇapādaṃ mṛtaṃ lohaṃ madhvājye śarkarānvitam || 67 ||

palaikaṃ bhakṣayennityaṃ sitākṣīraṃ pibedanu |
kāmayetstrīśataṃ nityaṃ dhātrīlohaprabhāvataḥ || 68 ||

iti dhātrīloham |

atha kāmeśvararasaḥ 

vānarībījacūrṇaṃ tu nistuṣaṃ māṣacūrṇitam |
nārikelodakairbhāvyaṃ yāmāntaṃ peṣayetsamam || 69 ||

piṣṭasya viṃśadviṃśena mṛtamabhraṃ niyojayet |
tadvattairvaṭikā kāryā madhvājyābhyāṃ tu bhakṣayet || 70 ||

pītvā kṣīraṃ sitāyuktaṃ ramyā rāmā ramecchatam |
satāmbūlaṃ śatāmūlamanupānaṃ nirantaram || 71 ||

ūrṇanābhibhavaṃ bījaṃ madhunā vaha peṣayet |
tena nābhipralepena bandhaḥ sadyo vimuñcati || 72 ||

antarikṣeṇa saṃgrāhya yatnādvā guṭikāmalam |
tena liṅgapralepena ramedrāmāśataṃ naraḥ || 73 ||

samyaṅmāritamabhrakaṃ kaṭphalaṃ kuṣṭhāśvagandhāmṛtā |
methī mocarasaṃ vidāramuśalī gokṣūramikṣūrakam |
rambhākandaśatāvarī hyajamudā māṣastilā dhānyakaṃ |
yaṣṭī nāgabalā kacūramadanaṃ jātīphalaṃ saindhavam || 74 ||

mārgīkarkaṭaśṛṅgabhṛṅgakaṭukaṃ jīradvayaṃ citrakaṃ 
cāturjātapunarnavā gajakaṇā brāhmī niśāvāsakam |
bījaṃ markaṭiśālmalaṃ phalatrikaṃ cūrṇaṃ samaṃ kalpaye(-)
ccūrṇaṃsaṃvijayāsitādviguṇitā madhvājyayoḥ piṃḍitam || 75 ||

karṣārddhaṃ vaṭikā vilehyamathavā sevyaṃ mudā sarvadā 
peyaṃ kṣīrasitānuvīryakaraṇe stambhepyayaṃ kāminīm |
vāmāvaśyakaraṃ paraṃ ca sukhadaṃ prauḍhāṅganādrāvakaṃ 
kṣīṇe puṣṭikaraṃ gadakṣayakaraṃ hantyāśu sarvāmayam || 76 ||

kāsaśvāsamahātisāraśamanaṃ mandāgnisaṃdīpanaṃ 
arśaḥ saṃgrahaṇīpramehanicaya śleṣmātiraktapraṇut |
nityānandakaraṃ viśeṣakavitā vācāṃ vilāsottamaṃ 
dhatte sarvaguṇaṃ haṭhātsvavadaśādhyānapradhānaṃ punaḥ || 77 ||

abhyāsena nihanti mṛtyupalitaṃ kāmeśvaro vatsarāt 
sarveṣāṃ hitakārako nigaditaḥ śrīnityanāthena saḥ | 
vṛddhānāṃ madanodayodayakaraḥ prauḍhāṅganāsaṃgame 
siddho'yaṃ samadṛṣṭapratyayakarī rājñā sadā sevyatām || 78 ||

yatkiñcinmadhurasnigdhaṃ jīvanaṃ bṛṃhaṇaṃ guru |
harṣaṇaṃ manasaścaiva tatsarvaṃ vṛṣyamucyate |
naro vīryakarānyogānsamyak śuddho nirāmayaḥ || 79 ||

iti kāmeśvararasaḥ |

atha vājīkaraṇakālaḥ 

āsaptateḥ prakurvīta varṣādūrddhvaṃ ca ṣoḍaśāt |
na pūrvaṃ ṣoḍaśādvarṣātsaptatyāḥ parato na ca || 80 ||

atha vājīkaraṇaphalam 

āyuḥkāmo naraḥ strībhiḥ saṃyogaṃ kartumarhati |
kalpenādagdhavayaso vājīkaraṇasevitaḥ || 81 ||

āyuṣmanto mandajarāvapurvīryabalānvitāḥ |
sthiropacitamāṃsāśca bhavanti strīṣu saṃyutāḥ || 82 ||

atha strīsaṃgamakālaḥ 

tribhistribhirahobhiśca seveta pramadāṃ naraḥ 
sarvartuṣu ca grīṣmeṣu pakṣātpakṣādvrajed budhaḥ || 83 ||

yogaṃ kṛtvā susevyaṃ suśṛtamapi payaḥ śītalaṃ cāmbu
pītvā gacchennārī surūpāṃ smaśaravaśagāṃ kāmukaḥ kāmalīlaḥ |

ratyā hṛṣṭaḥ prahṛṣṭo vyapagatasurataḥ sandhaye nityanityaṃ 
kāntāsaṃgāddivāpi hyasakṛdapi naro dhātu baiṣamyameti || 84 ||

atha anyathāstrīsaṅgame doṣāḥ 

glāniḥ kamporudaurbalyaṃ dhātvindriyabalakṣayaḥ |
kṣayavṛddhyupadaṃśādyā rogāścātīva durjayāḥ || 
akālamaraṇaṃ caiva bhajataḥ striyamanyathā || 85 ||

śoṣakāsajvarārśāṃsi śvāsakārśyātipāṇḍutā |
ativyavāyājjāyante rogāśca kṣayakādayaḥ || 86 ||

asevanānmohamadau granthiragneśca mārdavam |
tyajeccintādyaśucitāṃ śokādhyakṣaṃ ca maithunam |
jarāyāściṃtayā śukraṃ vyādhibhiḥ karmakarṣaṇāt || 87 ||

kṣayaṃ gacchatyanaśanātstrīṇāṃ caivātisevanāt |
kṣayādbhayādaviśvāsācchokastrīdoṣadarśanāt |
nārīṇāmavasannatvādabhighātādasevanāt || 88 ||

atha vājīkaraṇī yā nārī 

rūpayauvanasaundaryalakṣaṇairyā vibhūṣitā |
yā vaśyā śikṣitā yā ca sā strī bṛṣyatamā matā || 89 ||

atha vājīkaraṇayogyāḥ 

strīṣu kṣayaṃ gatavatāṃ vṛddhānāṃ caritaśritām |
kṣīṇānāmalpaśukrāṇāṃ strī kṣīṇāśca ye narāḥ || 90 ||

vilāsināmarthavatāṃ yauvane balaśālinām |
bahupatnīvatāṃ nṛṇāṃ yogā vājikarā hitāḥ || 91 ||


[iti śrīnityanāthaviracite kāmaratne vājīkaraṇaṃ nāma ṣaṣṭhopadeśaḥ] || 6 ||


[saptamopadeśaḥ] 

atha gāḍhīkaraṇam 

tatra bhakṣyaniṣedhaḥ 

tatra bhakṣyaniṣedhaḥ 

atyantamūlakaṭutiktakaṣāyamamlaṃ 
kṣāraṃ ca śākakhadiraṃ lavaṇādhikaṃ ca |
kāmī sadaiva ratinānvanitābhilāṣī 
no bhakṣayediti samastajanaprasiddhaḥ || 1 ||

prauḍhāṅganāyā navasūtikāyāḥ śrlathaṃ varāṅgaṃ na sukhāya yūnām |
tasmānnarairbheṣajato vidheyā gāḍhī kriyā manmathamandirasya || 2 ||

niśādvayaṃ paṅkajakeśarañca niṣpīḍya devadrumatulyabhāgam |
anena liptaṃ madanātapatraṃ prayāti saṃkocamalaṃ yuvatyāḥ || 3 ||

sadhātakīpuṣpaphalatrikeṇa jambūtvacā sārarasaṃ ghṛtena |
liptvā varāṅgaṃ madhukena tulyaṃ vṛddhāpi kanyeva bhavet purandhrī || 4 ||

pikākṣabījena manojagehaṃ vilipya yoṣā niyamaṃ caranti |
haṭhena gāḍhaṃ labhate tadaṅgaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ naraireṣa haṭhena yogaḥ || 5 ||

mṛṇālapadmaṃ payasā supiṣya dṛḍhā samāṅgī guṭikā vidheyā |
yasyā varāṅge nihitā kṣaṇena kanyātvametyāha sa mūladevaḥ || 6 ||

ikṣvākubījaṃ snuhisāraveṇa piṣṭvā varāṅgaṃ parilipya tena |
navaprasūtāpi haṭhena nārī kanyā bhavet saṃyamato na citram || 7 ||

indīvaravyāghrivacoṣaṇānāṃ turaṅgamārāsanayāminīnām |
lepena nāryāḥ smarasadmarandhraṃ saṃkocayatyāśu haṭhena yogaḥ || 8 ||

yā śakragopaṃ svayameva piṣṭvā vilimpati strī ca varāṅgadeśam | 
āhatya tasyāḥ kaṭhinaṃ ca gāḍhaṃ bhavenna cātrāsti vicāracaryā ||

madanakathanasāraḥ kṣaudratulyairvarāṅgaṃ 
śithilitamapi yasyāḥ pūritaṃ bhūya eva |
bhavati kaṭhinamuccaiḥ karkaśaṃ kāminīnāmiti 
nigadati yogaṃ rantidevo narendraḥ || 10 ||

aśvagandhairlipairghoniṃ gāḍhīkaraṇamuttamam ||

iti gāḍhīkaraṇam |

atha strīdrāvaṇam 

yadyapyaṣṭaguṇādhiko nigaditaḥ kāmo'ṅganānāṃ sadā 

no yāti dravatāṃ tathāpi jhaṭitistrī kāmināṃ saṃgame |
tasmādbheṣajasaṃprayogavidhinā saṃkṣepato drāvaṇaṃ 
kiñcitpallavayāmi nīrajadṛśāṃ prītyā paraṃ kāminām || 11 ||

sindūraciñcāphalamākṣikāṇi tulyāni yasyā madanātapatre |
pralipya tāsāṃ puruṣaprasaṅgātprāgeva vīryacyutimātanoti || 12 ||

vyoṣaṃ rajaḥ kṣaudrasamanvitaṃ vā kṣiptaṃ yadi syātsmarayaṃtragehe |
drutā bhavetsā sahasaiva nārī dṛṣṭaḥ sadāyaṃ kila yogarājaḥ || 13 ||

supakvaciñcāphalaghoṣamūlī guḍaṃ tathā mākṣikatulyabhāgam |
amībhirālipya punaḥ suliṅgaṃ bījaṃ karotyāśu nitambinīnām || 14 ||

saṭī kaṇā kṣaudramaheśabījaiḥ karpuratulyairupalipya liṃgam |
śataṃ naro yaḥ savilāsinīnāṃ retaḥ prapātaṃ kurute haṭhena || 15 ||

pārāvatapurīṣaṃ ca madhunā saindhavairyutam |
liṃgasya lepanāttena strīṇāṃ drāvaṇamuttamam || 16 ||

gokṣuvārtākyapāmargarasena liṃgalepanāt |
tatkṣaṇāddravate nārī padmapatre yathā payaḥ || 17 ||

pippalī candanaṃ caiva bṛhatī pakvatintiḍī |
etairliṅgapralepena dravennārī na saṃśayaḥ || 18 ||

agastipatradravasaṃyutena madhvājyasaṃmiśritakaṃṭakaṇena |
liptvā dhvajaṃ yo ramate'ṅganānāṃ sa śukramākarṣati śīghrameva || 19 ||

salodhradhattūrakapippalīnāṃ kṣudroṣaṇakṣaudravimiśritānām |
lepena liṃgasya karoti retaścyutiṃ vipakṣapramadājanasya || 20 ||

turagasalilamadhye bhāvitaṃ kṣetramāṣaṃ 
maricamadhukatulyāṃ pippalīṃ peṣayitvā |
pariramati vilipya svīyaliṅgaṃ naro yaḥ 
prabhavati vanitānāṃ kāmakallola mānaḥ || 21 ||

bilvapuṣpaṃ sukarpūraṃ muṇḍīpuṣpaṃ ca peṣitam |
liṃgalepena rāmāṇāṃ drāvo bhavati saṃgame || 22 ||

bṛhatīphalamūlāni pippalyo maricāni ca |
madhu rocanayā sārddhaṃ liṅgalepe dravanti tāḥ || 23 ||

kṣaudragaṃdhakalepena śilāyuktena tatphalam |
upahārapaśo raktaṃ gṛhṇīyādantarikṣataḥ || 24 ||

tacchuṣkaṃ cūrṇitaṃ sthāpyaṃ puṣpe raktāśvamāraje |
tatpuṣpaṃ dhārayedvastre tarjanyaṅguṣṭhayogataḥ |
ārvartya sammukhaṃ strīṇāṃ dṛṣṭamātre dravanti tāḥ || 25 ||

jambīraphalamadhye tu mūlaṃ vṛścikakaṇṭakam |
kṣiptvā baddhvā striyai dadyādghrāṇamātre dravanti tāḥ || 26 ||

āharedvāmajaṅghāṃ tu ṭiṭṭibhasya tu dakṣiṇaḥ || 27 ||

tanmadhye prakṣipedbhūrjjjapatramoṅkāralepitam |
raktāśvamārapuṣpeṇa mukhaṃ tasya nirodhayet |
karṇopari sthitaṃ tena dṛṣṭvā strī dravati dhruvam || 28 ||

jalena lāṅgalīmūlaṃ piṣṭvā haste pralepayet |
hastena strīkarasparśe dravatyagnau ghṛtaṃ yathā || 29 ||

maricakanakabījaiḥ pippalīlodhrayuktai(-)
rvimalamadhuvimiśrairmānavo  liptaliṅga |
smarati rativilāse kaṣṭasādhyāṃ ca nārī 
samucitaratirāgāṃ saṃvidadhyādavaśyam || 30 ||

sarveṣāṃ dravayogānāṃ mantrarājaṃ śivoditam |
japedaṣṭottaraśataṃ tatra yogasya siddhaye || 31 ||

mantra 

oṃ namo bhagavate rudrāya uḍḍāmareśvarāya 
drāvaya drāvaya strīṇāṃ madaṃ pātaya pātaya svāhā ṭhaḥ ṭhaḥ || iti || 32 ||

oṃ namo bhagavate rudrāya uḍḍāmareśvarāya drāvaya drāvaya strīṇāṃ madaṃ pātaya pātaya svāhā ṭhaḥ ṭhaḥ || 31-32 ||

iti drāvaṇam |

atha kāmadhvajasthūlīkaraṇam 

(dṛḍhīkaraṇam) 

sakuṣṭhamātaṅgabalābalānāṃ vacāśvagandhāgajapippalīnām |
turaṅgaśatrornavanītayogāllepena liṅgaṃ musalatvameti || 33 ||

dinedine yadā hyevaṃ sudhīḥ kuryātprayatnataḥ |
tadāsthūlaṃ bhavelliṅgaṃ niścitaṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ || 34 ||

salodhrakāśmīratuṅgagandhā mātaṅgagandhāparipācitena |
tailena vṛddhiṃ khalu yāti liṅgaṃ varāṅganālokamanoharaṃ tat || 35 ||

bhallātakāsthijalaśūkamathājapatramantarvidāhya matimān saha saindhavena |
etadvirūḍhabṛhatī phalatoyapiṣṭamālepanaṃ mahiṣavadvimalīkṛte'ṅge ||
sthūlaṃ mahattaraturaṅgamatulyamāśu śephaḥ karotyabhimataṃ na hi saṃśayo'sti || 36 ||

sūtakaṃ maricaṃ kuṣṭhaṃ nāgaraṃ kaṇṭakārikā |
aśvagandhā tilaṃ kṣaudraṃ saindhavaṃ śvetasarṣapāḥ || 37 ||

apāmārgo yavā māṣāḥ pippalīṃ peṣayejjalaiḥ |
lepo'yaṃ kurute vṛddhiṃ liṅgasya dṛḍhatāṃ dhruvam |
māsamātraṃ sadā liptvā mardayecca divāniśam || 38 ||

varāhavasayā liṅgaṃ madhunā saha lepayet |
sthūlaṃ dṛḍhaṃ ca dīrghaṃ ca māsālliṅgaṃ prajāyate || 39 ||

aśvagandhā vacā kuṣṭhaṃ bṛhatī ca śatāvarī |
tilaṃ tailena saṃpakvaṃ tallepaḥ sthūlaliṅgakṛt || 40 ||

aśvagaṃdhā varī kuṣṭhaṃ māṃsī siṃhīphalānvitam |
caturguṇena dugdhena tilatailaṃ vipācayet |
stanaliṅgakarṇapāṇivarddhanaṃ bhakṣaṇāditaḥ || 41 ||

ṭaṅkaṇaṃ ca mahārāṣṭrī jambūsūkaratailakam |
madhunā saha lepena liṅgaṃ syānmusalopamam || 42 || 

mahiṣīnavanītaṃ ca muśalīcūrṇamiśritam || 43 ||

dhānyarāśisthitaṃ bhāṇḍe saptāhācca samuddharet |
tena pralepayelliṅgaṃ māsaikād varddhate dhruvam || 44 ||

muśalī śītalā bhakṣyā liṅgavṛddhikarī matā |
māraṇotthaṃ kṛmi caiva kaṇṭakārīphalaṃ jalaiḥ || 45 ||

piṣṭvā liṃgapralepena sthūlaṃ bhavati niścitam |
tadvacca muśalī sājyā lepālliṃgasya dārḍhyakṛt || 46 ||

pippalīlavaṇakṣīrasitālepo'pi dīrghakṛt |
māṃsī vākṣaphalaṃ kuṣṭhamaśvagaṃdhā śatāvarī |
taile paktvā pralepena liṅgaṃsthaulyakaraṃ dhruvam || 47 ||

rohitāmatsyapittaṃ tu jalaukā lāṅgalī sadā |
anena mardayelliṅgaṃ varddhate musalopamam || 48 ||

sūtako hyaśvagandhā ca rajanī gajapippalī |
sitāyuktaṃ jalaiḥ piṣṭvā māsaikaṃ lepayettadā || 49 ||

adbhutaṃ varddhayelliṅgaṃ yonikarṇastanāni ca || 50 ||

yo markaṭīmūlamajājalena vyāluptakośaḥ śayane niśāyām |
piṣṭvā dhvajaṃ limpati tasya kāmaṃ bhavedayodaṇḍamiva kṣaṇena || 51 ||

hayāripatnīnavanītamadhye vacābalābhāgarasāmayaiśca |
lepena liṅgaṃ sahasaiva puṃsāṃ lohopamaṃ syāditi dṛṣṭametat || 52 ||

kṛṣṇāparājitāmūlaṃ grāhyaṃ khadirakīlakaiḥ |
kṛṣṇasūtraiḥ kaṭiṃ baddhvā ūrdhvaliṅgaṃ karoti ca || 53 ||

devadālīrasaṃ dhātrī kṣīrapānatsthiro dhvajaḥ |
ityevaṃ sarvayogānāṃ mantrarājaḥ śivoditaḥ |
anena maṃtritaṃ kṛtvā māsaikaṃ lepayettataḥ || 54 ||

oṃ namo bhagavate uḍḍāmareśvarāya sava sava prasava kuru kuru svāhā ṭhaḥ ṭhaḥ || 


dṛḍhīkaraṇaṃ tu vinā maṃtreṇa kāryam |
iti liṅgasthūlī karaṇam (dṛḍhīkaraṇam) |

iti kāmadhvajasthūlīkaraṇam |

atha stanavarddhanam 

kucotthāpanaṃ ca 

mātaṅgakṛṣṇāpyadhavājagandhā vacāyutā paryuṣitāmbumiśrā |
hayāripatnīnavanītayogātkurvanti pīnaṃ kucakumbhayugmam || 55 ||

tailaṃ vacādāḍimakalkasiddhaṃ siddhārthajaṃ lepanato nitāntam |
nārīkucau cārutarau ca pīnau kuryādasau yogavaraḥ pradiṣṭaḥ || 56 ||

śrīparṇikāyā rasakaṃ tu siddhaṃ tilodbhavaṃ tailavaraṃ pradiṣṭam |
lepena vakṣojayuge ca śīghraṃ vṛddhi prayātaḥ patite ramaṇyā || 57 ||

prathamakusumakāle nasyayogena pītaṃ 
saniyamamathavā syāttandulāmbho yuvatyāḥ |
kucayugalasapīnaṃkvāpi no yāti pātaṃ 
kathita iti puraivaṃ cakradattena yogaḥ || 58 ||

śālitandulokadaṃ karṣamātraṃ vāmadakṣiṇā nāsābhyāṃ nasyaṃ deyam || 

muṇḍīcūrṇaṃ daśapalaṃ toyaiścaturguṇaiḥ pacet |

arddhaśeṣaṃ haretkvāthaṃ kvāthārddhaṃ tilatailakam || 59 ||

tailaśeṣaṃ pacettena nasyaṃ pānaṃ ca kārayet |
patitaṃ yauvanaṃ strīṇāṃ māsāduttiṣṭhate dhruvam || 60 ||

śyāmā niśā balā lājā lavaṇaṃ kvāthayetsamam |
toye caturguṇe pācyaṃ pādaśeṣaṃ samāharet || 61 ||

tilatailaṃ kvāthapādaṃ tailārddhaṃ mahiṣīghṛtam |
snehaśeṣaṃ pacettailaṃ nasyena māsamātrataḥ || 62 ||

bālāstrīvṛddhanārīṇāṃ yauvanaṃ kurute'dbhutam || 63 ||

eraṇḍatailaṃ śakulasya tailaṃ tathāmabilvasya rasaṃ gṛhītvā |
saṃmarddayedūrdhvagahastakena tadā stanaṃ syātpatitaṃ navaṃ vai || 64 ||

śrīparṇīrasakarkābhyāṃ tailaṃ siddhaṃ tilodbhvam |
tattailaṃ tilake nāpi stanasyopari dāpayet || 65 ||

kāṭhinyaṃ vṛddhatāṃ yātaḥ patitau cotthitau ca tau |
vṛddhāyāḥ kanyakāyā vā hyabalāyāḥ payodharau || 66 ||

śvetobdakusumaṃ nityaṃ kṛṣṇadhenupayoyutam |
piṣṭvā stanayuge deyaṃ bhavetpīnapayodharā || 67 ||

vacāśvagandhāsaṃyuktā cāśvāripatrakaṃ tathā |
gajapippalikāyuktaṃ sadyobhinnajalena ca || 68 ||

peṣayitvā vidhānena lepayetstanamaṇḍale |
nayatte tu kadācidvai tāmratālaphalaṃ tathā || 69 ||

gaṃbhāripatrasurasaṃ tatsamaṃ tilatailakam |
samānaṃ jalabhāgaṃ ca dattvā pākaṃ samācaret || 70 ||

tailaśeṣaṃ parijñāya vastreṇa śodhayetkucau |
divāpralepanādeva lohatvaṃ jāyate'cirāt || 71 ||

iti stanavarddhanaṃ kucotthāpanam |

atha yonisaṃskāraḥ 

prakṣālayennimbakaṣāyatoyairniśājyakṛṣṇāguruguggulūnām |
dhūpena yoniṃ niśi dhūpayitvā nārī pramodaṃ vidadhāti bhartuḥ || 72 ||

prakṣālya nimbasya jalena bhūyastasyaiva valkena vilepayecca |
tyajecca ratyāścirakālabhūtaṃ gandhaṃ varāṅgasya na saṃśayo'tra || 73 ||

iti yonisaṃskāraḥ |

atha lomapātanavidhiḥ 

palāśabhasmānvitatālacūrṇe rambhāmbumiśrairupalipya bhūyaḥ |
kandarpagehaṃ mṛgalocanānāṃ romāṇi rohanti kadāpi naiva || 74 ||

ekaḥ pradeyo haritālabhāgaḥ pañca pradeyā jalajasya bhāgāḥ |
savastarorbhasmana eva pañca proktāśca bhāgāḥ kadalījalārdrāḥ || 75 ||

saṃsiddhapātreṣu ca saptaghasraṃ kṛtvā smarāgāravilepanaṃ ca |
romāṇi sarvāṇi vilāsinīnāṃ punarna rohaṃti kadācideva || 76 ||

rambhājalaissaptadinaṃ vibhāvya bhasmāni kambormasṛṇāni paścāt |
nalena yuktāni vilepanena romāṇi nirmūlayati kṣaṇena || 77 ||

tālakaṃ śaṅkhacūrṇaṃ tu maṃjiṣṭhābhasma kiṃśukam |
samabhāgapralepena romakhaṇḍanamuttamam || 78 ||

tālakaṃ śaṃkhacūrṇaṃ tu piṣṭvā ca kṣāratoyakaiḥ |
tena liptvā kacā gharme sthite gacchanti tatkṣaṇāt || 79 ||

pūgavṛkṣasya patrotthadravaiḥ piṣṭvā tu gandhakam |
tena liptvā sthite gharme romakhaṇḍanamuttamam || 80 ||

narāṇāṃ khaṇḍakeśānāṃ chucchundaryāśca tailataḥ |
na niryānti punarlepātrisaptāhe kṛte sati || 81 ||

kusumbhatailataptānāṃ saptavāraṃ tathā guṇam || 82 ||

sadyojātasya mahiṣīvatsasya malamāharet |
talliptvā veṣṭayedrātrau keśānvātāripatratāḥ ||
prātastaptodakaiḥ śaṣpāḥ patantyāmūlatotthitāḥ || 83 ||

pipīlikānāṃ kṛṣṇānāṃ sthūlānāṃ bhūgṛhaṃ haret |
chāyāśuṣkaṃ ca taccūrṇaṃ pañcāhaṃ lepayetsadā || 84 ||

pūrvavatkhaṇḍakeśānāṃ na punaḥ rohaṇaṃ bhavet || 85 ||

śaṃkhaṃ tālaṃ yavaṃ guñjāṃ kāñjikaiḥ peṣayetsadā |
lepātpatantiromāṇi pakvapatramiva drumāt |
lepānāddhanti keśāṃśca kaṭutailairmanaśśilā || 86 ||


iti lomapātanavidhiḥ |


[iti śrīnityanāthaviracite kāmaratne gāḍhīkaraṇādilomapātanaṃ nāma saptamopadeśaḥ] || 7 ||


[aṣṭamopadeśaḥ] 

atha ṣaṇḍhīkaraṇam 

tacchamanaṃ ca 

naro mūtrayate yatra kṛṣṇaṃ tatra tu vṛścikam |
nikhanyājjāyate ṣaṇḍha uddhṛte tu punaḥ sukhī || 1 ||

ajāmūtreṇa saṃbhāvyaṃ niśi ṣaḍbinducūrṇitam |
khānapānaprayogeṇa ṣaṇḍhatvaṃ jāyate nṛṇām || 2 ||

tilagokṣurayoścūrṇaṃ chāgadugdhena pācitam |
śītalaṃ madhunā yuktaṃ pibet ṣaṇḍhatvaśāntaye || 3 ||

jalaukādagdhacūrṇaṃ tu navanītena bhakṣitam |
yāvajjīvaṃ na sandehaḥ ṣaṇḍhatvaṃ prāpnuyānnaraḥ || 4 ||

dhattūrapuṣpabhakṣyeṇa punaḥ saṃpadyate sukham || 5 ||

yo goviṣāṇaṃ patitaṃ ca ghṛṣṭvā lipedratau svasya manobhavāstre |
ekāntakaṃ tatkurute'nyapatnyā nottiṣṭhate tāmapahāya patnīm || 6 ||

atyunnataṃ cāparagoviṣāṇaṃ ghṛṣṭvā punastena vilipya liṅgam |
prayāti bhūyaḥ prakṛtaṃ tadaṅgaṃ dṛṣṭo naraireṣa sadā prayogaḥ || 7 ||

niśāvicūrṇaṃ ghanasāracūrṇaṃ samīkṛtaṃ bastapayoviyuktam | ||
bhaktaṃ nipītaṃ kurute nikāmaṃ narasya ṣaṇḍhatvamiti prasiddham || 8 ||

tilasya daṇḍāviṭapasya cūrṇaṃ prasādhi rambhāpayaso'rddhamāsam |
sayāvakaṃ śarkarayānvitaṃ ca pītvā haret ṣaṇḍhakatāmavāpya || 9 ||

iti ṣaṇḍhīkaraṇaṃ tatsāmyañca | 

atha duṣṭāstrīkṛtadhvajapātotthāpanam |

bhūmicampakamūlaṃ ca saguvākaṃ samaṃ tathā |
tadbhakṣaṇādbhavetsadyo liṅgotthānaṃ na saṃśayaḥ || 10 ||

raktaśālmalimūlaṃ tu śivaṃ durgāṃ vināyakam | 
sampūjya vividhairdravyairnimantrya niśi saṃyataḥ || 11 ||

prātastvacaṃ haretsamyak śuṣkaṃ kuryācca cūrṇakam |
ghṛtena peṣitaṃ kṛtvā saindhavena sadā ruciḥ || 12 ||

prātarbhuktvā ca kiṃcittu bhoktavyaṃ praharāvadhi |
patitasya bhavelliṅgaṃsyotthānaṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ |
ayomayaṃ bhavelliṅgaṃ kodravānnaṃ vivarjayet || 13 ||

iti duṣṭāstrīkṛtadhvajapātotthānam |

atha yonibandhanaṃ mokṣaṇaṃ ca 

pūrvotthaṃ lāṃgalīmūlaṃ vāmapādasya pāṃsukam |
ekatra kārayeddhīmāndvayena śuktisaṃpuṭe || 
lepayedbhagabandhaḥ syāttakraiḥ prakṣālyamucyate || 14 ||

śmaśānacailamādāya vāmapādasya pāṃsukam |
sandhyāyāṃ bandhayettena poṭalī bhagabandhanī || 15 ||

oṃ amukībhagaṃ badhnāmi visphuraya randhraśoṇitam |
mayākṛtaṃ bhagabandhaṃ nāsti loke cikitsakāḥ || 16 ||

patirvā patimantro vā yecānye bhagamardakāḥ |
sarve vai vimukhaṃ yānti varjayetkāmukaistathā || 17 ||

oṃ ciṭhiciṭhikhaciṭikhaciṭi ṭhaḥ ṭhaḥ prayogadvayasyāyaṃ maṃtraḥ |

oṃ ciṭhiciṭhikhaciṭikhaciṭi ṭhaḥ ṭhaḥ | prayogadvayasyāyaṃ maṃtraḥ |

vacailācandanakṣīraiḥ prakṣālyāmarddayed bhagam |
yantramantrāditantreṇa yatkiñcicchatruṇā kṛtam || 18 ||

tattasyaiva bhavedyena siddhimantrassa ucyate |
saptabhirmaṃtritaṃ toyaṃ śuddhaṃ pūtaṃ pibettu yaḥ |
tasya śatrukṛto doṣaḥ śatrugehe bhaviṣyati || 19 ||

maṃtra

oṃ vajramuṣṭi vajrakībāḍī vajrabāndhau daśadvāra vajrapāṇīpibeccāṅge ḍākinī ḍāpinī rakṣova sarvāṅge maṃtrajayo śatrubhayo ḍākinīvāvoṃ jānuvāyau kāli kāli śāmanate brahmākī dhīśusāśu ḍākinī milikari varayo moro jīḍubhātakaretī patne pānīkare gu(-)ā kare yāne kare sūte kare parihāsekara nayana kaṭākṣi kare āpona hāthe parahāte jiyati saṃcāre kilanī potani anintuṣavarīkare ete vijñāna ahinanageyo mohikaretsārākuṭhititskemasarūpadre | oṃ mosiddhi gurūrapāyasvīliṃgaṃ mahādevakī ājñā ||

elāphalaṃ vāsavagopacūrṇaṃ guptaṃ kṣipedyoṣidusyasthamārge |
tasyaiva liṃgasya varapraveśaṃ syāttatra nānyasya kadācideva || 20 ||

gavyena dadhnā mathitaṃ vidhāya prakṣālayettena tadaṅgamuccaiḥ |
bhavedvarāṅgaṃ prakṛtaṃ yuvatyā ityāha karttā haramekhalāyāḥ || 21 ||

ākāśadeśe patitaṃ gṛhītvā yoṣinnakhaṃ dantamalaṃ supiṣṭvā |
liptvā dhvajaṃ tena ramettato yāṃ tasyāvināśaḥ puruṣāntareṇa || 22 ||

nirghātalohasya jalena bhūyaḥ prakṣālanaṃ kāmagṛhasya kuryāt |
punaḥ samāsādayati pradṛṣṭaṃ nārī tadaṅgaṃ khalu pūrvarūpam || 23 ||

muhurmuhuryā mathitena nārī prakṣālayetsaptadināni yatnāt |
tasyāstadaṅgaṃ punareva bhūyātpūrvānurūpaṃ na hi saṃśayosti || 24 ||

iti yonibandhanaṃ mokṣaṇam ca |

atha gṛhakodārakanivāraṇam 

vadhūṭanīyamūlaṃ tu tasyā hastena bandhanāt |
gṛhakodārakaṃ na syādyāvaddhaste bandhanam || 25 ||

iti gṛhakodārakanivāraṇam |

atha naṣṭapuṣpāyāḥ puṣpakaraṇam 

jyotiṣmatīkomalapatramagnau bhṛṣṭaṃ japāyāḥ kusumaṃ ca piṣṭam |
gṛhāmbunā pītamidaṃ yuvatyāḥ karoti puṣpaṃ smaramandirasya || 26 ||

lāṅgalīkandacūrṇaṃ vā apāmārgasya mūlakam |
indravāruṇikāmūlaṃ yonisthaṃ puṣpabandhanut || 27 ||

pārāvatapurīṣaṃ ca madhunā saṃpibettu yaḥ |
rajasvalā bhavennārī mūladevena bhāṣitam || 28 ||

tilamūlakaṣāyaṃ tu brahmadaṇḍīyamūlakam |
yaṣṭī trikaṭukaṃ cūrṇaṃ kvāthayuktaṃ ca pāyayet |
puṣparodhe raktagulme strīṇāṃ sadyaḥ praśasyate || 29 ||

tilakvāthe guḍaṃ tryūṣaṃ tilabhāgayutaṃ pibet |
kvāthaṃ raktabhave gulme naṣṭapuṣpe ca yojayet || 30 ||

dūrvādalaṃ tandulatulyabhāgaṃ niṣpiṣya piṣṭaṃ paripācitañca |
tadbhakṣayitvā vanitā pranaṣṭaṃ puṣpaṃ labheta svabalānurūpam || 31 ||

iti naṣṭapuṣpāyāḥ puṣpakaraṇam |

atha garbhasrāvaṇam 

tatrābhinavagarbhasrāvaṇam 

kṛte jāre kṣipedyonau tilatailāktasaindhavam |
dravate tatkṣaṇādeva śukrapuṣpaṃ sravatyapi || 32 ||

iti garbhasrāvaṇam |

atha garbhapātanam 

kāṇḍameraṇḍapatrasya yonāvaṣṭāṅgulaṃ kṣipet |
cāturmāsyo bhaved garbhaḥ sravate tatkṣaṇādapi || 33 ||

devadālīyacūrṇaṃ tu karṣaikaṃ toyapeṣitam |
pibedgarbhavatī nārī garbhaṃ sravati tatkṣaṇāt || 34 ||

dhattūramūlikā puṣye gṛhītā kaṭisaṃsthitā |
garbhaṃ nivārayatyeva raṇḍāveśyādiyoṣitām || 35 ||

rājikāṃ tilatailañca piṣṭvā nārī ṛto pibet |
tridinaṃ tena garbhasya sambhavo naiva jāyate || 36 ||

babbūlasya tu puṣpāṇi godugdhena pibedṛtau |
yā nārī garbhasambhūtiḥ punastasyā na jāyate || 37 ||

pibetprasūtisamaye kāñjiyukta jayābhavam |
puṣpaṃ na sā prasūtiṃ ca dhatte no garbhasaṃbhavaḥ || 38 ||

gṛhītaṃ revatī(-)ṛkṣe pippalasya ca vandakam |
godugdhe so'pi bhoktāraṃ mahāgarbhaṃ nivārayet || 39 ||

nirguṇḍīdravasaṃpiṣṭaṃ citramūlaṃ madhuplutam |
