karṣaṃ bhuktvā pātayati garbhaṃ raṇḍākulodbhavā || 40 ||

iti garbhapātanam |

atha raktanivāraṇam 

dhātrīṃ ca pathyāṃca rasāñjanaṃ ca kṛtvā vicūrṇaṃ sajalaṃ nipītam |
atyantaraktotthitamugravegaṃ nivārayet setunivāmbupūram || 41 ||

śelutvacā miśritatandulena vidhāya piṣṭaṃ viniyojanīyam |
kandarpagehe mṛgalocanāyā raktaṃ nihantyāśu haṭhena yogaḥ || 42 ||

mūlaṃ tu śarapuṅkhāyāḥ peṣayettandulodakaiḥ |
pāyayetkarṣamātraṃ tadatiraktapraśāntaye || 43 ||

kuśasya mūlaṃ kadalīdalaṃ vā balā siphā vā badarīphalaṃ vā |
guḍūcikā taṇḍulavāripītā strīṇāmanekaṃ rudhiraṃ jayecca || 44 ||

kuraṇṭakasya mūlāni madhukaḥ śvetacandanam |
yuktyā piṣṭvākṣamātraṃ tu pāyayettandulāmbunā || 45 ||

sakṛtpītvā māṣayūṣaṃ pradarātparimucyate |
ghṛtabhṛṣṭaṃ māṣayūṣabhojanaṃ śvetacandanam || 46 ||

candanaṃ kṣīrasaṃyuktaṃ saghṛtaṃ pāyayedbhiṣak |
śarkarāmadhusaṃyuktamasṛksrāvavināśanam || 47 ||

dārvīrasāñjanavṛṣābdakirātabilvabhallātakairatha kṛto madhunā kaṣāyaḥ | 
pīto jayatyatibalaṃ pradaraṃ saśūlaṃ pītaṃ vitāruṇavilohitanīlakṛṣṇam || 48 ||

aśokasya tvacā siddhaṃ kṣīraṃ raktaharaṃ pibet |
peṭārikāyāḥ patraṃ ca māṣacūrṇena saṃyutam || 49 ||

rambhādalairveṣṭayitvā dāhayecca prayatnataḥ |
tasya bhakṣaṇamātreṇa hyatiraktanivāraṇam || 50 ||

tanmūlaṃ tandulaiḥ piṣṭvā piṣṭakaṃ bharjjayed budhaḥ |
tasya bhakṣaṇamātreṇa raktādivikṛtiṃ haret || 51 ||

tasya valkalacūrṇaṃ tu bhṛṣṭatandulacūrṇakam |
bhakṣaṇādeva tadraktaṃ strīṇāṃ śamayati dhruvam || 52 ||

śatāvaryāstu mūlasya nijadrāvaṃ samāharet |
catvāriṃśatpalānyevaṃ vastrapūtaṃ prayatnataḥ || 53 ||

dravatulyagavāṃ kṣīraṃ kṣīrasya dviguṇaṃ ghṛtam |
jīvantikolamandārā atasī kṣīrakākulī || 54 ||

mudgaparṇī māṣaparṇī mahāmedā śatāvarī |
drākṣāpāraśukoyaṣṭijīrakaṃ pratikārṣikam || 55 ||

palārddhaṃ madhukaṃ puṣpaṃ sarvamekatra pācayet |
ghṛtaśeṣaṃ samuttārya śīte jāte ca nikṣipet || 56 ||

palāṣṭakaṃ kaṇācūrṇaṃ kṣaudraṃ vā pippalāṣṭakam |
sitādaśapalaṃ yojyamidaṃ śatāvarīghṛtam || 57 ||

lehyaṃ karṣaṃ śamedāśu dussādhyamatiraktajam |
doṣaṃ kṣayaṃ ca mandāgniṃ hṛdrogaṃ grahaṇīgraham |
kāmalāṃ vātarogāṃśca aśmarīṃ ca śirograham || 58 ||

iti raktanivāraṇam |

atha vandhyānāṃ garbhadhāraṇam 

janmavandhyā kākavandhyā mṛtavatsā kvacitstriyaḥ |
tāsāṃ putrodayārthāya śambhunā sūcitaṃ purā || 59 ||

janmavandhyā cikitsā 

samūlapatrāṃ sarpākṣīṃ ravivāre samuddharet |
ekavarṇagavāṃ kṣīre kanyā hastena peṣayet || 60 ||

ṛtukāle pibedvandhyā palārddhaṃ taddinedine |
kṣīraśālyamudgaṃ ca laghvāhāraṃ pradāpayet || 61 ||

evaṃ saptadinaṃ kuryād vandhyā bhavati garbhiṇī |
udvegaṃ bhayaśokaṃ ca divānidrāṃ vivarjjayet || 62 ||

na karma kārayetkiñcidvarjjayecchītamātapam |
na tayā paramāṃ sevāṃ kārayetpūrvavatkriyām |
patisaṅgādgarbhalābho nātra kāryā vicāraṇā || 63 ||

ekameva tu rudrākṣaṃ sarpākṣīkarṣamātrakam || 64 ||

pūrvavacca gavāṃ kṣīrair(-)ṛtukāle pradāpayet |
mahāgaṇeśamantreṇa rakṣāṃ tasyānubandhayet || 65 ||

evaṃ taptadinaṃ kuryād vandhyā bhavati putriṇī |
oṃ dadanmahāgaṇapate rakṣāmṛtaṃ matsutaṃ dehi || 66 ||

patramekaṃ palāśasya garbhiṇīpayasānvitam |
pītvā ca labhate putraṃ rūpavantaṃ na saṃśayaḥ |

pathyamuktaṃ yathāpūrvaṃ tadvatsaptadināvadhi || 67 ||

oṃ dadanmahāgaṇapate rakṣāmṛtaṃ matsutaṃ dehi |

devadālīyamūlaṃ tu grāhayetpuṣyabhāskare || 68 ||

niṣkatrayaṃ gavāṃ kṣīraiḥ pūrvavatkramayogataḥ |
vandhyā ca labhate putraṃ deyaṃ pathyaṃ yathāpurā || 69 ||

śītatoyena saṃpiṣṭaṃ śarapuṃkhīyamūlakam |
karṣaṃ pītvā labhed garbhaṃ pūrvavatkramayogataḥ || 70 ||

mustā priyaṃgusauvīraṃ lākṣākṣaudraṃ samaṃ pibet |
karṣaṃ tandulatoyena vandhyā bhavati putriṇī |
pathyamuktaṃ yathāpūrvaṃ tadvatsaptadinaṃ pibet || 71 ||

samūlāṃ sahadevīṃ ca saṃgṛhya puṣyabhāskare || 72 ||

chāyāśuṣkaṃ ca taccūrṇamekavarṇagavāṃ payaḥ |
pūrvavatpibate nārī vandhyā bhavati gurviṇī || 73 ||

mūlaṃ śiphā vā kila lakṣmaṇāyā ṛtau nipīya tridinaṃ payobhiḥ |
kṣīrānnacaryā niyamena bhuṅkte putraṃ prasūte vanitā na citram || 74 ||

sapippalīkeśaraśṛṅgaveraṃ kṣudropaṇaṃ gavyaghṛtena pītam |
vandhyāpi putraṃ labhate haṭhena yogastu so'yaṃ munibhiḥ pradiṣṭaḥ || 75 ||

turaṅgagandhā ghṛtavārisiddhamājyaṃ payaḥ snānadine ca pītvā |
prāpnoti garbhaṃ niyamaṃ carantī vandhyā ca nūnaṃ puruṣaprasaṅgāt || 76 ||

puṣyārkayogoddhṛtalakṣmaṇāyā mūlaṃ tathā vajratarośca piṣṭvā |
apyekavarṇāpayasā nipītaṃ striyaḥ smṛtaṃ putrakaraṃ munīndraiḥ || 77 ||

kandamūlaṃ ghṛtaiḥ piṣṭvā ṛtau sā garbhiṇī bhavet || 78 ||

puṣyoddhṛtaṃ lakṣmaṇāmeva cūrṇaṃ puṃsā nipiṣṭaṃ saghṛtaṃ nipītam |
kṣīrodanaṃ prāśya patiprasaṅgād garbhaṃvidadhyāttaruṇī na citram || 79 ||

kṛṣṇāparājitāmūlaṃ bastakṣīreṇa saṃpibet |
ṛtusnātā tridhā yā tu vandhyā garbhadharā bhavet || 80 ||

nāgakeśarakaṃ cūrṇaṃ nūtanaṃ gavyadugdhataḥ |
pibetsaptadinaṃ dugdhaṃ ghṛtairbhojanamācaret |
tadṛtau labhate garbhaṃ sā nārī patisaṃgatā || 81 ||

putrajīvakapatraikaṃ pibetkṣīrair(-)ṛtau ca yā || 82 ||

patisaṅgācca sā nārī satyaṃ putravatī bhavet |
tasya mūlaṃ caikavarṇākṣīraiḥ pītvā ca putriṇī || 83 ||

kākolyau lakṣmaṇāmūlaṃ ṣaṣṭikasya ca tandulam |
nāryekavarṇāpayasā pītvā garbhavatī ṛtau || 84 ||

aśvinyāṃ bodhivṛkṣasya vandākaṃ grāhayed budhaḥ |
gokṣīraiḥ pānamātreṇa vandhyā putravatī bhavet || 85 ||

tilarasaguḍakaṃ vai gopurīṣāgniyogāt |
taruṇavṛṣabhamūtraṃ prasthayuktaṃ vipakvam |
ṛtudivasavimadhye saptavāraiśca pītaṃ 
janayati sutamekaṃ niścitaṃ puṣpitaiva || 86 ||

kadambapatraṃ śvetaṃ ca bṛhatīmūlameva ca |
etāni samabhāgāni hyajākṣīreṇa peṣayet || 87 ||

trirātraṃ pañcarātraṃ vā pibedetanmahauṣadham |
ṛtau nipīyamāne tu garbho bhavati niścitam || 88 ||

gokṣurasya tu bījaṃ ca pibennirguṇḍikārasaiḥ |
trirātraṃ saptarātraṃ vā vandhyā bhavati putriṇī || 89 ||

karkoṭabījacūrṇaṃ tu ekavarṇaṃ gavāṃ payaḥ |
ṛtau nipīyamāne tu garbho bhavati niścitam || 90 ||

bhagākhye caiva nakṣatre vaṭavṛkṣasya mūlakam |
haste baddhvā labhetputraṃ sundaraṃ kulavarddhanam || 91 ||

aśvatthasya tu vandākaṃ pūrvedyuḥ sunimaṃtritam |
ṛtusnāte tu pītaṃ syādapi vandhyā labhetsutam || 92 ||

ekavarṇasavatsāyā goḥ kṣīreṇa supeṣitam |
bhāvitaṃ vaṭavandākaṃ pītaṃ vandhyā sutaṃ labhet || 93 ||

iti janmavandhyācikitsā |

atha kākavandhyācikitsā 

pūrvaṃ putravatī yā sā kvacidvandhyā bhavedyadi |
kākavandhyā tu sā jñeyā cikitsāsyāstu kathyate || 94 ||

viṣṇukrāntā samūlāṃ tu piṣṭvā dugdhaistu māhiṣaiḥ |
mahiṣīnavanītena ṛtukāle tu bhakṣayet || 95 ||

evaṃ saptadinaṃ kuryātpathyamuktaṃ ca pūrvavat |
garbhaṃ sā labhate nārī kākavandhyā suśobhanam || 96 ||

aśvagandhīyamūlaṃ tu grāhayetpuṣyabhāskare || 97 ||

peṣayenmahiṣīkṣīraiḥ palārddhaṃ bhakṣayetsadā |
saptāhāllabhate garbhaṃ kākavandhyā cirāyuṣam || 98 ||

iti kākavandhyācikitsā |

atha mṛtavatsācikitsā 

garbhaṃ saṃjātamātreṇa pakṣānmāsācca vatsarāt |
mriyate dvitrivarṣādvā yasyāḥ sā mṛtavatsakā |
tatra yogaḥ prakarttavyo yathā śaṅkarabhāṣitam || 99 ||

mārgaśīrṣe'thavā jyeṣṭhe pūrṇāyāṃ lepite gṛhe || 100 ||

nūtanaṃ kalaśaṃ pūrṇaṃ gandhatoyena kārayet |
śākhāphalasamāyuktaṃ navaratnasamanvitam || 101 ||

suvarṇasūtrikāyuktaṃ ṣaṭkoṇamaṇḍale sthitam |
tanmadhye pūjayeddevīmekāntīṃ nāma viśrutām || 102 ||

gandhapuṣpākṣatairdhūpairdīpairnaivedyasaṃyutaiḥ |
arcayedbhaktibhāvena madyamāṃsaiḥ samatsyakaiḥ || 103 ||

brāhmī māheśvarī caiva kaumārī vaiṣṇavī tathā |
vārāhī ca tathendrāṇī ṣaṭsu patreṣu mātaraḥ || 104 ||

pūjayenmantrabījaiśca phaiṃkārairnāmaviśrutaiḥ |


pūjāmaṃtraḥ

oṃ hrīṃ phaiṃ ekāntidevatāyai namaḥ |
anena mantreṇa pūjā japaśca kāryaḥ ||
dadhibhaktaiśca piṇḍāni saptasaṃkhyāni kārayet || 105 ||

ṣaṭsaṃkhyāḥ ṣaṭsu patreṣu mātṛbhyaḥ kalpayetpṛthak |
bilvābhaṃ saptamaṃ piṇḍaṃ śucisthāne bahiḥkṣipet || 106 ||

oṃ hrīṃ phaiṃ ekāntidevatāyai namaḥ 

tadbhuktvā gṛhamāgaccheccakrāgre yāgamācaret |
kanyakā yoginī vāmā bhojayetsakuṭumbakaiḥ || 107 ||

dakṣiṇāṃ dāpayettāsāṃ devatāgre ca nānyathā |
visṛjya devatāṃ cātha nadyāṃtatkalaśodakam || 108 ||

sakulaṃ vīkṣayeddhīmāñchubhena śubhamādiśet |
viparīte punaḥ kāryaṃ yāvattāvatsusiddhidam || 109 ||

prativarṣamidaṃ kuryyārddīrghajīvīsutaṃ labhet || 110 ||

prāṅmukhī kṛttikā(-)ṛkṣe vandhyākarkoṭakīṃ haret |
tatkandaṃ peṣayettoyaiḥ karṣamātraṃ sadā pibet |
ṛtukāle tu saptāhaṃ dīrghajīvī suto bhavet || 111 ||

yā bījapūradrumamūlamekaṃ kṣīreṇa siddhaṃ haviṣāvamiśram |
ṛto tu pītvā supatiṃ prayāti dīrghāyuṣaṃ sā tanayaṃ prasūte || 112 ||

iti vandhyānāṃgarbhadhāraṇam |

atha phalaghṛtam 

mañjiṣṭhā madhukaṃ kuṣṭhaṃ triphalā śarkarā balā |
medā payasyā kākolī mūlaṃ caivāśvagandhajam |
ajamodā haridredve hiṃguḥ kaṭukarohiṇī || 113 ||

utpalaṃ kumudaṃ drākṣā kākolyau candanadvayam |
eteṣāṃ kārṣikairbhāgairghṛtaprasthaṃ vipācayet || 114 ||

śatāvarīrasaṃ kṣīraṃ ghṛtaṃ deyaṃ caturguṇaṃ |
sarpiretannaraḥ pītvā nityaṃ strīṣu vṛṣāyate || 115 ||

putrāñjanayate nārī medhāvipriyadarśanāt |
yā caivāsthiragarbhā syādyā nārī janayenmṛtam || 116 ||

alpāyuṣaṃ vā janayedyā ca kanyāṃ prasūyate |
yonidoṣe rajodoṣe garbhasrāve ca śasyate || 117 ||

prajāvarddhanamāyuṣyaṃ sarvagrahanivāraṇam |
nāmnā phalaghṛtaṃ hyetadrahasyaṃ parikīrtitam || 118 ||

jīvadvatsaikavarṇāyā ghṛtamatra tu dīyate |
araṇyagomayenātra vahnerjvālā pradīyate || 119 ||

iti phalaghṛtam |

atha garbhasrāvarakṣā 

akasmātprathame māsi garbhe bhavati vedanā |
gokṣīraiḥ peṣayettulyaṃ padmakośīracandanam |
palamātraṃ pibennārī tryahādgarbhaḥ sthiro bhavet || 120 ||

athavā madhukaṃ dāru śākavṛkṣasya bījakam |
saṃpiṣya kṣīrakākolī pibetkṣīraistu gobhavaiḥ || 121 ||

nīlotpalaṃ mṛṇālaṃ ca yaṣṭī karkaṭaśṛṅgikā |
gokṣīraistu dvitīye ca pītvā śāmyanti vedanāḥ || 122 ||

athavāśvatthavalkaṃ ca tilaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ śatāvarīm |
mañjiṣṭhāsahitāṃ piṣṭvā pibetkṣīraiścaturguṇaiḥ || 123 ||

śrīkhaṇḍaṃ tagaraṃ kuṣṭhaṃ mṛṇālaṃ padmakeśaram |
pibecchītodakaiḥ piṣṭaṃ tṛtīye vedanāvatī || 124 ||

athavā kṣīrakākolīṃ balāṃ piṣṭvā payaḥ pibet || 125 ||

nīlotpalaṃ mṛṇālāni gokṣuraṃ ca kaśerukam |
turyamāse gavāṃ kṣīraiḥ pibecchāmyati vedanā || 126 ||

athavā madhukaṃ rāsnā śyāmā brāhmaṇayaṣṭikāḥ |
anantāṃ peṣayitvā tu gavyakṣīraiśca saṃpibet || 127 ||

punarnavāṃ sakākolīṃ tagaraṃ nīlamutpalam |
gokṣuraṃ pañcame māse garbhakleśaharaṃ pibet || 128 ||

athavā bṛhatīyugmaṃ yajñāṅgaṃ kuṭmalaṃ varam |
goghṛtaṃ kṣīrasaṃyuktaṃ pibetpiṣṭvā ca pañcame || 129 ||

sitāṃ kapitthamajjāṃ ca śītatoyena peṣayet |
ṣaṣṭhe māsi gavāṃ kṣīraiḥ pibetkleśanivṛttaye || 130 ||

athavā gokṣuraṃ śigrumadhukaṃ pṛśniparṇikām |
balāyuktaṃ pibetpiṣṭvā godugdhaiḥ ṣaṣṭhamāsake || 131 ||

kaśeruṃ pauṣkaraṃ mūlaṃ śṛṅgāṭaṃ nīlamutpalam |
piṣṭvā ca saptame māsi kṣīraiḥ pītvā praśāmyati || 132 ||

athavā madhukaṃ drākṣā śṛṅgāṭañca kaśerukam |
mṛṇālaṃ śarkarāyuktaṃ kṣīraiḥ peyaṃ tu saptame || 133 ||

yaṣṭī padmākṣakaṃ mustaṃ keśaraṃ gajapippalīm |
nīlotpalaṃ gavāṃ kṣīraiḥ pibedaṣṭamamāsake || 134 ||

athavā bilvamūlaṃ tu kapitthaṃ bṛhatīphalam |
ikṣupaṭolayomūlamebhiḥ kṣīraṃ prasādhayet || 135 ||

tatkṣīramambhasā pītvā garbhe śāmyati vedanā || 136 ||

viśālābījakaṃkolaṃ madhunā saha peṣayet |
vedanā navame māsi śāntimāpnoti nānyathā || 137 ||

athavā madhukaṃ śyāmā hyanantā kṣīrakākulī |
ebhiḥ siddhaṃ pibetkṣīraṃ navame vedanāvatī || 138 ||

śarkarā gostanī drākṣā sakṣaudraṃ nīlamutpalam |
pāyayeddaśame māsi gavāṃ kṣīraiḥ praśāntaye || 139 ||

athavā śuṇṭhisaṃsiddhaṃ gokṣīraṃ daśame pibet |
athavā madhukaṃ dāru śuṇṭhikṣīreṇa saṃpibet || 140 ||

sāmānya cikitsā 

dhātryañjanaṃ sāvarayaṣṭikākyaṃ tryahaṃ nipītaṃ pramadā haṭhena |
saptāhamātraṃ viniyojya nārī stabhnāti garbha calitaṃ na citram || 141 ||

kṣaudraṃ vṛkṣaṃ candanasindhujātaṃ mahendramājyaṃ payasā supiṣṭam |
garbhaṃ kṣarantaṃ pratihanti śīghraṃ yogo'yamuktaḥ kila mūladevaiḥ || 142 ||

kulālahastodbhavakardamasya vatsīpayaḥ kṣaudrayutasya mātram |
garbhacyuti śūlamayīṃ nivārya karoti garbhaṃ prakṛtaṃ haṭhena || 143 ||

kaśeruśṛṅgāṭakajīrakāṇi payoghanairaṇḍaśatāvarībhiḥ |
siddhaṃ payaḥśarkarayā vimiśraṃ saṃsthāpayed garbhamudasya śūlam || 144 ||

kandaṃ kaumudakasya mākṣikayutaṃ kṣīrājyamiśraṃ pibet 
saptāhaṃ sitayā supakvasabalāśītīkṛtaṃ vāyunā |
garbhasrāvamarocakaṃ sapavanaṃ śokaṃ tridoṣaṃ vamiṃ 
śūlaṃ sarvavidhaṃ nihanti niyamādevaṃ ca yattatsmṛtam || 145 ||

hrīberātiviṣāmustāmaricaiḥ saṃśṛtaṃ jalam |
dadyādgarbhe pracalite pradare kukṣirujyapi || 146 ||

kuvalayakandaṃ satilaṃ pītvā kṣīreṇa madhusitāyuktam |
gurutaradoṣaiścalitaṃ garbhaṃ saṃsthāpayedāśu || 147 ||

nīlotpalamṛṇālāni madhukaṃ śarkarātilāḥ |
dravamāṇeṣu garbheṣu garbhesthāpanamuttamam || 148 ||

iti garbhasrāvarakṣā |

atha garbhaśuṣkanivāraṇam 

gokṣīraṃ śarkarāyuktaṃ garbhaśuṣkapraśāntaye |
pibedvā madhukaṃ cūrṇaṃ gambhārīphalacūrṇakam |
samāṃśaṃ gavyadugdhena gurviṇī tatpraśāntaye || 149 ||

iti garbhaśuṣkanivāraṇam |

atha sukhaprasavavidhiḥ 

śvetapunarnavāmūlacūrṇaṃ yonau praveśayet |
kṣaṇātprasūyate nārī garbheṇātiprapīḍitā || 150 ||

uttarābhimukhaṃ grāhyaṃ śvetaguñjīyamūlakam |
kaṭyāṃ baddhvāvimuktaṃ ca garbhaputraṃ tu tatkṣaṇāt || 151 ||

vāsakasya tu mūlaṃ tu cottarasthaḥ samuddharet |
kaṭyāṃ baddhvā saptasūtraiḥ sukhaṃ nārī prasūyate || 152 ||

uttare ca samāloḍyaṃ śvetaguñjāphalaṃ kiyat |
sukhaprasavamāpnoti tatkṣaṇānnātra saṃśayaḥ |
yoniṃ vā lepayettena sā sukhena prasūyate || 153 ||

sahadevyāśvamūlaṃ vā kaṭisthaṃ prasavetsukham || 154 ||

apāmārgasya mūlaṃ tu grāhayeccaturaṅgulam |
nārī praveśayedyonau tatkṣaṇātsā prasūyate || 155 ||

toyena lāṅgalīkandaṃ ghṛṣṭvā yoniṃ pralepayet |
nābhiṃ ca lepayettena tatkṣaṇātsūyate dhruvam || 156 ||

guñjāphalārddhakhaṇḍaṃ ca toyapūgaṃ tathārddhakam |
pibedvā toyapiṣṭaṃ ca sā sukhena prasūyate || 157 ||

guñjātaromūlayugaṃ vidhānādutpāṭya puṣye ca ravau nibaddham |
kaṭītale mūrddhani nīlasūtraiḥ śīghraṃ prasūtiṃ kurute'ṅganāyāḥ || 158 ||

agāradhūmaṃ gṛhavāriṇā vā pītvā'balā śīghrataraṃ prasūte |
alambuṣāmūlamatho nibaddhaṃ yogadvayaṃ bhūpatirityavādīt || 159 ||

samātuluṅgaṃ madhukasya cūrṇaṃ madhvājyamiśraṃ pramadā nipīya |
vyathāvihīnaṃ prasavaṃ haṭhena prāpnoti naivātra vikalpabuddhiḥ || 160 ||

atra mātuluṅgasya mūlaṃ yojyam na tu phalam kvāthayitvā peyam |

daśamūlīśṛtaṃ toyaṃ ghṛtasaindhavasaṃyutam |
śūlāturā pibennārī sā sukhena prasūyate || 161 ||

sukhaprasavamantraḥ 

oṃ manmatha oṃ manmatha oṃ manmatha manmathavāhini lambodara muñca muñca svāhā 

anena mantreṇa jalaṃ sutaptaṃ pātuṃ pradeyaṃ śucinā nareṇa |
toyābhipānāt khalu garbhavatyā prasūyate śīghrataraṃ sukhena || 162 ||

oṃ manmatha oṃ manmatha oṃ manmatha manmatha vāhini lambodara muñca muñca svāhā |

oṃkāraṃ ca haṃkāraṃ ca akāreṇa supūjitam |
oṃkāraṃ śirasaṃ kṛtvā ante namastrimūrtaye || 163 ||

aṃ oṃ hāṃ namastrimūrtaye |

anenaiva tu mantreṇa japtavyaṃ sūtikāgṛhe |
sukhaṃ prasavamāpnoti sā putraṃ labhate dhruvam || 164 ||

iti sukhaprasavavidhiḥ |

atha bālānāṃ sūtikāyāśca bhūta(-)pretādikanivāraṇam 

bilvamūlaṃ devadāruṃ gośṛṅgaṃ ca priyaṅgu ca |
mārjārasya malaṃ kuṣṭhaṃ vaṃśatvagajamūtrakaiḥ || 165 ||

piṣṭvā dhūpo nihantyāśu grahabhūtajvarādayaḥ |
oṃdrāvitaṃ tāpe ṭhaṃ ṭhaḥ svāhā anena dhūpaṃ dadyāt |
ḍākinī rākṣasāḥ pretā piśācā brahmarākṣasāḥ || 
aikāhiko dvyāhikaśca jvaro naśyati tatkṣaṇāt || 166 ||

oṃ drāvitaṃ tāpe ṭhaṃ ṭhaḥ svāhā |

śrīvāsaṃ sarṣapaṃ kuṣṭhaṃ vacā tailaṃ ghṛtaṃ vasā |
dhūpo bālagrahe deyo bālānāṃ grahaśāntaye || 167 ||

śirīṣanimbayoḥ patraṃ gośṛṅgasya tvacā vacaḥ |
vaṃśatvak śikhipicchaṃ ca kaṃgunā ca samaṃ ghṛtam || 168 ||

dhūpo bālagrahān hanti svayaṃ mantreṇa mantrayet |

dhūpamantraḥ 

oṃ drutaṃ muñca muñca uḍḍāmareśvara ājñāpayati svāhā || || 169 ||

punarnavānimbapatrasarṣapaghṛtaviracito dhūpaḥ |
garbhiṇyā bālānāṃ satataṃ rakṣākaraḥ kathitaḥ || 170 ||

oṃ drutaṃ muñca muñca uḍḍāmareśvara ājñāpayati svāhā |

dāḍimasya tu vandākaṃ jyeṣṭhā(-)ṛkṣe samuddharet |
dvāre baddhaṃ tu bālānāṃ sarvagrahanivāraṇam || 171 ||

puṣyārke śvetaguñjāyā mūlamuddhṛtya dhārayet |
bālānāṃ kaṇṭhadeśe ca ḍākinībhayanāśanam || 172 ||

śvetāparājitāpatraṃ jayāpatraṃ dvayo rasam |
nasyaṃ kuryyātpalāyante ḍākinīdānavādayaḥ || 173 ||

sarpatvakśikhijāriṣṭapallavaṃ rajanī vacā |
rasonaṃ hiṃgu golomaśṛṅgīmaricamākṣikaiḥ |
dhūpaḥ sarvajvaraghno'yaṃ kumārāṇāṃ jvarāpahaḥ || 174 ||

chucchundarī malaṃ māṃsaṃ haridrābilvapatrakam || 175 ||

indrajaṃ sarṣapaṃ patraṃ dhūpayogena yojitam |
nihanti rodanaṃ rātrau bālasyāśu na saṃśayaḥ || 176 ||

matsyarājasya pittena marice bhāvayed budhaḥ |
ravivāre raudraśuṣkamañjanātsarvabhūtahṛt || 177 ||

narasiṃhasya mantraṃ tu sakṛduccaritaṃ haret |
ḍākinīgrahabhūtāni tamaḥ sūryyodaye yathā || 178 ||

nṛsiṃhamantraḥ 

oṃ narasiṃhāya hiraṇyakaśiputravakṣaḥ sthalavidāraṇāya tribhuvanavyāpakāya bhūtapretapiśācaḍākinīkulonmūlanāya stambhodbhavāya samastadoṣān hara hara visara visara paca paca hana hana kampaya kampaya matha matha hrīṃ hrīṃ hrīṃ phaṭ phaṭ ṭhaḥ ṭhaḥ ṭhaḥ ehi ehi rudra ājñāpayati svāhā ||

oṃ hrīṃ hrīṃ hrīṃ hrūṃ hrūṃ hrūṃ phaṭ svāhā |

anena sarṣapamabhimantritaṃ kṛtvā rogiṇaṃ prahārayet tadā sarve grahāḥ palāyante ||

bālagrahābhibhūtānāṃ baliṃ yatnena kalpayet |
śuci paktvā tu saptāhaṃ matsyaṃ māṃsaṃ surāphalam || 179 ||

puṣpadhūpākṣataṃ gandhaṃ dīpaṃ ca dakṣiṇodikam |

catuṣpathe kṣipedātrau śuddhe nūtanakharpare || 
śanau vā kujavāre vā bāladoṣopaśāntaye || 180 ||

balidānamantraḥ 

oṃ sarvabhūtebhyo baliṃ gṛhāṇa gṛhāṇa svāhā |

atha bālasyāhituṇḍikādiroganivāraṇam 

candragraste śikhīmūlaṃ vidhānenoddhared budhaḥ |
baddhaṃ gale ca jaghane bālo'hituṇḍikāṃ jayet || 181 ||

śvetārkamūlaṃ saṃgṛhya gṛhastambhe ca bandhayet |
puṣyārke vā ravervāre bālo'hituṇḍikāṃ jayet || 182 ||

udumbarabhavaṃ mūlaṃ śiśukaṭyāṃ ca bandhayet |
bṛhatkūṣmāṇḍavṛttaṃ vā tenāhituṃḍikāṃ jayet || 183 ||

iti bālasyāhituṇḍikārogānivāraṇam |

atha strīṇāṃ puṣparakṣā 

palāśarājādanayoḥ phalāni puṣpāṇyatho śālmalipādapasya |
ājyena māsārddhanidaṃ pibanti rakṣā bhavenniścitameva puṣpe || 184 ||

tuṣāmbunā pāvakavṛkṣamūlaṃ niḥkvāthayitvā niyamaṃ carantī |
ṛtvantakāle tridinaṃ purandhrī rakṣā bhavedāmaraṇāntameva || 185 ||

phalaṃ kadambasya ca mākṣikāni tuṣodakena tridinaṃ sakṛd vā |
snānāvasāne niyamena pītvā rakṣāmavaśyaṃ kurute haṭhena || 186 ||

traihāyanaṃ vā guḍamatti nityaṃ palapramāṇaṃ vanitārddhamāsam |
jīvāntikaṃ niścitameva tasyā vandhyātvamuktaṃ kavipuṅgavena || 187 ||

karṣadvayaṃ rākṣasavṛkṣabījaṃ saptāhamātraṃ sitaśālidhānyam |
ṛtau nipītaṃ mṛgaśāvakākṣyā rakṣārthametanniyataṃ pradiṣṭam || 188 ||

iti strīṇāṃ puṣparakṣā |

atha durbhagākaraṇam 

jyeṣṭhānakṣatre nimbavandākaṃ yasyā aṅge dīyate sā durbhagā bhavati || 189 ||

iti durbhagākaraṇam |

dātṛśaktipradāyaka yantre 

atha kalahakaraṇam 

vibhītakaṃ tu śākhoṭamūlapatreṇa saṃyutam |
sthāpayedyadgṛhadvāre tasya vai kalaho bhavet || 190 ||

mārjāramūṣikadvijadigambarāṇāṃ lomabhirdhūpāt |
ārdrāyāṃ yatra gṛhe tatra vai jāyate vairam || 191 ||

viśākhā nakṣatre nimbavṛkṣasyottaramūlaṃ vivastro 
vimukhībhūya utpāṭya mukhena yasya ca ālaye 
kṣipettasya gṛhe pratyahaṃ kalaho bhavati ||

śākhoṭamūlaṃ patraṃ ca ekīkṛtya sthāpayet |
tathā dūrīkṛte tu tadgṛhe bhadraṃ bhavati |
tannakṣatre śākhoṭabadarībījadvayamekīkṛtya 
yasya gṛhe sthāpayettasya nityaṃ kalaho bhavati || 192 ||

brahmadaṇḍīṃ samūlāṃ ca kākamācīsamanvitām |
jātīpuṣparasaiḥ piṣṭvā saptarātraṃ punaḥ punaḥ || 193 ||

eṣa dhūpaḥ pradātavyaḥ śatrugotrasya madhyataḥ |
yathāgotraṃ samāghrāti pitā putraiḥ samaṃ kaliḥ || 194 ||

[iti śrīnityanāthaviracite kāmaratne ṣaṇḍhīkaraṇādi kalahakaraṇāntaṃ nāmāṣṭamopadeśaḥ] || 8 ||



[navamopadeśaḥ]


atha sarvāriṣṭavināśārthaṃ rakṣāvidhiḥ 

īśvareṇa purā devyai yadyattatkathitaṃ tathā |
kādidviravasānaṃ ca hyakṣaraṃ svarasaṃyutam || 1 ||

īkāreṇāpi saṃpūjya adho rephatrayānvitam |
oṃkāra śirasaṃ kṛtvā japtavyaṃ siddhimicchatā || 2 ||

svasaṃyamanamantro'yaṃ śatārddhaṃ japamātrataḥ |
aśeṣāriṣṭanāśaḥ syādityāha purasūdanaḥ || 3 ||

oṃ hrīṃ hrīṃ hrīṃ oṃ hrīṃ krīṃ khrīṃ vā 
oṃ krīṃ khrīṃ kṣīṃ oṃ ṭhrīṃ thrīṃ phrīṃ hrīṃ 

kaparaṃ caparaṃ caiva ṭhaparaṃ taparaṃ tathā |
paparaṃ varṇamāhṛtya ikāreṇa supūjitam |
adhorephasamāyuktamoṃkāraśirasaṃ tathā || 4 ||

mantra

khrūṃ chraṃ ṭhraṃ thraṃ phraṃ hram | vā 
oṃ khrīṃ chrīṃ ṭhrīṃ thrīṃ phrīṃ hrīṃ (śuddham)

śraddhayā tu mahāmantraṃ ye paṭhanti sadā mune |
sarvathā tasya puṃsaḥ syātsarvāriṣṭavināśanam || 5 ||

oṃ khrīṃ chrīṃ ṭhrīṃ thrīṃ phrīṃ hrīṃ (śuddham) |

hastena raktapuṣpeṇa grathitayā ca mālayā |
abhimantrya śatenāpi dadyāddevyai sadānaghe || 6 ||

yāvajjīvaṃ śubhaṃ tasya sarvalābho dinedine |
na gṛhe'niṣṭapātaḥ syāllikhitvā sthāpane'pi ca || 7 ||

akṣarāṇāmantyavarṇāṃllikhitvā pañcadhā'naghe 
adhorephasamāyuktamoṅkāraśirasaṃ tathā || 8 ||

hakāreṇa ca sampūjyamante phaḍakṣaraṃ smṛtam |
īkārena ca sampūjya ante phaḍakṣarāntim |
oṃ kṣīṃ kṣīṃ kṣīṃ kṣīṃ phaṭ ||

mantro'yaṃ mama rūpasya dhyāna jāpaṃ tathaiva ca |
mamaiva hṛdayaṃ tasya sadā tadgatamānasaḥ || 9 ||

oṃ kṣrīṃ kṣrīṃ kṣrīṃ kṣrīṃ kṣrīṃ phaṭ |

sadā syāttadgṛhe kṣemaṃ sahasrārddhasya jāpanāt |
trailokye tatsamo nāsti nityaṃ phalamavāpnuyāt || 10 ||

janmāntare sukhī prāṇī śṛṇu devi mahāphalam |
nityaṃ sampadyate rājñā patnyā putreṇa bāndhavaiḥ ||
jñātibhiḥ sajjanaiścāpi śatrubhiśca viśeṣataḥ || 11 ||

antadvayaṃ samāgṛhyamadhorephasamanvitam || 12 ||

oṃkārasaṃyutaṃ kṛtvā rekhābindusamāyutam || 13 ||

oṃ kṣau kṣauṃ |

anenaiva tu mantreṇa ye japanti mahājanāḥ |
te sarve kṣemamāyānti satataṃ tasya jāpanāt || 14 ||

śvetārkamūlaṃ puṣyārke samuddhṛtya vidhārayet |
bāhubhyāṃ vyādhayo na syustvariṣṭāni viśeṣataḥ || 15 ||

taddarśanena naśyanti ḍākinīdānavādayaḥ |
taddhūpena palāyante pretādyā dūrato dhruvam || 16 ||

pūrvābhādrapadā(-)ṛkṣe vandākaṃ tu śirīṣajam |
saṃgṛhya śirasi kṣipte hyabhayaṃ bhavati dhruvam || 17 ||

viṣṇukrāṃtābhavaṃ mūlaṃ hastasthaṃ caurabhītijit |
narasiṃhasya mantreṇa sakṛduccarite haret |
ḍākinīgrahabhūtāni tamaḥ sūryodaye yathā || 18 ||

oṃ hrīṃ hrīṃ hrīṃ hrūṃ hrūṃ hrūṃ phaṭ svāhā |

bhūtapretapiśācādibhaye smṛtvā naro'bhayaḥ |
bhairavīṃ tu mahāpūrvīṃ bhavedeva na saṃśayaḥ || 19 ||

iti sarvāriṣṭavināśārthaṃ rakṣāvidhiḥ |

atha nidrākaraṇam 

nigaḍe caurikāyāṃ ca paṭhed vāratrayaṃ manum |
sarve praharikā yānti nidrāyā vaśameva ca || 20 ||

mantraḥ 

oṃkālakālikā mahiṣāsuranāśinī yoganidrāṇī amukārapuriyādevī kālikāmālāyoganidrāṇī udayapānā || nandīkāla māyādharī nāganidrāṇī yā amukārapurīdaṇḍapaharī bhūpai loṭai kālikāra ājñā gaḍāgaḍī pīṭhe kālikāra ājñā || 

iti laukikamantraḥ | 

atha nigamoktam (śāstrokta) 

oṃ hrīṃ caṇḍācaṇḍa ugracaṇḍārikā kālikā nidraya nidraya | iti mantreṇa mahānidrā bhavati || etanmantraṃ paṭhitvā tasya gṛhe vā paṭhitvā dhūliṃkṛtvā kṣipet |

oṃ hrīṃ caṇḍācaṇḍa ugracaṇḍārikā kālikā nidraya nidraya |

kālikā vidyā kāla mohai devāsuranarake ho sthira lahai || kālikā tribhuvana jagata kālikāra dāsī pahari | jāgaṃtāli nidra gela mahi mandrabhe yaśī | kālikāra ājñā nidrālī lāge udaya deṣi āniṃdrabhāge ||

guvākaṃ khāditvā tasyāvaśiṣṭaṃ vivaraṃ kṛtvā saṃyojya tatra prasrāvayet |
tasmin vāṭikāyāmāyāti tasyānena mantreṇa nidrāṃ karoti || 21 ||

kākajaṃghājaṭā nidrāṃ janayecchirasi sthitā |
mūlaṃ vā kākamācyāśca kṛṣṇāyāstadguṇaṃ smṛtam || 22 ||

munikhaṇḍakaśākaṃ vā śayyāsthāne khanedatha |
karañjamūlaṃ śirasi baṃdhanātkurute tathā || 23 ||

nidrākaraṇamantraḥ 

oṃ śuddhe śuddhe mahāyogini mahānidre svāhā 

oṃ śuddhe śuddhe mahāyogini mahānidre svāhā |

nīlotpalaṃ samaricaṃ nāgakeśaramūlakam |
piṣṭvā tadrañjayeccakṣurnidrāmāpnotyasaṃśayam || 24 ||

iti nidrākaraṇam |

atha nidrānāśanam 

kūṣmāṇḍaṃ mahiṣīśṛṅgaṃ piṣṭvā tatsamabhāgakam |
lepayeddakṣiṇe pṛṣṭhe tasya nidrākṣayo bhavet || 25 ||

śobhāñjanasya bījāni nīlotpalasupuṣpakam |
samanāgeśvaraṃ piṣṭvā nidrāṃ muñcati cāñjanāt || 26 ||

bṛhatīpakvaphalakaṃ piṣṭvā ca madhuyaṣṭibhiḥ |
yasya netre'ñjanaṃ dadyānnidrā tasya vinaśyati || 27 ||

kanakadhattūramūlaṃ mṛtābhraketakīpuṣparajaḥ |
etāni piṣṭvā kapaṭaveṣena khādayennidrābhañjanaṃ bhavati || 28 ||

iti nidrānāśanam |

atha bandhanamocanam 

mārgaśīrṣasya pūrṇāyā śikhimūlaṃ samuddharet |
bandhanānmucyate tena śikhābaddho na saṃśayaḥ || 29 ||

bandhanamocanamantraḥ 

oṃ namaḥ kanakapiṅgale rudrahṛdayāṃśe raudrāsthidhāriṇi tiṣṭha tiṣṭha sara sara sarvānmohaya sarvānmohaya bhagavati śikhije timire mahāmāye svāhā ||

aṣṭottaraśataṃ japtvā śikhāyāṃ pūrvauṣadhaṃ bandhayettataḥ siddhiḥ |
plakṣāṃśena kakārāntaṃ likhed bandhanamocanam || 30 ||

oṃ namaḥ kanakapiṃgale rudrahṛdayāṃśe rudrāsthidhāriṇī tiṣṭha tiṣṭha sara sara sarvān mohaya mohaya bhagavati śikhije timire mahāmāye svāhā |

sukhanāmā piśācī amukaṃ hana hana paca paca śīghraṃ vaśamānaya svāhā |

atha nigaḍādibhañjanam 

hastārke senduvārasya mūlaṃ cottaragaṃ haret |
sparśanaṃ bandhavicchedaṃ kurute śīghra mārutaḥ || 31 ||

māṃsī raktotpalaṃ tulyaṃ kṛkalāse ca bhojayet | 
tanmalairguṭikāsparśāttālayantraṃ bhinatyalam || 32 ||

supakvamiṣṭikāṃ kṛṣṇavajrī grāhyastu yogibhiḥ |
sūkṣmacūrṇaṃ tu tāṃ kṛtvā lohakiṭṭamathāpi vā || 33 ||

sūtrai rajjuṃ dṛḍhīkṛtya tilatailena lepitam |
taccūrṇaṃ loḍitaṃ kṛtvā mahālohaṃ bhinattyalam || 34 ||

nigaḍabhañjanamaṃtrāḥ 

oṃ namo bhagavate rudrāya uḍḍāmareśvarāya bahurūpāya nānārūpadharāya hasa hasa nṛtya nṛtya tuda tuda nānākautukendrajāladarśakāya ṭhaḥ ṭhaḥ svāhā | anena sarvayogābhimantraṇāt siddhirbhavati || 35 ||

oṃ namo bhagavate rudrāya uḍḍāmareśvarāya bahurūpāya nānārūpadharāya hasa hasa nṛtya nṛtya tuda tuda nānākautukendrajāladarśakāya ṭhaḥ ṭhaḥ svāhā |

vāghavāhinī siṃhe yā kāli kāli katvāmikā āryādevī mantritoradāsaraṇe mantritoradāsaraṇe nāhithilaśato idavītribhuvanamātu causaṣṭhimi nibandhana bhāgi āpalā caṇḍācaṇḍacāmuṇḍīcāmuṇḍavikaṭakālikāmādaśana āge amukābandhanabhāṅgiyā āpalāṁ bāndhava bāndhava thāyāpāyeniścalacausaṣṭhībananavirakālī kāmāchāṃḍe huṃkāraḥ causaṣṭhībandhanakācāraṃbhāgībha(-)ilalyā ikhamakālikāra ājñā | athavā jā ājā devimaiciṃtovadāsarasevānanā(-)hivināsantombī devī tribhuvanaraṇaramāpācausṛṣṭibandhanabhāgī ambelā | caṇḍācaṇḍacā(-) muṇḍāvikaṭṭa(-) kālikāmādana āge amukārabandhabhāgī āṃphelāvabādhavāghapāyātraniyana causaṣṭhībandhana mailaviralākālikāmāchaḍe huṃkāra cauśaṭhibandhanakāṭhārabhādribharalakṣārasvārakālikāra ājñā | athavā oṃ agnimukhīpipācī amukaṃ hana hana paca paca śīghraṃ me vaśamānaya svāhā etanmatra dvayaṃ pūrvamaṣṭottaraśataṃ japtvā saptavāraṃ japte nānāvidha bandhanacchedo bhavati |

oṃ vāgha vāhini siddhe pākādi likā lāyi | ā(-)ā jā devī meṃ cinto vadā sarase bāna nāhiṃ vināśaṃ tombidevī tribhuvana raṇaramā pācau sṛṣṭi(-)bandhana bhāgī ābhpelā caṇḍā caṇḍa cāmuṇḍā vikaṭa kālikā mādana āge amukāra vana dhana bhāyī āphelā va vāgha vāgha pāyātrani pana causaṭhi bandhanamala viralā kāsikā māchaḍe hūṃkāra causaṭhi bandhana kāra ṭhāra bhādri bhāla kṣāra svāra kālikā ājñā || 

oṃ daṃ huṃ oṃ etanmaṃtraṃ paṭhitvā karāṅgulyā prahāre dvāri datte dvāraṃ muktaṃ bhavati |

āya āya ciñciṭhi ciṭhi hāṃlā vajranandikā kālikā || anena maṃtreṇa śvetasarṣapaṃ śvetodumbarapuṣpaṃ triḥpaṭhitvā prathamadvāre kṣipet sarvadvārāṇi bhañjanti || 36 ||

iti nigaḍabhañjanam |

atha gṛhakleśanivāraṇam 

takrapiṣṭena tālena lepayetputrikākṛtim |
tāmāghrāya gṛhādyānti makṣikā nātra saṃśayaḥ || 37 ||

śvetārkadugdhakulmāṣaṃ tilacūrṇasamanvitam |
arkapatreṣu vinyastaṃ mūṣakāntakaraṃ gṛhe || 38 ||

tālakaṃ chāgaviṇmūtraṃ palāṇḍuṃ saha peṣayet |
ālipya mūṣikaṃ tena jīvitaṃ ca visarjayet |
taṃ dṛṣṭvā ca gṛhaṃ tyaktvā palāyante hi kautukam || 39 ||

mārjārasya malaṃ tālaṃ piṣṭvā mūṣikamālipet |
tamāghrāya gṛhaṃ tyaktvā sadyo niryānti mūṣikāḥ || 40 ||

gandhakaṃ haritālaṃ ca brāhmītrikaṭusaṃyutam |
chāgalīmūtrataḥ piṣṭvā pūrvavanmūṣikaṃ lipet || 41 ||

maghāyāṃ bradhnakaṃ kṣetre sthāpayenmadhukodbhavam |
makṣikāmūṣakānāṃ ca jāyate tuṇḍabandhanam || 42 ||

mūṣakākarṣako dīpaḥ sāvarīguḍatailajaḥ || 43 ||

pūrve brahmā me baddhaḥ paścime viṣṇurme baddhaḥ uttare rudro me baddhaḥ dakṣiṇe yamo me baddhaḥ pātāle vāsukī me baddhaḥ phaṇisahasre baddhaḥ hrūṃ aṅguṣṭhāya namaḥ | karasampuṭaṃ kṛtvā tālatrayaṃ dadyāt | tato mūṣikamaśakanivāraṇaṃ bhavati || 44 ||

pūrve brahmā me baddhaḥ paścime viṣṇurme baddhaḥ uttare rudro me baddhaḥ dakṣiṇe yamo me baddhaḥ pātāle vāsukī me baddhaḥ phaṇisahasre baddhaḥ hrūṃ aṅguṣṭhāya namaḥ |

rohiṣatṛṇapuṣpaṃ tu vartimadhye niveśayet |
taddīpadarśanādeva kṣipraṃ naśyanti matkuṇāḥ || 45 ||

arkatūlamayīvarti bhāvayettāvakena ca |
dīpaṃ tatkaṭutailena niśśeṣā yānti matkuṇāḥ || 46 ||

arjjunasya phalaṃ puṣpaṃ lākṣā śrīvāsaguggulum |
śvetāparājitāmūlaṃ bhallātakaviḍaṅgakam || 47 ||

dhūpaṃ sarjarasopetaṃ pradeyaṃ gṛhamadhyataḥ |
sarpāśca matkuṇā mūṣā gandhādyāṃti diśo daśa || 48 ||

guḍaśrīvāsabhallātaviḍaṅgaṃ triphalāyutam |
lākṣāraso'rkapuṣpaṃ ca dhūpo vṛścikasarpahṛt || 49 ||

mustāsiddhārthabhallātakapikacchūphalaṃ guḍam |
cūrṇaṃ bhānuphalopetaṃ dahetsarjarasaiḥ samam || 50 ||

matkuṇā maśakāḥ sarpā mūṣikā viṣakīṭakāḥ || 
palāyante gṛhaṃ tyaktvā yathā yuddheṣu kātarāḥ || 51 ||

sarjjarasaḥ śakramedorjjunamūlamarubakaketakanakhabaddhaḥ |
etairdhūpo racitaḥ kīṭabhujagamaśakamakṣikādiharaḥ || 52 ||

rājavṛkṣaphalaṃ baddhaṃ khaṭvāyāṃ matkuṇāpaham |
lākṣāsarjjarasośīraṃ sarṣapaḥ patrakaṃ param || 53 ||

somarājasya vṛkṣasya pallavāgreṇa vartikām |
kṛtvā dīpaṃ prakurvīta matkuṇañca vinaśyati || 54 ||

bhallātakaviḍaṅgāni viśvakaṃ puṣkaraṃ tathā |
jambulo maśakaṃ hanti dhūpādvā gṛhamadhyataḥ || 55 ||

iti gṛhakleśanivāraṇam |

atha kṣetropadravanāśanam 

atha kṣetrasya śasyānāṃ sarvopadravanāśanam |
vālukāśvetasiddhārthān prakṣipet kṣetramadhyataḥ || 56 ||

śalabhāḥ sarpakīṭāśca varāhamṛgamūṣikāḥ |
maśakāstatra no yānti mantravidyāprabhāvataḥ || 57 ||

mantraḥ 

oṃ namaḥ surebhyaḥ balajaḥ japapari pari pari raja pari pari mili svāhā | oṃ surebhyo namaḥ || namaskṛtya imāṃ vidyāṃ prayojayet | 

vidyā prayojayāmīti vidyā me siddhyatu svāhā | akhilajambūkānāṃ mṛgāṇāṃ śaśakānāmanyeṣāṃ prāṇināṃ śalabhādīnāmanyeṣāṃ prāṇināṃ tuṇḍabandhanaṃ karomītyatra prāṇe kṛtaghnasya tena pāpena lipyate yatra mantraṃ vyatikramati svāhā ||

etanmantradvayena vālukābhiḥ saha śvetasarṣapānsaptavāramabhimantrya kṣetramadhye kṣipetsarvopadravo naśyati ||

mūṣakajambūkīṭānāṃ kurute tuṇḍavedhanam |

oṃ namaḥ surebhyaḥ balaja japa pari pari pari raja pari pari mili svāhā oṃ surebhyo namaḥ |

vidyāṃ prayojayāmīti vidyā me siddhyatu svāhā |

akhila jambūkānāṃ mṛgāṇāṃ śaśakānāmanyeṣāṃ prāṇināṃ śalabhādīnāmanyeṣāṃ prāṇināṃ tuṇḍabandhanaṃ karomītyatra prāṇe kṛtaghnasya tena pāpena lipyate yatra mantraṃ vyatikramati svāhā |

etanmantradvayena bālukābhiḥ saha śvetasarṣapān saptavāramabhimantrya kṣetramadhye kṣipet sarvopadravo naśyati | mūṣajambūkakīṭānāṃ kurute tuṇḍavedhanam |

vidyāmaṅkuśanāthasya mantraṃ vā bhairavasya ca ||

namonagaranāthāya hayaharaharaśila hayaharaharaśila sarveṣāṃ prāṇināṃ tuṇḍabandhanaṃ kuru kuru hruṃ phaṭ svāhā | ujjayanīnagarī bhairavabole mahādevabhaṇḍāraphūlabole hanumantasākṣī asti(-)astu | anena mantreṇa saptābhimaṃtritaṃ candanaṃ bāṭikāmadhye niḥkṣipet | puṣpaphalaṃ samastaṃ nirupadravaṃ bhavati || 58 ||

oṃ namo nagaranāthāya haya harahara śilaśila sarveṣāṃ prāṇināṃ tuṇḍabandhanaṃ kuru kuru hruṃ phaṭ svāhā |

ujjayinīnagarī bhairava bole mahādevabhaṇḍāra phūla bole hanumantasākṣī asti astu |

devadālīṃ ca siddhārthaṃ guṭikāṃ kārayed budhaḥ |
kṣetramadhye tu nikṣipya sarvapakṣibhayaṃ haret || 59 ||

pūrvāṣāḍhākhya(-)ṛkṣe tu vandāṃ vibhītavṛkṣajām |
śasyamadhye kṣipettena śasyavṛddhirbhaveddhruvam || 60 ||

iti kṣetropadravanāśanam |

atha gomahiṣyādidugdhavarddhanam 

oṃ huṃkāriṇīprasava oṃ śītalam anena saptavāraṃ tṛṇādikamabhimantrya bhoktuṃ dadyāt tadā bahulaṃ dugdhaṃ prasavati || 61 ||

oṃ huṃkāriṇīprasava oṃ śītalam |

iti gomahiṣyādidugdhavardhanam |

[iti śrīnityanāthaviracite kāmaratne ariṣṭanāśādiśasyopadravanāśanaṃ gomahiṣyādidugdhavardhanaṃ nāma navamopadeśaḥ] || 9 ||


[atha daśamopadeśaḥ] 

atha uccāṭanam 

maṅgalavāre rātrau śmaśānāṅgāraṃ kṛṣṇavastreṇa kṛtvā raktasūtreṇa saṃveṣṭya yasya gṛhopari kṣipetsaptāhābhyantare tasyoccāṭanaṃ bhavati || 

pañcāṅgulaṃ citrakasya kīlaṃ grāhyam punarvasau saptābhimantritaṃ gehe khanet | uccāṭanaṃ bhavet |

oṃ lohitamukhe svāhā asya aṣṭottarasahasrajapena puraścaraṇam || 1 ||

bharaṇyāmaṅgulaikaṃ tu ulūkasyāsthikīlakam |
saptābhimantritaṃ yasya khaneduccāṭanaṃ bhavet |


mantraḥ

oṃ dahadaha halahala svāhā || 2 ||

kākolūkasya pakṣāṃstu hutvā hyaṣṭottaraṃ śatam |
yannāmnā mantrayogena samastoccāṭanaṃ bhavet ||

oṃ namo bhagavate rudrāya daṃṣṭrākarālāya amukaṃ saputrabāndhavaiḥ saha hana hana daha daha paca paca śīghraṃ uccāṭaya uccāṭaya huṃ phaṭ svāhā ṭhaḥ ṭhaḥ || 3 ||

oṃ daha daha hala hala svāhā |

oṃ namo bhagavate rudrāya daṃṣṭrākālāya amukaṃ saputrabāndhavaiḥ saha hana hana daha daha paca paca śīghraṃ uccāṭaya uccāṭaya huṃ phaṭ svāhā ṭhaḥ ṭhaḥ || 2-3 ||

oṃ tāre tāre oṃ ture svāhā 

lepayetkākapittena kīlamaṅgulasambhavam |
nikhanedyasya bhavane tasyoccāṭanaṃ bhavet |

oṃ hrīṃ daṇḍin daṇḍin mahādaṇḍinnamo'stu te ṭhaḥ ṭhaḥ || 4 ||

oṃ hrīṃ daṇḍin daṇḍin mahādaṇḍin namo'stu te ṭhaḥ ṭhaḥ || 4 ||

narāsthikīlakaṃ dvāre nikhanyāccaturaṃgulam |
mantrayuktamarerdvāre satyamuccāṭanaṃ bhavet || 

oṃ namo bhagavate rudrāya amukaṃ gṛhṇa gṛhṇa paca paca trāsaya trāsaya troṭaya troṭaya nāśaya nāśaya paśupatirājñāpayati ṭhaḥ ṭhaḥ || 5 ||

oṃ namo bhagavate rudrāya amukaṃ gṛhṇa gṛhṇa paca paca trāsaya trāsaya troṭaya troṭaya nāśaya nāśaya paśupatirājñāpayati ṭhaḥ ṭhaḥ || 5 ||

mṛtakasya puruṣasya nirmālyaṃ cailameva ca |
pretālayāt samāgṛhya yasya gehe nidhāpayet ||

aṣṭamyāṃ ca caturddaśyāṃ tasyaivoccāṭanaṃ bhavet |
eṣa yogo mayā khyāto vinā mantreṇa siddhyati |
uddhṛte sati śāntirbhavati || 6 ||

śvetalāṅgalikāmūlaṃ sthāpayedyasya veśmani |
nikhanettu bhavettasya sadyaḥ uccāṭanaṃ dhruvam || 7 ||

siddhārthaṃ śivanirmālyaṃ yadgehe nikhaned budhaḥ |
uccāṭanaṃ bhavettasya hyuddhṛte tu punaḥ sukhī || 8 ||

saṃgṛhya vṛkṣātkākasya nilayaṃ pradahecca tam |
citāgnau bhasmataḥ śatrordattaṃ śirasi sundarī || 8 ||

tamuccāṭayate devi śṛṇu yogamanuttamam |
uccāṭanaṃ bhavettasya uddhṛte ca punaḥ sukhī || 9 ||

kīlamaudumbaraṃ śinyā mantritaṃ caturaṅgulam |
taṃ yasya nikhaned gehe tasyaivoccāṭanaṃ bhavet || 10 ||

oṃ śini śini svāhā |

atha uccāṭanaprakārāntaramāha 

uccāṭanavidhiṃ vakṣye yathoktaṃ śrīmatottare |
nimbapatre likhennāma mahiṣāśvapurīṣakaiḥ |
kākapakṣasya lekhanyā lekhanīyamanantaram |

mantrastu 

oṃ kākatuṇḍidhavalāmukhi devi amukamuccāṭaya amukamuccāṭaya huṃ phaṭ svāhā || 11 ||

etanmantraṃ mahādevi likhitvā pūrvavastubhiḥ 
nimbavṛkṣasthitaṃ sarvaṃ kākagehaṃ khanedatha |
śmaśānavahnimānīya dhatūrakāṣṭhadīpitam |
vahniṃ kṛtvā mahātailairathavā kaṭuvastubhiḥ |
pūrvoktamanunā tasya homayedvidhipūrvakam || 12 ||

oṃ kākatuṇḍidhavalāmukhi devi amukamuccāṭaya amukamuccāṭaya huṃ phaṭ svāhā |

dhavalāmukhīṃ ca saṃpūjya pañcopacārayogataḥ |
tasmādbhasma prakṣipecca śatrośca mandiropari |
uccāṭanaṃ bhavettasya saputrapaśubāndhavaiḥ || 13 ||

dhavalādhyānam 

dhūmravarṇāṃ mahādevīṃ trinetrāṃ śaśiśekharām |
jaṭājūṭasamāyuktāṃ vyāghracarmaparicchadām |
kṛśāṅgīmasthimālāṃ ca kartrikāṃ ca tathāmbujām || 14 ||

koṭārākṣīṃ bhīmadaṃṣṭrāṃ pātālasadṛśodarām |
svānte dhyātvā pūjayaidvai yogadhyānaparo janaḥ |
eṣa yogavidhiḥ khyāto vīratantre maheśvarī || 15 ||

ityuccāṭanam |

atha vidveṣaṇam 

kapiromahiṃgudārvīkharacarmāṇi cūrṇayet |
taccūrṇaṃ mantrasaṃjaptaṃ nāmakarmavidarbhitam |
trisahasraṃ punastena snigdhayorantarātmanoḥ |
dhūpairatīva vidviṣṭau snigdhāvapi bhaviṣyataḥ || 16 ||

tālapatre likhenmantraṃ nāmakarmavidarbhitam |
kṛtaprāṇapratiṣṭhāntaṃ prajaptaṃ trisahasrakam |
viṣāliptaṃ dvidhā kṛtvā nikhanetsindhurodhasi |
snigdhayorāśu vidveṣaḥ syādumeśānayorapi || 17 ||

ekahaste kākapakṣamulūkasya tathāpare |
mantrayitvā militvāgraṃ kṛṣṇasūtreṇa bandhayet || 18 ||

añjaliṃ ca jale caiva tarpayeddhastapakṣakaiḥ |
evaṃ saptadinaṃ kuryādaṣṭottaraśataṃ japet |
vidveṣo jāyate tatra mahākautukamadbhutam || 19 ||

oṃ devadatta yajñadattayostu svāha |

kākolūkīye yādṛśaṃ vairaṃ tādṛśaṃ vairaṃ devadatta yajñadattayorbhavatu svāhā | 

mārjāramūṣikāviṣṭhāsādhyaputtalikā kṛtā |
nīlavastreṇa saṃveṣṭya mantrayitvā śatena ca |
vidveṣo jāyate tatra bhrātarau tātaputrakau |

mantraḥ 

oṃ namo mahābhairavāya ca śmaśānavāsinyai amukāmukayorvidveṣaṃ kuru kuru krūṃ phaṭ || 20 ||

oṃ namo mahābhairavāya ca śmaśānavāsinyai amukāmukayorvidveṣaṃ kuru kuru krūṃ phaṭ 

oṃ namo mahābhairavāya ca śmaśānavāsinyai amukāmukayorvidveṣaṃ kuru kuru krūṃ phaṭ |

ekahaste ca kākapakṣamulūkasya tathāpare |
darbheṇa dhārayedyatnāt trisaptāhaṃ jalāñjalim |
raktāśvamārapuṣpaikaṃ mantrayuktaṃ jalāñjalim || 21 ||

nityaṃ nityaṃ pradātavyamaṣṭottarasahasrakam |
parasparaṃ bhaveddveṣaḥ siddhayoga udāhṛtaḥ || 22 ||

oṃ namaḥ kaṭīṭanī pramoṭanīkī gaurī gaurī amukasyāmukena saha kākolūkādivat kuru kuru svāhā |

iti vidveṣaṇam |

atha vyādhikaraṇam 

tannivāraṇaṃ ca

oṃ amukaṃ hana hana svāhā anena mantreṇa kaṭutailāktaṃ trikaṭuṃ juhuyāttadā śatrurbadhiro bhavati || 23 ||

bhallātakarasairguñjāṃ kuryādatisucūrṇitām |
kṣipedgātre bhavetkuṣṭhaṃ sitākṣīraṃ pibetsukhī || 24 ||

oṃ amukaṃ hana hana svāhā |

vānarīphalaromāṇi viṣaṃ bhallātacūrṇakam |
guñjāyutaṃ kṣipedgātre syāllūtā vedanānvitā ||

uśīraṃ candanaṃ caiva priyaṃgu raktacandanam |
tagaraṃ peṣayettoyairlepāllūtādināśanam || 25 ||

iti vyādhikaraṇam |

atha jvarānayanam 

oṃ cāmuṇḍe hana hana daha daha paca paca matha matha calha calha amukaṃ gṛhṇa gṛhṇa svāhā anena kaṭutailenāktanimbapatrāṇi yasya nāmnā hūyante tasya śīghraṃ jvaro bhavati | citrakapuṣpasahasraṃ hunet cāturthikajvaro bhavati | lavaṇamaṣṭādhikasahasraṃ hunet dāhajvaro bhavati || 26 ||

oṃ cāmuṇḍe hana hana daha daha paca paca amukaṃ gṛhṇa gṛhṇa svāhā anena mantreṇa nimbapatrakaṭutailaṃ sādhyanāma gṛhītvā juhuyāt sa jvareṇa gṛhyate | anena lavaṇāhutīraṣṭasahasraṃ juhuyāt sa śūlena gṛhyate || 27 ||

oṃ cāmuṇḍe hana hana daha daha paca paca amukaṃ gṛhṇa gṛhṇa svāhā |

tenaiva vetrapatramaṣṭasahasraṃ juhuyāt sa caturthajvareṇa gṛhyate |
raktapuṣpacitrakarasena yasya nāmābhilikhya bhūrje |
arkalatikāyāṃ sthāpayetsa dāhajvareṇa gṛhyate || 

mantraḥ

oṃ namaḥ śrīnṛsiṃhāya devāya danujāraye namaḥ kṛṣṇāya || 28 ||

snukpayolepanenaiva pānena śvetakuṣṭhakṛt |
tāmbūle indragopaṃ ca dattvāsau śvetakuṣṭhajit |
pītvā yatne yathāpūrvaṃ bhakṣedvā somarājikām |

oṃ namo bhagavate rudrāya uḍḍāmareśvarāya amukaṃ rogeṇa gṛhṇa gṛhṇa paca paca tāḍaya tāḍaya kledaya kledaya hūṃ phaṭ svāhā ṭhaḥ ṭhaḥ uktayogānāmayaṃ mantraḥ || 29 ||

oṃ namaḥ śrīnṛsiṃhāya devāya danujāraye namaḥ kṛṣṇāya |

oṃ namo bhagavate rudrāya uḍḍāmareśvarāya amukaṃ rogeṇa gṛhṇa gṛhṇa paca paca tāḍaya tāḍaya kledaya kledaya hūṃ phaṭ svāhā ṭhaḥ ṭhaḥ |

kṣipedṛkṣe mṛgaśire tintikāṣṭhasya kīlakam |
pañcāṅgulaṃ riporgehe vahnimāndyaṃ prajāyate || 30 ||

sāmudraṃ lavaṇaṃ vahni kevalaṃ vā samudrajam |
bandhakyā udaraṃ nyastaṃ sarvamaṃtaḥ puṭe pacet || 31 ||

iti jvarānayanam |

athākṣirogajananam

karavīrārdrakāṣṭhena tamādāya sucūrṇayet |
khāne pāne'rpayedyasya tasya cakṣuḥ praṇaśyati || 32 ||

dṛṣṭiṃ stambhayituṃ tasya maricākṣaphalaṃ vacā || 33 ||

karavīrapuṣpamaṣṭasahasramuktamantreṇa hunet |
akṣirogo bhavati || 34 ||

ullūkamastakaṃ grāhyaṃ lavaṇena prapūrayet |
mṛtpātrasthaṃ saptavastramakṣakāṣṭhena cālayet || 35 ||

oṃ namo bhagavate rudrāya uḍḍāmareśvarāya amukaṃ rogeṇa gṛhṇa gṛhṇa paca paca tāḍaya tāḍaya kledaya kledaya hūṃ phaṭ svāhā |

iti akṣirogajananam |

atha śatrubhrāmaṇam 

aśvatthakīlamaśvinyāṃ yasya gehe daśāṅgulam 
sthāpayeddirghayātrā syāttasyāpi na hi saṃśayaḥ || 36 ||

śṛgālasyāsthikīlaṃ ca sthāpyaṃ syāccaturaṅgulam |
riporgehe soma(-)ṛkṣe dīrghayātrā ca tasya vai || 37 ||

iti śatrubhrāmaṇam |

atha unmattīkaraṇam |

tālakaṃ dhūrtabījaṃ ca ghanacūrṇaṃ tu bhakṣayet |
dattvonmatto bhavecchatruḥ sitākṣīraiḥ punaḥ sukhī || 38 ||

tālakaṃ laśunaṃ mūrdhni kṣiptaṃ yasya piśācakṛt |
surāmāṃsasitākṣīraṃ bhakṣaṇācca sukhāvaham || 39 ||

madhvājyābhyāṃ svarṇamākṣīṃ piṣṭvā tatkṛtakajjalam |
dattaṃ yasyāñjanaṃ netre unmatto'sau prajāyate || 40 ||

goghṛtaṃ saindhavaṃ tulyaṃ varāhasya tu pittakam |
ajākṣīreṇa saṃyojyaṃ pānenonmattanāśanam || 41 ||

mayūrapārāvatakukkuṭānāṃ grāhya purīṣaṃ kanakaṃ ca tālam |
tanmūrdhni dattaṃ kurute piśācavannivarttate muṇḍitamastakena || 42 ||

guḍaṃ karañjabījaṃ ca ghanacūrṇaṃ samaṃsamas |
phalasyānte pradātavyamunmatto bhakṣaṇād bhavet || 43 ||

śarkarāśatapuṣpājyakṣīrapāne sukhāvaham ||

oṃ namaḥ unmattakāriṇī vidye ṭhaḥ ṭhaḥ | uktayogānāmayameva mantraḥ || 44 ||

oṃ namaḥ unmattakāriṇī vidye ṭhaḥ ṭhaḥ |

iti unmattīkaraṇam |

atha māraṇam 

narāsthikīlakaṃ puṣye gṛhṇīyāccaturaṅgulam |
nikhanedyasya gehe tu bhavettasya kulakṣayaḥ || 45 ||

oṃ hrūṃ hrīṃ phaṭ svāhā |

aśvāsthikīlamaśvinyāṃ nikhaneccaturaṅgulam |
śatrugehe nihantyāśu kuṭumbaṃ vairiṇāṃ kulam ||

huṃ huṃ phaṭ svāhā saptābhimantritaṃ śatrugehe nikhanet kulakṣayaṃ yāti || 46 ||

oṃ hrūṃ hrīṃ phaṭ svāhā |

oṃ huṃ huṃ phaṭ svāhā |

oṃ ḍaṃ ḍāṃ ḍiṃ ḍīṃ ḍuṃ ḍūṃ ḍeṃ ḍaiṃ ḍoṃ ḍauṃ ḍaṃ ḍaḥ amukaṃ gṛhṇa gṛhṇa huṃ huṃ ṭhaḥ ṭhaḥ anena narāsthikīlakaṃ sahasrābhimantritaṃ citāmadhye nikhanet sa jvareṇa naśyati ||

oṃ ṇaṃ ṇāṃ ṇiṃ ṇīṃ ṇuṃ ṇūṃ ṇeṃ ṇaiṃ ṇoṃ ṇauṃ ṇaṃ ṇaḥ ṭhaḥ ṭhaḥ 

oṃ sureśvarāya svāhā 

sarpāsthyaṅgulamekaṃ tu cāśleṣāyā riporgṛhe |
nikhanetsaptadhā japtaṃ mārayedripusantatim || 47 ||

oṃ sīṃ śoṣaṇe svāhā 

nimbaṣaḍbindukau grāhyau viṣaṃ tvagvānarīphale |
etaccūrṇaṃ pradātavyaṃ śatruśayyāsanādiṣu |
jāyante sphoṭakāstīvrā daśāhānmaraṇaṃ bhavet || 48 ||

oṃ sureśvarāya svāhā |

snānabhūmūtra bhūmṛtsnāṃ sarpavakre viniḥkṣipet || 49 ||

veṣṭayetkṛṣṇasūtreṇa mārgamadhye hyadhomukham ||
nikhanenmriyate śatrussamutthāne sukhī bhavet || 50 ||

vāmadantaṃ kulīrasya hyadhobhāgasya cāharet |
śarāgre phalakaṃ kuryāddhanuṣaścitrakendhanaiḥ || 51 ||

gavāṃ śirāguṇaṃ kṛtvā śatruṃ kuryācca mṛnmayam |
tadvajjātena bāṇena mriyate tatkṣaṇādripuḥ || 52 ||

ārdrāyāṃ nimbavandākaṃ śatroḥ śayanamandire |
nikhanenmriyate śatruruddhṛte ca punaḥ sukhī || 53 ||

tathā śirīṣavandākaṃ pūrvoktenoḍunā haret |
śatrorgehe sthāpayitvā ripornāśo bhaviṣyati || 54 ||

kṛṣṇavṛṣabharaktena gaṅgāmṛttikayā saha |
tilakaṃ bhāladeśe ca kṛtvā sambhāvayettu yam |
viddhaḥ syāttatkṣaṇādeva proñchite ca śubhaṃ bhavet || 55 ||

kṛṣṇachāgāśvapādasya khurasthaṃ romakaṃ haret || 56 ||

kṛṣṇakukkuṭakākasya grāhyaṃ pakṣacatuṣṭayam |
sarvaṃ dagdhvā tu bhāṇḍāntastadbhasma jalasaṃyutam || 57 ||

lalāṭe tilakaṃ kṛtvā vāmahastakaniṣṭhayā |
yaṃ śiro namyate tasya vedho bhavati niścitam || 58 ||

ūrṇanābhiśca ṣaḍbinduḥ samāṃśaṃ kṛṣṇavṛścikam |
yasyāṅge tatkṣipeccūrṇaṃ saptāhātsphoṭakaṃ bhavet |
mayūrapucchanīlābjaṃ piṣṭvā lepaiḥ sukhāvaham || 59 ||

ripuviṣṭhāṃ vṛścikaṃ ca khanitvā bhuvi niḥkṣipet || 60 ||

ācchādya prāvareṇātha tatpṛṣṭhe mṛttikāṃ kṣipet |
mriyate malarodhena uddhṛte ca punaḥ sukhī || 61 ||

oṃ hrīṃ kṣaḥ amukaṃ kṣam | anena mantreṇa rājikālavaṇaśivanirmālyāni kaṭutailena sahasrahomāt śatrorvadhaḥ || 62 ||

oṃ hrīṃ kṣaḥ amukaṃ kṣam |

iti māraṇam |

atha aśvanāśanam 

kṛṣṇajīrakacūrṇena añjitāśvo na paśyati |
takreṇa kṣālayeccakṣuḥ sustho bhavati ghoṭakaḥ || 63 ||

ghrāṇe chuchundarīcūrṇaṃ datte patati ghoṭakaḥ 
susthaścandanapānena nasyaṃ prāpya na saṃśayaḥ || 64 ||

aśvāsthikīlamaśvinyāṃ kuryātsaptāṃgulaṃ punaḥ |
nikhanedaśvaśālāyāṃ mārayatyeva ghoṭākān || 65 ||

bharaṇyāmuktamantreṇa citikāṣṭhasya kīlakam |
aṣṭāṃgulaṃ tu nikhanedaśvaśālā vinaśyati || 66 ||

mantraḥ 

oṃ namo bhagavate rudrāya oṃ aśvān hana hana svāhā | oṃ paca paca svāhā |

oṃ namo bhagavate rudrāya oṃ aśvān hana hana svāhā oṃ paca paca svāhā |

iti aśvanāśanam |

atha sasyanāśanam 

punarvasau cityakāṣṭhakīlakaṃ tryaṃgulaṃ kṣipet |
śatābhimantritaṃ kṣetre sasyaṃ tatra vinaśyati || 67 ||

oṃ lohitamukhe svāhā |

ārdrāyāṃ niḥkṣipetkīlaṃ bhallūkasyāsthisambhavam |
kṣetramadhye tadā śatroḥ śasyaṃ sarvaṃ vinaśyati || 68 ||

oṃ lohitamukhe svāhā 

viśākhāyāṃ kālikāṣṭhakīlamaṣṭāṃgulaṃ kṣipet |
kadalīvāṭikāmadhye nāśayetkadalī phalam || 69 ||

iti sasyanāśanam |

atha rajakasyavastranāśanam 

pūrvāphālguninakṣatre jātikāṣṭhasya kīlakam |
aṣṭāṃgulapramāṇaṃ tu nikhanedrajakasthale |
śatābhimantritaṃ tena tasya vastrāṇi nāśayet || 70 ||

oṃ kumbhaṃ svāhā |

iti rajakasyavastranāśanam |

atha dhīvarasya matsyanāśanam 

saṃgrāhya pūrvaphālgunyāṃ badarīkāṣṭhakīlakam |
aṣṭāṃgulaṃ ca nikhanennāśayeddhīvare gṛhe || 71 ||

oṃ jale svāhā || oṃ matsyikā svāhā || mantradvayasya tulyaṃ phalam |

saptāṃgulaṃ maghā(-)ṛkṣe bhallātaṃ kāṣṭhakīlakam || 72 ||

gṛhītvā dāśagehe tu deyaṃ matsyān vināśayet |
kṛttikāyāmarkakāṣṭhakīlakaṃ tryaṃgulaṃ kṣipet |
śatrorvāpīhradādau ca matsyastatra vinaśyati || 73 ||

iti dhīvarasya matsyanāśanam |

atha kumbhakārasya bhāṇḍanāśanam 

haste vai tryaṃgulaṃ kīlaṃ karavīrasya kāṣṭhajam |
nikhanetkumbhakārasya śālāyāṃ bhāṇḍanāśakṛt || 74 ||

pañcāṃgulaṃ nimbakīlaṃ tadṛkṣe pūrvavatphalam || 75 ||

gokṣuraṃ meṣaśṛṅgaṃ ca bījaṃ vā kokilākṣakam |
śūkarasya malaṃ vātha mūlaṃ vā śvetaguñjakam |
pākasthāne tu bhāṇḍānāṃ kṣiptaṃ sphoṭayate dhruvam || 76 ||

tālaṃ karañjabījaṃ ca ṭaṅkaṇena samanvitam |
kṛtvā bhāṇḍārasphuṭaṃtyeva muktānāṃ mantra ucyate || 77 ||

oṃ mada mada svāhā || oṃ guru hara svāhā athavā 

oṃ damanya damanya svāhā mantratrayasyatulyaṃ phalam ||

iti kumbhakārasya bhāṇḍanāśanam |

atha tailikasya tailanāśanam 

madhukasya tu kīlaṃ tu citrāyāṃ caturaṃgulam |
nikhanettailaśālāyāṃ tailaṃ tatra vinaśyati || 78 ||

oṃ daha daha svāhā sahasrajapaḥ ||

bhallātakāṣṭhaṃ citrāyāṃ nikhanettailike gṛhe |
aṣṭāṃgulaṃ tadā tatra grāhako nahi gacchati || 79 ||

iti tailikasya tailanāśanam |

atha gopānāṃ gavāṃkṣīranāśanam 

nikṣipedanurādhāyāṃ jambukāṣṭhasya kīlakam |
aṣṭāṃgulaṃ gopagehe godugdhaṃ ca vinaśyati || 80 ||

iti gopānāṃ gavāṃkṣīranāśanam |

atha tāmbūlaparṇanāśanam 

navāṃgulaṃ pūgakāṣṭhakīlakaṃ nikṣiped gṛhe |
tāmbūlikasya kṣetre vā ṛkṣe śatabhiṣāhvaye |
tadā tasya ca tāmbūlaṃ nāśamāyāti niścitam || 81 ||

iti tāmbūlaparṇanāśanam |

atha śākanāśanam 

gandhakaṃ cūrṇakaṃ tatra kṣipejjalayutena vai |
naśyanti sarvaśākāni śeṣāṇyalpabalāni ca || 82 ||

iti śākanāśanam |

atha tantuvāyasya sūtranāśanam 

aśvinyāṃ jāmbiraṃ kāṣṭhaṃ tantuvāyagṛhe kṣipet |
dvādaśāṃgulamānaṃ tu sūtraṃ tatra vinaśyati || 83 ||

iti tantuvāyasya sūtranāśanam |

atha śauṇḍikasya madirānāśanam 

kṛttikāyāmarkakāṣṭhaṃ ṣoḍaśāṃgulakaṃ kṣipet |
śauṇḍikasya ca gehe ca madirā tatra naśyati || 84 ||

iti śauṇḍikasya madirānāśanam |

atha karmakārasya lauhanāśanam 

rohiṇyāṃ badarīkāṣṭhaṃ kīlamekādaśāṃgulam |
karmakāragṛhe kṣiptaṃ lohaṃ taptaṃ bhavennahi || 85 ||

iti karmakārasya lauhanāśanam |

śarīravedhamocanamaṃtraḥ 

atra pārīśasambhramaṇakāya vedhacchedakajñānavijñāna nā phūṭai | amukārakāyaṃ hūṃkalikā(-) caṇḍītu(-)imāremaśilapātharapaḍe amukāragāme nārasasamārai putrīmārauṃ tāraka(-)ulaṭāvedhe virūpākṣarivānī ulaṭāvedhe pitra pānī je mora piḍe kare ghā(-)ulaṭāvedhe tākakatātujī khā phoṭaphokaṭaṇḍīvirūpākṣera ājñā vāratrayaṃ paṭhitvā pratiprātaḥ trigaṇḍūṣajalaṃ peyam yadi kenāpi viddhaṃ syāt śarīraṃ tadaiva tena kāryamiti || 86 ||

maṃtra 

amu pānīrā śambhukāya vedha chedaka jñānavijñāna nā phūṭai amukārakāyaṃ hūṃ kālikā caṃḍītu imoramā śila pātharapaḍe amukāra gāme nāra(-) sasamārai putrī mārauṃ tāraka ulaṭā vedhe virūpākṣa ribānī ulaṭāvedhe pitra pānī je mora piṇḍe kare | ghā(-)ulaṭā vedhe tāka tākatujī khā phoṭa phoṭa ḍaṇḍī virūpākṣera ājñā | 


[iti śrīnityanāthaviracite kāmaratne uccāṭanādi karmakāralauhanāśanaṃ nāma daśamopadeśaḥ] || 10 ||



[ekādaśopadeśaḥ] 

atha vividhakautukam 

śikhinastu śikhācūrṇaṃ bhojayeddinasaptakam |
tadviṣṭhāliptahastasya dravyaṃ lupyati tatkṣaṇāt || 1 ||

saptāhaṃ tilatailena bhāvayedātape khare |
aṅkolibījacūrṇaṃ tu yojyaṃ peṣyaṃ punaḥ punaḥ || 2 ||

tattailaṃ grāhayedyatnāttailakārasya yantrataḥ |
athavā kāṃsyapātre dve tena kalkena lepayet || 3 ||

utthāpya sthāpayedgharme sammukhantu parasparam |
tayoradhaḥ kāṃsyapātre patitaṃ tailamāharet || 4 ||

idamevāṅgulītailaṃ viddhaṃ sarvatra yojayet |
liptamaṅgulitailena maṇḍitaṃ tatkṣaṇāddiśet |
saphalo jāyate vṛkṣastatkṣaṇānnātra saṃśayaḥ || 5 ||

padminībījacūrṇaṃ tu bhavyamaṅgulitailataḥ |
nyastaṃ jale mahāścaryastatkṣaṇātpadmasambhavaḥ || 6 ||

yāni kāni ca bījāni jalajasthalajāni ca |
aṅgulītailaliptāni tāni tānyudbhavanti ca || 7 ||

yatkiñcitkāṇḍamūlotthaṃ patrapuṣpaphalādikam |
aṅgulītailaliptaṃ tu tulyarūpaṃ bhaved dhruvam || 8 ||

guñjāphalāmbupiṣṭaṃ ca lepayetpādukādvayam |
vinā kleśaṃ naro gacchet krośamekaṃ na saṃśayaḥ || 9 ||

laghu dārumayaṃ pīṭhaṃ guñjāpiṣṭena lepayet |
śuṣkamantarjalaiḥ sārddhamupaviṣṭaṃ na majjati || 10 ||

guñjābījaṃ tvaconmuktaṃ cūrṇaṃ bhāvyaṃ nṛmūtrakaiḥ |
saptavāraṃ tataḥ kāṣṭhaṃ liptamaṅgulisambhavam || 11 ||

tailamādāya talliptaṃ pūrvavatpādukāgatiḥ || 12 ||

vartissarjjarasaiḥ pūrṇā tailaliptā jale sthitā |
jvālitā dīpavartistu jvalatyeva na saṃśayaḥ || 13 ||

kaṭutumbyutthatailena pārāvatacaṭodbhavam |
malaṃ ca śikhimūlaṃ ca peṣitaṃ gardabhāsthinām || 14 ||

lalāṭe tilakaṃ tena kṛtvā saṃdṛśyate punaḥ | 
daśāsyo nātra sandeho yathā laṅkeśvaro nṛpaḥ || 15 ||

śigrubījotthitaṃ tailaṃ pārāvatapurīṣakam |
varāhasya vasāyuktaṃ śikhimūlaṃ samaṃ samam || 16 ||

lalāṭe tilakaṃ tena yaḥ karoti sa vai janaḥ |
dṛśyate pañcavakro'sau yathā sākṣānmaheśvaraḥ || 17 ||

rātrau kṛṣṇacaturdaśyāṃ mayūrāsye viniḥkṣipet |
bhṛṅgībījamadaḥ kṛṣṇāṃ kṛṣṇabhūmaunivāpayet || 18 ||

tajjātabhārṅgīṃ saṃgṛhya tayā kuryāttu rajjukam |
tadrajjubaddhaḥ puruṣo mayūro dṛśyate janaiḥ || 19 ||

tadyoge kṛṣṇamārjāravakre vairaṇḍabījakam |
tajjātairaṇḍabījānāmekavakreṇa dhārayet |
taṃ prapaśyanti mārjāraṃ manuṣyā nātra saṃśayaḥ || 20 ||

śṛgālaśvānameṣāṃśca yaddine vāpayetpṛthak |
mayūrāsye yathā bhārṅgī yāti siddhiśca tādṛśī || 21 ||

raktaguñjāphalaṃ vāpyaṃ strīkapāle'tha secayet |
jātaṃ phalaṃ kṣipedvakre strīrūpo dṛśyate pumān || 22 ||

narādisarvajantūnāṃ grāhyaṃ sadyohataṃ śiraḥ |
tacca kṛṣṇacaturdaśyāṃ sarvabījānvitaṃ vapet || 23 ||

bhṛṅgīdhattūrabījāni guñjānīvaikasaṃyutam |
nikhanetkṛṣṇabhūmyāṃ tu balipūjāsamanvitam || 24 ||

secayetphalaparyantaṃ yāvadvījāni cāharet |
tattadbīje kṛte vakre tattadrūpaṃ bhaved dhruvam || 25 ||

ityevaṃ kautukaṃ loke nānārūpasya darśanam |
muktabījo bhavetsvastho nātra kāryā vicāraṇā || 26 ||

haritālaṃ śilācūrṇamaṃgulītailabhāvitam |
talliptavastraṃ śirasi sthitaṃ paśyati vahnivat || 27 ||

tathaiva vāṅkolatailena sphuratyeva na saṃśayaḥ || 28 ||

sindūraṃ gandhakaṃ tālaṃ samaṃ piṣṭvā manaśśilām |
talliptavastradhṛk cāsau rātrau saṃdṛśyate'gnivat || 29 ||

dūre'pi sthitalokaiśca rātrau tu kautukaṃ mahat |
khadyotabhūlatācūrṇe lalāṭe tilake kṛte |
rātrau saṃdṛśyate jyotistasminsthāne tu kautukam || 30 ||

munipuṣparasaiḥ puṣpairghṛṣṭvā śvetāñjanaṃ tataḥ |
añjitākṣo naraḥ paśyenmadhyāhne tārakāmayam || 31 ||

vāpyaṃ vārttākubījaṃ ca nṛkapāle mṛdā saha || 32 ||

tajjātabījamūlaṃ vā mukhaṃ prakṣipya mānavaḥ |
śatayojanaparyantaṃ paśyetsarvaṃ yathāntikam || 33 ||

vāri makṣikayā sārdhaṃ tajjalaṃ yasya bhakṣaṇe |
dīyate niḥsarettasya hyadhovāyau tu kautukam || 34 ||

anena sarvayogānāmabhimantraṇe siddhiḥ aṣṭottaraśatajapenapuraścaraṇam || 35 ||

oṃ namo bhagavate rudrāya uḍḍāmareśvarāya bahurūpāya nānārūpadharāya hasa hasa nṛtya nṛtya tuda tuda nānākautukendrajāladarśakāya ṭhaḥ ṭhaḥ svāhā ||

iti vividhakautukam |

atha khaḍgastambhanam 

siddhi vastu sumati moharamācāndra suraja mohovarabhā(-)ī moho varaṃ āge kopa khāṇḍā phūṭai rakṣākara devī kālikā caṇḍī ā(-)ī cāndasuraja tuji malemuji phūṭai rāmera ājñā siddhi | anena vāratrayābhimantritam dhūlinā prokṣite gātre kṛpāṇadhārā rekhā bhavati nānyathā || 36 ||


[iti śrīnityanāthaviracite kāmaratne vividha kautukaṃnāmaikādaśopadeśaḥ] || 11 ||



[dvādaśopadeśaḥ] 

atha kāmyasiddhiḥ 

puṣyārke tu samāgṛhya mūlaṃ śvetārkasambhavam |
aṅguṣṭhapramitāṃ tasya pratimāṃ tu prapūjayet || 1 ||

gaṇanāthasvarūpāṃ ca bhaktyā raktāśvamārajaiḥ 
kusumaiścāpi gandhādyairhaviṣyāśī jitendriyaḥ || 2 ||

pūjayennāmamantraiśca tadvījāni namo'ntakaiḥ |
yānyānprārthayate kāmānmāsaikena tu labhyate || 3 ||

pratyekaṃ kāmyasiddhyarthaṃ māsamekaṃ prapūjayet || 4 ||

gaṇeśabījamāha | pañcāntakaṃ oṃ antarikṣāyasvāhā | anena pūjayet | pañcāntakaṃ gaṇeśaśaśidharaṃ bījaṃ gaṇapaterviduḥ | oṃ hrīṃ pūrvadayāṃ oṃ hrīṃ phaṭ svāhā | anena mantreṇa raktāśvamārapuṣpāṇi ghṛtakṣaudrayutāni juhuyāt vāñchitaṃ dadāti ||

oṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ mānasesiddhikari hrīṃ namaḥ | anena mantreṇa raktakusumamekaṃ japitvā nadyāṃ kṣipet | evaṃ lakṣaṃ japettato bhagavatī varadā aṣṭaguṇānāmekaṃ guṇaṃ dadāti |

iti kāmyasiddhiḥ |

atha vāk siddhiḥ 

kṛttikāyāṃ snuhīvṛkṣavandākaṃ dhārayetkare |
vākyasiddhirbhavettasya mahāścaryamidaṃ smṛtam || 5 ||

mantreṇa grāhayetsvātīnakṣatre badarībhavam |
vandākaṃ tatkare dhṛtvā yadvastu prārthyate naraiḥ || 6 ||

tatkṣaṇātprāpyate sarvaṃ mantramatraiva kathyate || 7 ||

oṃ antarikṣāya svāhā ||

iti vāk siddhiḥ |

atha guptadhanaguptaveśacaurādiprakāśanam 

dhanāni yatra vā santi ye vā caurādikāstathā |
guptaveṣā mahātmāno gandharvāṃ yakṣiṇīśvarāḥ || 8 ||

janturdhātuśca vṛkṣādyā martyaloke sthitā dhruvam |
prakāśaṃ jāyate sarvaṃ tacchṛṇuṣva samāhitā || 9 ||

vandā śākhoṭacūtasthā gokṣuraṃ lavaṇaṃ padam |
ajākṣīreṇa saṃpeṣya lalāṭe tilake kṛte |
guptā prakāśamāyānti tatkṣaṇānnātra saṃśayaḥ || 10 ||

āśleṣāyāṃ śanervāre sāyaṃ dāḍimabījakam |
rasaṃ saṃgṛhya tuvarīṃ kṛṣṇāṣṭamyāṃ tu bhūmije |
padmamūlaṃ maṅgale'hanyañjanaṃ kārayetsudhīḥ |
prakāśaṃ pūrvavatsarvaṃ jāyate nātra saṃśayaḥ || 11 ||

iti guptadhanaguptaveśacaurādiprakāśanam |

atha dhanurvidyā 

indreṇa purārjunaṃ prati yā vidyā kathitā sā saptaviṃśatyakṣarā(-) oṃ kālāyutā raktāghorā oṃkāra śataguṇa ādhāre ekādaśaśatasahasra indra ājñā | etanmantreṇa śaraṃ dhṛtvā navadhā paṭhitvā ākarṇapūritedhanuṣi śaraṃ melayet | sahasradhā bhavati | kalau daśadhā | mahādevena indraṃ prati yā kathitā sā saptadaśākṣarā | cāndradhanurguṇarekhā kāṇḍabrahmajñāna oṃ oṃ oṃ etanmantraṃ paṭhitvā pañcavāraṃ tadā kṣipetpūrvavadbhavati || 12 ||

kālāyutā raktāghorā oṃkāra śataguṇa ādhāre ekādaśaśata sahasra indra ājñā | 

cāndradhanurguṇarekhākāṇḍa brahmajñāna oṃ oṃ oṃ |

sarpaiḥ kavalitaṃ bhekamardhamātraṃ samuddharet |
chittvā sarpasya muṇḍaṃ ca ātape śoṣayetpṛthak || 13 ||

piṣṭvā pṛthagvaṭī kāryā lakṣyalābhapradā smṛtā |
lakṣye tu bhekatilakaṃ śarāgre sarpamuṇḍajam || 14 ||

dattvā tilakamākarṇaṃ guṇaṃ dhanuṣi vedhayet | 
lakṣyasya tilakaṃ bāṇo vindatyeva na saṃśayaḥ || 15 ||

oṃ rakte dhanurakte kāṇḍarakte halijā mā māro amukāra amuka(-)āṃga āmukaṭā(-)ī māro tridaśadevagaṇarudrāsākṣī amukāra māro devena rākhī arjuna kṛṣṇabhavānīra ājñā || etanmantraṃ paṭhitvā yasya yadaṅge mārayettadaṅgaṃ vidhyati | kintu prathamaparīkṣāyāṃ śanimaṅgalāhani mṛtasya brāhmaṇasya vaṃśamānīya dhanuḥ kāṇḍaṃ sajjīkṛtvā tatpramāṇaṃ guṇaṃ dattvā tatra tatsamaye vā puṣpahāramekaṃ dattvā muṣṭisthāne haṃsajīvamekaṃ bhañjayitvā ekanārikelajalena prakṣālya kāṇḍatrayeṇa lakṣyaṃ viddhvā sādhayet | yadā drutaṃ dhanuḥkāṇḍena lakṣyaṃ śatroraṅgasamīpe vedhayettadā vṛthā na syāt ||

oṃ rakte dhanurakte kāṇḍarakte halijā mā māro amukāra amuka(-)āṃga āmukaṭā(-)ī māroṃ tridaśadevagaṇarudrāsākṣī amukāra māro devena rākhī arjuna kṛṣṇabhavānīra ājñā ||

iti dhanurvidyā |

atha dhanadhānyākṣayakaraṇam 

ṛkṣe ca pūrvaphālgunyāṃ dāḍimīvṛkṣasambhavam |
vṛkṣādanī dhane deyamakṣaye bhavati dhruvam |
vandākaṃ tu maghā(-)ṛkṣaṃ bahuvārakavṛkṣajam |
dhānyāgāre pradātavyamakṣayaṃ bhavati dhruvam || 16 ||

śephālikāyāṃ vandākaṃ hastarkṣe ca samuddharet |
dhānyamadhye tu saṃsthāpyaṃ taddhānyamakṣayaṃ bhavet || 17 ||

bharaṇyāṃ kuśalavandākaṃ gṛhītvā sthāpayed budhaḥ |
sampūrṇaṃ dhanadhānyāntasthaḥ karotyakṣayaṃ dhruvam || 18 ||

udumbarasya vandākaṃ rohiṇyāṃ grāhayed budhaḥ |
sthāpayetsaṃcitārthaṃ tu sadā bhavati cākṣayam |
mantreṇa mantritaṃ kṛtvā maṃtropyatraiva kathyate |
oṃ namo dhanadāya svāhā || 19 ||

iti dhanadhānyākṣayakaraṇam |

atha śrutidharavidyādikaraṇam 

pathyā pāṭhā kaṇā śuṇṭhī saindhavaṃ maricaṃ vacā |
śigru pratipalaṃ cūrṇaṃ dvātriṃśatipalaṃ ghṛtam || 20 ||

ghṛtāccaturguṇaṃ kṣīraṃ dattvā sarvaṃ vipācayet |
ghṛtaśeṣaṃ samuttāryya lihed vāgbuddhidāyakam || 21 ||

atha brāhmīghṛtam 

vacā brāhmī phalaṃ kuṣṭhaṃ saindhavaṃ tilapuṣpikā |
cūrṇayitvā dravairbhāvyaṃ maṇḍūkībrāhmisambhavaiḥ || 22 ||

dinamekaṃ tataḥ pācyaṃ kalkāccaturguṇaṃ ghṛtam |
ghṛtāccaturguṇaṃ deyaṃ kṣīraṃ brāhmīniyojitam |
ghṛtaśeṣaṃ samuttārya lihedvāgbuddhidāyakam || 23 ||

iti brāhmīghṛtam |

dve haridre vacā kuṣṭhaṃ pippalī viśvabheṣajam |
ajājī cājamodā ca yaṣṭīmadhukasaṃyutam || 24 ||

etāni samabhāgāni śuṣkacūrṇāni kārayet |
taccūrṇaṃ sarpiṣā lehyaṃ karṣaikaṃ vākyaśuddhikṛt |
bhakṣayenmāsamekaṃ tu bṛhaspatisamo bhavet || 25 ||

brāhmī muṇḍī vacā śuṇṭhī pippalī samacūrṇakam |
madhunā bhakṣayetkarṣaṃ spaṣṭavāgjāyate dhruvam || 26 ||

vacāsthi kāravī gundrā muśalī madhukaṃ balā |
apāmārgasya pañcāṅgaṃ kṣaudreṇa pūrvavatphalam || 27 ||

apāmārgavacāśuṇṭhī viḍaṅgaṃ śaṅkhapuṣpikā |
śatāvarī guḍūcī ca samaṃ cūrṇaṃ harītakī |
ghṛtena bhakṣayetkarṣaṃ nityaṃ granthasahasradhṛk || 28 ||
aśvagandhājamodā ca pāṭhā kuṣṭhaṃ kaṭutrayam || 29 ||

śatapuṣpī viśvabījaṃ saindhavaṃ ca samaṃ samam |
etadarddhaṃ vacā caiva cūrṇitaṃ madhusarpiṣā || 30 ||

bhakṣayetkarṣamātraṃ tu jīrṇānte kṣīrabhojanam |
sahasragranthadhārī syānmūko'pi vākpatirbhavet || 31 ||

lihejjyotiṣmatītailaṃ balayā vacayā saha |
stokaṃ stokaṃ krameṇaiva yāvanniṣkacatuṣṭayam ||
nirvāte madhusevī syādbrahmacārī kavirbhavet || 32 ||

sūryasya grahaṇe vendoḥ samantrāmāharedvacām |
cūrṇitāṃ saghṛtāṃ bhuktvā saptāhe vākpatirbhavet || 33 ||

ityevamādi yogānāṃ mantrarājaḥ śivoditaḥ |
japtvāyutaṃ ca siddhiḥ syātpaścāttaireva bhakṣayet || 34 ||

oṃ hrūṃ hayaśīrṣavāgīśvarāya namaḥ |
dhātrīphalarasairbhāvyaṃ vacācūrṇaṃ dināvadhi |
ghṛtena lehayenniṣkaṃ vākśuddhismṛtibuddhikṛt || 35 ||

oṃ hrūṃ hayaśīrṣavāgīśvarāya namaḥ |

vacācūrṇaṃ kṣipetkṣīre punarmantreṇa mantritam |
bhojyaṃ kṣīreṇa śālyannaṃ saptāhe vākpatirbhavet || 36 ||

saptame aṣṭame caiva sākṣācchutidharo bhavet |
vacācūrṇaṃ pībetkṣīrairghṛtaiḥ kṣodraiśca yatpunaḥ |
saptāhakramayogena lehyaṃ syātpūrvavatphalam || 37 ||

puṣyārkayoge saṃgṛhya śvetārkasya tu mūlakam |
chāyāśuṣkaṃ ca taccūrṇaṃ mantreṇaivābhimaṃtritam || 38 ||

karṣamarddhaṃ palaṃ vāpi prātarutthāya saṃpibet |
oṃ maheśvarāya namaḥ anena mantreṇābhimantrya pibet ||
takreṇa sarpiṣā vāpi jīrṇānte kṣīrabhojanam || 39 ||

evaṃ saptāhamātreṇa kavirbhavati bālakaḥ || 40 ||

iti śrutidharavidyādikaraṇam |

atha kinnarīkaraṇam 

haridrā ca vacā kuṣṭhaṃ pippalī ca yavānikā |
maricaṃ saindhavaṃ śuṇṭhī caiṣāṃ cūrṇaṃ tu kārayet |
madhunā sahitaṃ cūrṇaṃ peṣayitvā śilātale || 41 ||

dinaiśca saptabhiścaiva bhakṣitavyaṃ nirantaram |
jāyate susvaraḥ puṃsāṃ kinnaraiḥ saha gīyate || 42 ||

vibhītakaṃ kaṇā śuṇṭhī saindhavaṃ tvaksamaṃ samam |
gomūtreṇa pibetkarṣaṃ kinnaraiḥ saha gīyate || 43 ||

jātīpatraṃ kaṇā lājā mātuluṅgadalaṃ madhu |
palaṃ lehyaṃ bhavennādaḥ kinnarādhika eva ca || 44 ||

devadārukaṇāvyoṣaṃ śatāhvā patrakaṃ niśā | 
vacā saindhavaśigrūtthaṃ mūlaṃ peṣyaṃ samaṃ samam || 45 ||

karṣaikaṃ madhusarpirbhyāṃ māsamātraṃ sadā lihet ||
kaṇṭhaśuddhirbhavettasya kinnaraiḥ saha gīyate || 46 ||

śuṇṭhī ca śarkarā caiva kṣaudreṇasaha saṃyutā |
kokilasvara eva syādguṭikābhuktimātrataḥ || 47 ||

ārdrakaṃ bhṛṅgakoraṇṭavāsā brāhmī vacā tathā |
vacā cūrṇaṃ samāṃśena palaikaṃ vāriṇā pibet || 48 ||

māsi māsi caturdaśyāṃ kṛṣṇapakṣe dvisaptakam |
gandharvasadṛśaṃ gānaṃ kokilānāṃ svaro yathā || 49 ||

nirguṇḍīmūlacūrṇaṃ tu tilatailena yo lihet | 
kaṇṭhaśuddhirbhavettasya kinnaraiḥ saha gīyate || 50 ||

bibhītakaṃ kaṇā śuṇṭhī saindhavaṃ tvak samaṃ samam |
gomūtreṇa pibet karṣa kinnaraiḥ saha gīyate || 51 ||

iti kinnarīkaraṇam |

atha cakṣuṣyanirmalīkaraṇam 

varṣākāle kākamācī samūlā tailapācitā |
khādetsamāsataścakṣurgṛdhradṛṣṭisamaṃ bhavet || 52 ||

śvetaṃ punarnavāmūlaṃ ghṛtaghṛṣṭaṃ sadāñjayet |
jalasrāvaṃ nihaṃtyāśu tanmūlaṃ tu niśāyutam ||
añjane cakṣurogāśca na bhavanti kadācana || 53 ||

dviniśā saindhavaṃ tryūṣaṃ bījaṃ kārañjakaṃ samam |
bhṛṅgīdravairyutaṃ vāpi timiraṃ paṭalaṃ haret || 54 ||

śambūkaṃ vā varāṭaṃ vā dagdhaṃ śuṣkaṃ vicūrṇitam |
añjayannavanītena hanti puṣpaṃ cirantanam || 55 ||

ajāmūtreṇa bhūdhātrībhūlaṃ piṣṭvā ca vartikā || 56 ||

navanītasamāyuktā hanti puṣpaṃ cirantanam |
añjanānnāśayetpuṣpaṃ kṣaudrairvā svarṇamākṣikam || 57 ||

maricairmardane rakte vartī rātryandhatāñjayet |
jayantī vābhayā vātha ghṛṣṭvā stanyairniśāndhahṛt |
śoṇitaṃ carmakopaṃ ca māṃsavṛddhiṃ ca nāśayet || 58 ||

kṛṣṇājasya ca māṃsāntaḥ pippalīṃ maricaṃ kṣipet || 59 ||

kārayitvā ghṛte pacyād ghaṭikānte tamuddharet |
madhyājyastanyasaṃpiṣṭaṃ rātryandhaharamañjanam || 60 ||

ajāpittagataṃ vyoṣaṃ dhūmasthāne viśoṣayet |
cirabilvarasairghṛṣṭaṃ rātryandhaharamañjanam || 61 ||

ghṛtena puṣpaṃ madhunāśrupātaṃ tailena kaṇḍūṃ timiraṃ jalena |
rātryandhakaṃ kāñjikayā nihanti punarnavā netrapunarnavaṅkarī || 62 ||

harītakī vacā kuṣṭhaṃ pippalī maricāni ca |
vibhītakasya majjā ca śaṃkhanābhirmanaśśilā || 63 ||

sarvametatsamaṃ kṛtvā gavyakṣīreṇa peṣayet |
nāśayettimiraṃ kaṇḍūṃ paṭalānyarbudāni ca || 64 ||

api dvivārṣikaṃ puṣpaṃ māsaikenaiva nāśayet |
adhikāni ca māṃsāni yaśca rātrau na paśyati || 65 ||

vartiścandrodayā nāma nṛṇāṃ dṛṣṭiprasādinī |
chāyāśuṣkā vaṭī kāryā nāmnā candrodayā vaṭī || 66 ||

yastraiphalaṃ cūrṇamapathyavarjyaṃ sāyaṃ samaśnāti havirmadhubhyām |
samyucyate netragatairvikārairbhṛtyairyathā kṣīṇadhano manuṣyaḥ || 67 ||

iti cakṣuṣyanirmalīkaraṇam |

atha karṇasya badhiratvanāśanam 

śikharikṣārajalena tatkṛtakalkena sādhitaṃ tilajam |
apaharati karṇanādaṃ bādhiryaṃ cāpi pūraṇāt || 68 ||

daśamūlakaṣāyeṇa tailaprasthaṃ vipācayet |
etatkalkaṃ pradāyaiva bādhirye paramauṣadham || 69 ||

nīlībradhnarasastailaṃ siddhakāñjikasaṃyutam |
kaduṣṇapūraṇātkarṇe niśśeṣakṛmināśanaḥ || 70 ||

dantena carvayenmūlaṃ nandyāvarttapalāśakam |
tannālīpūrite karṇe dhruvaṃ gomakṣikāñjayet || 71 ||

tāmbūlabhakṣaṇaṃ kṛtvā tatra sandāpayed budhaḥ |
tatra sthitāstu kṛmayo nāśamāyānti niścitam || 72 ||

mūṣalībākucīcūrṇaṃ khāded bādhiryaśāntaye |
manaḥśilāpāmārgo'tha cūrṇaṃ madhuplutam |
bhakṣayetkarṣamātraṃ tu badhiratvapraśāntaye || 73 ||

laśunāmalakaṃ tālaṃ piṣṭvā taile caturguṇe || 74 ||

tailāccaturguṇaṃ kṣīraṃ pācyaṃ tailāvaśeṣitam |
tattailaṃ nikṣipetkarṇe bādhiryaṃ ca vināśayet || 75 ||

iti karṇasyabadhiratvanāśanam |

atha karṇapālīvarddhanam 

siddhārthaṃ bṛhatī caiva hyapāmārgaṃ samaṃ samam |
chāgī kṣīraiḥ pralepo'yaṃ karṇapālī vivarddhayet || 76 ||

mūṣalīkandacūrṇaṃ ca mahiṣīkṣīrasaṃyutam |
loḍayetsnigdhabhāṇḍe tu dhānyarāśau niveśayet |
saptāhādutthite lepyaṃ karṇapālī vivarddhate || 77 ||

guñjāmūlakṛtaṃ cūrṇaṃ mahiṣīkṣīrasaṃyutam || 78 ||

śṛtaṃ dadhi tataḥ kuryānnavanītaṃ tadudbhavam |
karṇayorlepayennityaṃ varddhayennātra saṃśayaḥ || 79 ||

aśvagandhā vacā kuṣṭhaṃ gajapippalikā samam |
mahiṣīnavanītena lepātkarṇo vivarddhate || 80 ||

varāhotthena tailena lepaḥ karṇavivardhanaḥ |
carmacaṭakasya raktena lepātkarṇo vivarddhate || 81 ||

iti karṇapālīvardhanam |

atha dantadṛḍhīkaraṇam 

yamaciñcājayāpuṅkhāmūlaṃ vā hayamārajam |
caladantā dṛḍhāyante pratyahaṃ dantadhāvanāt || 82 ||

tāmrapātre kṣaṇaṃ pācyamabhayā cūrṇakaṃ madhu |
piṣṭvā ca guṭikā kāryā dantairdhāryā kṛmīn haret || 83 ||

dantairdhāryaṃ snuhīmūlaṃ kṛmināśaṃ karotyalam |
kāśīśaṃ ghṛtasaṃpakvaṃ dhāryaṃ dante vyathāpaham || 84 ||

viśālayoḥ palaṃ cūrṇaṃ taptalohe parikṣipet |
taddhūmaspṛṣṭadantānāṃ kīṭapāto bhavantyalam || 85 ||

jātikorakapatraṃ ca carvayet prātarutthitaḥ |
sthirāḥ syuścalitā dantāstatkāṣṭhairdantadhāvanāt || 86 ||

guṃjāmūlaṃ tu karṇābhyāṃ baddhaṃ dantakṛmipraṇut |
trisūtaṃ raupyamekaṃ tu jambīrarasamarditam |
jambīraphalamadhyasthaṃ vastrairbaddhvātryahaṃ pacet || 87 ||

kṣīramadhye samuddhṛtya guṭikāṃ tāṃ tataḥ punaḥ |
bhāvitaṃ bhānudugdhena tālakaṃ sūkṣmapeṣitam || 88 ||

tanmadhye guṭikāṃ kṣiptvā vastre baddhvā dinatrayam |
madhubhāṇḍagataṃ paścāduddhṛtā cāsyadhāritā |
gharṣaṇāccalitān dantāṃstatkṣaṇātkurute dṛḍhān || 89 ||

tālakaṃ bhānudugdhena dinamekaṃ vimardayet |
tadgarbharasahomotthāṃ piḍikāṃ tārasaṃyutām || 90 ||

jambīraphalamadhyasthāṃ dolāyantre tryahaṃ pacet || 91 ||

tailakṣaudrayute bhāṇḍe samuddhṛtya vidhārayet |
dantarogān haretsarvān gharṣaṇāccalitā dṛḍhāḥ || 92 ||

caladdantasthirakaraṃ kāryaṃ bakulacarvaṇam |
bakulasya tu bījaṃ tu piṣṭvā koṣṇena vāriṇā |
mukhe ca dhārayeddhīmān dantadārḍhyaṃ karaṃ param || 93 ||

bakulasya tvacā kvāthamuṣṇaṃ vakreṇa dhārayet |
dṛḍhāḥ syuścalitā dantāḥ saptāhānnātra saṃśayaḥ || 94 ||

iti dantadṛḍhīkaraṇam |

atha gaṇḍamālānivāraṇam 

karkandhukolāmalakapramāṇaiḥ kakṣāṃsamanyāgalavaṃkṣaṇeṣu |
medaḥkaphābhyāṃ ciramandapākaiḥ syādgaṇḍamālā bahubhiśca gaṇḍaiḥ ||

kāñcanāratvacaḥ kvāthaḥ śuṇṭhīcūrṇena saṃyutaḥ |
mākṣikāḍhyaḥ sakṛtpītaḥ kvātho varuṇamūlakaḥ |
gaṇḍamālāṃ haratyāśu cirakālānubandhinīm ||

iti gāṇḍamālānivāraṇam |

atha āhārakaraṇam 

bradhnakenāpi vṛkṣasya pīṭhaṃ kṛtvā śanaiḥ sthitaḥ |
yo'sau bhuṅkte ghṛtaiḥ sārddhaṃ bhojanaṃ bhīmasenavat || 95 ||

saṃdhyāyāmāmravṛkṣasya karttavyamabhimaṃtritam |
prātaḥ puṣpāṇi saṃgṛhya mālāṃ śirasi dhārayet ||
kaupīnaṃ saṃparityajya bhuṅkte'sau bhīmasenavat || 96 ||

udbhrāṃtapatramādāya kapilā śvānadantakam |
kaṭyāmeva dvayaṃ baddhvā bhuṅkte'sau bhīmasenavat || 97 ||

gṛhītvā mantritān mantrī vibhītatarupallavān |
ākramya dakṣiṇāṃ jaṅghāṃ viṃśatyāhārabhugbhavet || 98 ||

oṃ namaḥ sarvabhūtādhipataye grasa grasa śoṣaya śoṣaya bhairavī ājñāpayati svāhā | uktayogānāmayaṃ mantraḥ |

adharaṃ kṛkalāsasya śikhāsthāne vibandhayet |
vāyuputra ivāścaryamasau bhuṅkte na saṃśayaḥ || 99 ||

oṃ nābhivegena urvaśī svāhā |

iti āhārakaraṇam |

athānāhārakaraṇam 

antrāṇi kṛkalāsasya majjāṃ kārañjabījakam |
piṣṭvā tadguṭikāṃ kuryāt trilohena tu veṣṭitām || 100 ||

tāṃ vakre dhārayedyastaṃ kṣutpipāsā na bādhate |
oṃ sāṃsāṃśarīramamṛtamākarṣaya svāhā |
padmabījamahāśālī chāgīdugdhena pācayet |
sājyaṃ ca pāyasaṃ bhuktvā dvādaśāhaṃ kṣudhāpaham || 101 ||

oṃ sāṃ sāṃ śarīramamṛtmākarṣaya svāhā |

udumbarasya jambīraśāliśimbīśirīṣajam |
bījaṃ saṃcūrṇya cājyena bhuktvā pakṣaṃ kṣudhāpaham || 102 ||

udumbaraphalaṃ pakvamiṅgudītailabhāvitam |
bhuktvā māsaṃ kṣudhāṃ hanti pipāsāṃ ca na saṃśayaḥ || 103 ||

apāmārgasya bījāni tvagvarjyāni prapācayet |
pāyasaṃ chāgalīkṣīrairbhuktaṃ māsakṣudhāpaham || 104 ||

oṃ namo bhagavate rudrāya amṛtārkamadhye saṃsthitāya mama śarīre amṛtaṃ kuru kuru saḥ svāhā uktayogānāmayaṃ mantraḥ || 104-105 ||

ityānāhārakaraṇam |

atha pādukāsādhanam 

aśvanālāṃgulītailaiḥ peṣayecchvetasarṣapam |
talliptapādahastastu yojanānāṃ śataṃ vrajet || 106 ||

aṅkolasya tu mūlaṃ tu tilatailena pācayet |
pādau tu jānuparyantaṃ liptvā dūrādhvago bhavet || 107 ||

oṃ hrīṃ namaḥ oṃ namaścaṇḍikāyai gaganaṃ gaganaṃ cālaya cālaya veśaya hili hili vegavāhinī hrīṃ hrīṃ svāhā | uktayogadvayasyāpameva mantraḥ ||

kākasya hṛdayaṃ netre jihvā caiva manaśśilām |
gairikaṃ sindhujaṃ caiva ajāmārī ca mālatī || 108 ||

samaṃ rudrajaṭā caiva vidāryā saha peṣayet |
talliptapādaḥ sahasā sahasraṃ yojanaṃ vrajet || 109 ||

valīpalitanirmukta yāvadābhūtasaṃplavam || 110 ||

kākajihvā brahmacārī guḍalohena veṣṭayet |
mukhe prakṣipya gacchanti yojanaṃ śatameva ca || 111 ||

āgacchanti tadā tūrṇaṃ te narā nātra saṃśayaḥ || 112 ||

oṃ namo bhagavate rudrāya namo haritagadādharāya trāsaya trāsaya kṣobhaya kṣobhaya cālane cālane svāhā |

aṅkolatailasaṃpiṣṭāṃ śvetasarṣapalepitām |
pādukāmuṣṭracarmotthāṃ samāruhya śataṃ vrajet || 113 ||

iti pādukāsādhanam |

anāvṛṣṭinivāraṇam 

hrūṃ hrīṃ (athavā) huṃ śrīṃ huṃ | imaṃ mantraṃ jala madhye praviśya yadi japet tadā anāvṛṣṭi haret | mahāvṛṣṭirbhavati ||

[iti śrīnityanāthaviracite kāmyasiddhayādi anāvṛṣṭinivāraṇaṃ nāma dvādaśopadeśaḥ] || 12 ||


[trayodaśopadeśaḥ] 

atha nidhidarśakamañjanam 

añjanānāṃ tu sarveṣāṃ mantraṃ sādhyamaghorakam |
vinā mantreṇa vidyāśca nāśayanti pade pade || 1 ||

ambikāyantramāśritya japedaṣṭasahasrakam |
tataḥ sarvavidhānāni susādhyāni ca prārabhet || 2 ||

oṃ siddho liṃ namaḥ oṃ śaṃ śo liṃ nmaḥ oṃ kaṃ ko liṃ namaḥ oṃ kaṃ ko liṃ namaḥ oṃ khida khida vinda vinda jolīnamahāna mada mada mahādharaṇavarṇane namaḥ sarvasukhadārāya dharaṇa maḍa to romā dharaṇa oṃ hrīṃ ambike oṃ hrīṃ ambike namaḥ |

oṃ ambe ambāle ambike avatāraya ṭhaḥ ṭhaḥ svāhā |

hrūṃ hrūṃ hrīṃ kṣauṃ yūṃ klīṃ |

oṃ bahurūpaṃ viśvarūpaṃ vidyādharamaheśvaram |
japāmyahaṃ mahādevaṃ sarvasiddhipradāyakam || 3 ||

oṃ bahurūpaṃ viśvarūpaṃ vidyādhara maheśvara |

oṃ namo rudrāya rudrarūpāya namo bahurūpāya namo viśvarūpāya namo viśvātmane namaḥ tatpuruṣayakṣāya namo yakṣarūpāya namaḥ ekasmai namaḥ ekāya namaḥ eka rauravāya namaḥ ekayakṣāya namaḥ ekekṣaṇāya namo yakṣāya namo varadāya namaḥ tuda tuda svāhā |

kajjalānāṃ pātanārthe grāhyo yatnena pāvakaḥ |
dīkṣitasya gṛhe śreṣṭhaṃ citāyāṃ tu viśeṣataḥ |
rajakasya gṛhadvāpi taskarasya gṛhācca yaḥ || 4 ||

oṃ jvalitavidyuddhūmāya svāhā || ayamagnigrahaṇamantraḥ | vā jvalitavidyute svāhā oṃ namo bhagavate vāsudevāya bandha bandha śrīpataye svāhā | anena mantreṇa vartimabhimantrayet || oṃ namo bhagavate siddhasādhakāya jvala jvala paca paca pātaya pātaya bandhaya bandhaya saṃhara saṃhara darśaya darśaya nidhi namaḥ | anena dīpaṃ jvālayet | oṃ aiṃ mantraḥ sarvasiddhebhyo namo viśvebhyaḥ svāhā || anena kajjalaṃ grāhyam | oṃ kāli kāli mahākāli rakṣedamañjanaṃ namo viśvebhyaḥ svāhā | anena mantreṇa yatkiñcidañjanadravyamabhimanyatrayet || oṃ sarve sarvahite klīṃ sarve sarvahite sarvauṣadhi prayāhi te || virate namo namaḥ svāhā || anena mantreṇāñjanayogyāṃ mūlikāmabhimantrayet | ādau kevalahemaśalākayā netramañjayitvā tatastayaiva śalākayā añjanadravyamañjayet || añjayitvāñjanaṃ paścātsaptadhārasya patrakam | bandhayet pratinetraṃ tu acchidraṃ tadadhomukham || 5 ||

oṃ jvalitavidyuddhūmāyā svāhā 

oṃ namo bhagavate vāsudevāya dhara dhara bandha bandha śrīpataye svāhā 

oṃ namo bhagavate siddhasādhakāya jvala jvala paca paca pātaya pātaya bandha bandha saṃhara saṃhara darśaya darśaya nidhiṃ namaḥ 

oṃ aiṃ mantraḥ sarvasiddhebhyo namo viśvebhyaḥ svāhā 

oṃ kāli kāli mahākāli rakṣeda mañjanaṃ namo viśvebhyaḥ svāhā 

oṃ sarve sarvahite klīṃ sarve sarvahite sarvauṣadhi priyāhi te virate namo namaḥ svāhā 

tasyopari sitaṃ vastraṃ paṭṭajaṃ cāpi bandhayet |
nāñjyādadhikahīnāṅgaṃ śvadaṣṭaṃ cāgnidagdhakam || 6 ||

sampūrṇāṅgaṃ śuci snātvā dvidinaṃ naktabhojanam |
bhoktavyaṃ kṣīraśālyannaṃ tridinānte tato'ñjayet || 7 ||

añjitasya śikhābandhaṃ karttavyaṃ mantra ucyate || 8 ||

oṃ namo bhagavate rudrāya tula tula maheśvara māheśvara nujvala nujvala vijvala vijvala mijvala mijvala hara hara yakṣarakṣapūjite yakṣakumāri sulocane svāhā || oṃ namo bhagavate rudrāya oṃ nanna nanna mahennavihenna vihennamihenna vihennamihenna harahara rakṣa harahara rakṣa pūjite yakṣakumārisulocane svāhā |

yakṣāṇāṃ mūrtimāśritya udayāstaṃ manuṃ japet |
pūrvameva samākhyātaṃ śikhābandhaṃ śivoditam || 9 ||

idaṃ sarvāñjane jñātavyam |

oṃ namo bhagavate rudrāya tula tula maheśvara māheśvara nujvala nujvala vijvala vijvala mijvala mijvala hara hara yakṣarakṣapūjite yakṣakumāri sulocane svāhā |

oṃ namo bhagavate rudrāya oṃ nanna nanna mahennavihenna vihennamihenna vihennamihenna harahara rakṣarakṣa pūjite yakṣakumārisulocane svāhā |

śaratkāle tu saṃgrāhyā bhūtalā raktavarṇakā |
sindūrapūritāṃ kṛtvā varti tūlena veṣṭayet || 10 ||

atikṛṣṇatilātailaṃ grāhayedrakṣayetsudhīḥ |
tailavartyoḥ prayogeṇa kajjalaṃ cottarāyaṇe || 11 ||

grāhayitvāñjanaṃ cakṣurnidhiṃ paśyati sādhakaḥ |
pramāṇaṃ ca vijānāti gṛhṇāti ca yathepsitam || 12 ||

atikṛṣṇasya kākasya jihvāmāṃsaṃ samāharet |
veṣṭayadravitūlena vati tenaiva kārayet || 13 ||

ajāghṛtena dīpaṃ tu prajvālyādāya kajjalam |
añjitākṣo narastena nidhiṃ paśyati pūrvavat || 14 ||

saptadhā padmasūtrāṇi bhāvayedikṣujai rasaiḥ |
uddhṛtya jvālayeddīpamaṅgulītailasaṃyutam || 15 ||

grāhyaṃ kṛṣṇatrayodaśyāṃ kajjalaṃ nidhidarśakam |
sarvāñjanamidaṃ siddhaṃ śambhunā parikīrtitam || 16 ||

dīpakajjalayoḥ pātraṃ karttavyaṃ naramuṇḍajam |
sarveṣāṃ kajjalānāṃ tu satyaṃ syācchivabhāṣitam || 17 ||

raktena kṛkalāsasya bhāvayitvā manaśiḥlām |
tenaivāñjitanetrastu nidhiṃ paśyati pūrvavat || 18 ||

gṛhītvā cānurādhāyāṃ vandāṃ śākhoṭavṛkṣajām |
gorocanasamaṃ piṣṭvā tvañjanaṃ nidhidarśakam |
etatsarvāñjanaṃ khyātaṃ prasiddhaṃ śivabhāṣitam || 19 ||

agastyavṛkṣajāṃ kuryātpādukāṃ nidhidarśakām |
pādukāñjanayogena siddhayogā bhavanti vai || 

oṃ namo bhagavate rudrāya uḍḍāmareśvarāya śili śili dhūmare nāgavetālinī svāhā || anena pādukāmabhimaṃtrayet || 20 ||

tulasīmūlikāṃ puṣye śanivāre samuddharet |
niṣpiṣya kāñjikenātha madhunā punarañjayet || 21 ||

pādajātaṃ kumāraṃ vā kanyakāṃ vā tadā nidhiḥ |
dṛśyate nātra saṃdehaḥ pātālaṃ gatavānapi || 22 ||

khanyamāne ca sarpāśca nissaranti pade pade || 23 ||

auṣadhena vinā tebhyo bhayaṃ syānmantriṇāmapi |
tasmādauṣadhayogena pādalepena tāñjayet || 24 ||

oṃ namo bhagavate rudrāya kajjalalepāñjanaṃ darśaya darśaya svāhā ṭhaḥ ṭhaḥ |

arkasya karavīrasya panasasya tu mūlikām |
piṣṭvā pādapralepācca dūre gacchanti pannagāḥ || 25 ||

iti nidhidarśakamañjanam |

atha adṛśyakaraṇam 

caturlakṣamimaṃ mantraṃ śmaśāne prajapecchuciḥ |
nagnavṛttistatastuṣṭā paṭaṃ yacchati yakṣiṇī || 26 ||

tenāvṛto naro'dṛśyo vicaredvasudhātale |
nidhiṃ paśyati gṛhṇāti na vighnaiḥ paribhūyate || 27 ||

oṃ hrāṃ hrīṃ spheṃ śmaśānavāsinī svāhā |
niśācarīṃ niśi dhyātvā japtvā vāmena pāṇinā |
adṛśyakariṇīṃ vidyāṃ lakṣajāpye prayacchati || 28 ||

oṃ hrāṃ hrīṃ spheṃ śmaśānavāsinī svāhā |

rātrau kṛṣṇacaturdaśyāṃ śmaśānāntaḥ śivālaye |
balinā copahāreṇa kuryādarcanamuttamam || 29 ||

tato dīpāṅgulītailairvartiḥ syādarkatantubhiḥ |
prajvālya nṛkapāle tu tatpātre dhṛtakajjalam |
añjayennetrayugalaṃ devairapi na dṛśyate || 30 ||

oṃ namo niśācaramahāmaheśvara mama paryaṭataḥ sarvalokalocanāni bandhaya bandhaya devyājñāpayati svāhā |

oṃ namo niśācaramahāmaheśvara mama paryaṭataḥ sarvalokalocanāni bandhaya bandhaya devyājñāpayati svāhā |

arkaśālmalikārpāsapaṭṭapadmajatantubhiḥ || 31 ||

pañcabhirvartikābhiśca nṛkapāleṣu pañcasu |
navanītena dīpāḥ syuḥ kajjalaṃ nṛkapālataḥ || 32 ||

grāhayetpañcabhiryatnātpūrvavacca śivālaye |
pañcasthānīyajātaṃ tu ekīkuryāttu taṃ punaḥ |
mantrayitvāñjayennetre devairapi na dṛśyate || 33 ||

oṃ hūṃ phaṭ svāhā kāli kāli mahākāli māṃsaśoṇitabhakṣiṇi raktakṛṣṇamukhe devī mā me paśyatu mānuṣeti oṃ hūṃ phaṭ svāhā ||

etanmantrāyutajapātsiddhirbhavati || uktādṛśyaprayogāṇāmayameva maṃtraḥ | anena mantreṇāṣṭottaraśatābhimantritāṅgulitaila prayogātsiddhā bhavanti || 34 ||

oṃ hūṃ phaṭ svāhā kāli kāli mahākāli māṃsaśoṇitabhakṣiṇi raktakṛṣṇamukhe devī mā me paśyatu mānuṣeti oṃ hūṃ phaṭ svāhā ||

aṅgulītailasaṃsiktā yavāḥ saptadināvadhi |
trilohaveṣṭitāsteṣāṃ guṭikāṃ kārayecchubhām |
adṛśyakāriṇī sā tu mukhasthā nātra saṃśayaḥ || 35 ||

tattaile sarṣapāḥ śvetāstrilohena tu veṣṭitāḥ 
guṭikā mukhamadhyasthā sā'dṛśyakariṇī matā || 36 ||

kṛṣṇakākasya rudhiraṃ pittaṃ gomāyusambhavam |
kākārinakhacañcvāpi samabhāgaṃ vicūrṇayet || 37 ||

ṛkṣe punarvasau varti kṛtvā netre ca rañjayet |
adṛśyo bhavati kṣipre sarvakāryaprasādhakaḥ || 38 ||

kṛṣṇakukkuṭapucchāgraṃ nirmālyaṃ mṛtakasya ca |
kākanetraṃ ca maricaṃ piṣṭvā kāryaṃ ca mūtrakaiḥ || 39 ||

kalāyārddhapramāṇena vaṭīṃ kṛtvā praśoṣayet |
tenaivāñjitanetrastu adṛśyo bhavati dhruvam || 40 ||

kṛṣṇamārjārāntarasthaṃ raktaṃ saṃgṛhyabhāvayet |
naktamālasya tailena tatra śvetārkasūtrajām || 41 ||

varti prajvālya vajrasya dale saṃgṛhya kajjalam |
tenāñjanena manujastvadṛśyo bhavati dhruvam || 42 ||

sukṛṣṇaṃ caiva mārjāraṃ mārayitvā catuṣpathe || 43 ||

prokṣaṇaṃ kārayitvā tu dinānāṃ pañcaviṃśatiḥ |
tatsaṃgṛhya prayatnena kṣālayecchītavāriṇā || 44 ||

yadasthi ca śrotrabhedi syādgrāhyaṃ yatnato'bhayam |
pūjayitvā mahākālīṃ gorocanasamanvitam || 45 ||

nakulasya tu pittena bhāvayitvā prapeṣayet |
tadvartitilakādeva naro'dṛśyo bhaved dhruvam || 46 ||

nṛmāṃsaṃ ca śivāmāṃsaṃ yatnato grāhayed budhaḥ ||
prathamarajasvalāyāśca rudhireṇa vaṭīṃ kuru ||
trilohaveṣṭitā sā tu mukhasthā'dṛśyakāriṇī || 47 ||

kṛṣṇamārjjāramuṇḍe tu kṛṣṇaguñjāṃ pravāpayet |
tatphalaṃ vadanasthaṃ hi sākṣādadṛśyakārakam || 48 ||

kokāyā nayanaṃ vāmaṃ trilohena praveṣṭayet |
sā vaṭī mukhamadhyasthā adṛśyaṃ kurute dhruvam || 49 ||

divābhītasya nayanaṃ trilohena praveṣṭayet |
mukhasthaṃ kurute'dṛśyaṃ yathecchaṃ vicarenmahīm || 50 ||

ṛkṣe caivānurādhāyāṃ vandāṃ rāsalavṛkṣakām |
mukhe prakṣipya ca naro'dṛśyaḥ syānnahi saṃśayaḥ || 51 ||

śākhoṭasya ca vandākaṃ nakṣatre mṛgaśīrṣake |
gṛhītvā pānapātreṇa adṛśyo jāyate naraḥ || 52 ||

bharaṇyāṃ tu samāgṛhya vandāṃ kārpāsasambhavām || 53 ||

haste baddhvā hyadṛśyaḥ syātsvātyāṃ vā nimbavṛkṣajām |
pibeduttaraṣāḍhāyāmaśokavṛkṣasambhavām || 54 ||

vandāṃ tadā adṛśyaḥ syādaśvinyāṃ bilvavṛkṣajām |
vandākaṃ vā kare dhṛtvā adṛśyo jāyate naraḥ || 55 ||

iti adṛśyakaraṇam |

atha mṛtasañjīvanī 

mṛtasañjīvanīvidyāṃ pravakṣyāmi samāsataḥ |
liṅgamaṅkolavṛkṣādhaḥ sthāpayitvā prapūjayet || 56 ||

navaṃ ghaṭaṃ ca tatraiva pūjayelliṅgasaṃnidhau |
vṛkṣaṃ liṅgaṃ ghaṭaṃ caiva sūtrenaikena veṣṭayet || 57 ||

caturbhissādhakairnityaṃ praṇipatya krameṇa tu |
evaṃ dvidvidinaṃ kuryādaghoreṇa samarcayet || 58 ||

oṃ aghorebhyotha ghorebhyo ghoraghoratarebhyaḥ |
sarvataḥ sarva sarvebhyo namaste rudra rūpebhyaḥ ||
ukta yogānāmayaṃ mantraḥ |

puṣpādiphalapākāntaṃ sādhanaṃ kārayed budhaḥ |
phalāni pakvānyādāya pūrvoktaṃ pūrayed ghaṭam || 59 ||

tadghaṭaṃ pūjayennityaṃ gandhapuṣpākṣatādibhiḥ |
tuṣavarjjaṃ tataḥ kuryādbījānāṃ ghaṭṭayenmukham || 60 ||

tanmukhaṃ bṛhaṇaṃ vṛttaṃ kiñcit kiñcitpralepayet | 
vistīrṇamukhabhāgāntaḥ kumbhakārakarodbhavām || 61 ||

mṛttikāṃ lepayettatra tāni bījāni ropayet |
kuṃḍalyākārayogena yatnādūrdhvamukhāni vai || 62 ||

śuṣkaṃ taṃ tāmrapātrorddhvaṃ bhāṇḍaṃ deyamadhomukham |
ātape dhārayettailaṃ grāhayettaṃ ca rakṣayet || 63 ||

māsārddhaṃ caiva tattailaṃ māsārddhaṃ tilatailakam |
nasyaṃ deyaṃ mṛtasyaiva kāladaṣṭasya tatkṣaṇāt || 64 ||

athavā(-) puṃśukraṃ pārade tulyaṃ tena tailena mardayet |
nasyaṃ deyaṃ mṛtasyaikaṃ kāladaṣṭasya vā kṣaṇāt || 65 ||

tatkṛtvā jīvyate satyaṃ gatenāpi yamālayam |
rogāpamṛtyusarpādimṛto jīvati hi svayam |
jīvamāyāti nocitraṃ mahādevena bhāṣitam || 66 ||

puṣyabhāskarayogena guḍūcīmūlamāharet |
karṣamuṣṇajalaiḥ pīto mṛte mṛtyuharo bhavet || 67 ||


[iti śrīnityanāthaviracite kāmaratne nidhidarśanāñjanādi mṛtyusañjīvanī kathanaṃ nāma trayopadeśaḥ] || 13 ||




[caturdaśopadeśaḥ] 

atha viṣanivāraṇam 

śambhunoktaṃ samāsena viṣaṃ sthāvarajaṅgamam |
kṛtrimaṃ yogajaṃ caiva vṛścikādyaṃ tu sambhavam || 1 ||

kramāllakṣaṇameteṣāṃ mantrayuktaṃ vadāmyaham |
nāma vakṣye viṣāṇāṃ tu śambhunā kīrtitaṃ purā || 2 ||

darado vatsanābhaśca mustakaṃ puṣkaraṃ viṣam |
krūraṃ śaṭhaṃ karmaṭhaṃ ca haridraṃ kālakūṭakam || 3 ||

indrabījaṃ citrabījaṃ haritaṃ gālavaṃ viṣam |
śṛṅgī karkaṭaśṛṅgī ca meṣaśṛṅgī halāhalam || 4 ||

śākūṭaṃ raktaśṛṅgī ca hyañjanaṃ puṇḍarīkakam |
saṃkocaṃ madhupākaṃ ca rohiṇaṃ meduraṃ tathā || 5 ||

pañcaviṃśatibhirbhedairvijñeyaṃ sthāvaraṃ viṣam |
etanmadhye hyatikrūraṃ saṃkocaṃ kālakūṭakam || 6 ||
