bhṛṅgī mustaṃ vatsanābhaṃ pañcamaṃ tu viṣādviṣam |
eṣāṃ dehapraviṣṭānāṃ śṛṇu lakṣaṇamucyate || 7 ||

vāntimūrcchātisāraṃ ca bhrāntiśśūlaṃ ca kampanam |
kāsaśvāsau tīvradāho lakṣayed gadgadasvaram || 8 ||

putrajīvaphalāmajjāṃ śītatoyena peṣayet |
bhojane cāñjane pāne lepe sarvaviṣāpaham || 9 ||

sthāvaraṃ jaṅgamaṃ krūraṃ kṛtrimaṃ yogajaṃ tathā |
niṣkamātraṃ na sandehaḥ kāladaṣṭo'pi jīvati || 10 ||

śāḍvalaṃ kaṅkaṇaṃ tutthaṃ kaṭphalaṃ rajanī vacā |
naramūtreṇa saṃpītvā ekaikaṃ tu viṣaṃ haret || 11 ||

samūlapatrāṃ sarpākṣīṃ tathaiva devadālikām |
girikaṇyāśca vā mūlaṃ naramūtreṇa pūrvavat || 12 ||

trikaṭuṃ devadāliṃ ca nasye sarvaviṣāpaham |
brahmadaṇḍīyamūlaṃ tu madhunā saha bhakṣayet || 13 ||

śvetāṅkolasya mūlaṃ tu mukhasthe tilake'thavā |
mukhasthairaṇḍamūlaṃ vā chāyāśuṣkaṃ viṣāpaham || 14 ||

ṭaṅkaṇaṃ devadāliṃ ca jalaiḥ pāne viṣāpaham |
nīlasarpasya pucchaṃ tu kṛkalāsasya pucchakam || 15 ||

tāmreṇa veṣṭitaṃ kṛtvā mudrikāṃ tāṃ ca dhārayet |
tayā spṛṣṭajalaṃ pītaṃ sthāvaraṃ jaṅgamaṃ haret || 16 ||

mantraḥ 

ātarathākī yātarathā ślātohāte upajīlo vṛkṣamuṃcaculu karapāpiyo siṃdūrasāvāṇī kālakūṭaviṣa śrīgorakṣera vāṇīmukhe dile haye amṛtavāṇī viṣakhā(-)u viṣajāro viṣakaroti bhara viṣahari āche tridaśara īśvara mahādevera ājñā gorakṣera vāṇī || 1 ||

sindūrasāra vāṇī kālakūṭa viṣaśarīramadhye hayāpānī śrīgorakṣera ājñā || 2 ||

khedāya guru ādama sikha āmukāre kāndenāhī kālakūṭa viṣa || 3 ||

mahādevera ājñā gorakṣera vāṇī kālakūṭa viṣa śarīramadhye hayāpānī kālakūṭaviṣa dṛṣṭi hana hana oṃ pārṇā | mantrābhyukṣitaṃ viṣaṃ maṃtreṇa vāratrayamabhimantritaṃ bhakṣayet || 4 ||

ātarathākī yātarathā ilātā hārata upajīle vṛkṣamuṃji caluka cijā pijo sindūra sāraṇī kālakūṭa viṣa śarīramadhye hayāpānī śrīgorakṣera ājñā | viṣa ukhāḍa viṣājāro viṣākaroti bhara viṣahārī āche tridaśa īśvara mahādevera ājñā gorakṣera vāṇī sindūra sāravāṇī kālakūṭa viṣa śarīramadhye hayāpānī 

bhūnāgasattvasaṃjātāṃ mudrikāṃ dhārayetkare |
na tasyākramate satyaṃ viṣaṃ sthāvarajaṅgamam |
tatspṛṣṭodakapānena viṣaṃ sarvaṃ vinaśyati || 17 ||

śirīṣabadhnakaṃ grāhyaṃ revatyāṃ candanānvitam |
tadghṛṣṭaṃ marditaṃ gātre tasyāṅge viṣanāśanam || 18 ||

varāhagodhānakulaśaśakukkuṭapittakam |
śvettāyā girikarṇyāśca phalamūlaṃ vipeṣayet || 19 ||

pāne sarvaviṣaṃ hanti mṛtopyuttiṣṭhate kṣaṇāt |
nāmnā cāmṛtayogoyaṃ purā rudreṇa bhāṣitaḥ || 20 ||

paṇavaṃ paṭahaṃ caiva hyanenaiva pralepayet |
mṛto'pi viṣayogena śrutvā vādyaṃ prabudhyate || 21 ||

śvetāparājitāmūlaṃ pītvā dugdhena mānavaḥ |
sthāvaraṃ ca viṣaṃ hanti udarasthaṃ na saṃśayaḥ || 22 ||

sasindhukāñjikaṃ pītvā sthāvarādiviṣaṃ haret || 23 ||

viṣanivāraka mantra

oṃ namo bhagavate uḍḍāmareśvarāya kuñcitāmṛta marcatajaṭāya ṭhaḥ ṭhaḥ svāhā |

iti viṣanivāraṇam |

atha (jaṅgama) sarpaviṣanivāraṇam 

jātīnāṃ nāma rūpaṃ ca jaṃgamānāmihocyate |
brāhmaṇāḥ śvetavarṇāḥ syuḥ kṣatriyā raktavarṇakāḥ ||
vaiśyāstu pītavarṇāśca kṛṣṇavarṇāstu śūdrakāḥ || 24 ||

sarpaviṣaharaṇamantraḥ

oṃ meghamāledhimāle haraharaviṣavegaṃ hāhāhahāsavārihaṃ ambe ambe sarvaviṣanāśini mahāmāye hūṃ hūṃ laṃ saḥ ṭhaḥ ṭhaḥ svāhā | jaḥ jaḥ jaḥ sarvaviṣanāśinīmeghamālā nāma vidyā oṃ prauṭhaḥ nīlakaṇṭhāya svāhā | oṃ namo bhagavati raktāṅge raktalocane kapilajaṭe kapilaśarīre kaṭkaṭkahakahabhañjabhañjaśūlāgrapāṇi ugracaṇḍatarpemahātarpe kṛṣṇe atikṛṣṇe idaṃ mānuṣaṃ śarīra manupraviśya bhrama bhrama bhrama bhrama bhrāmaya bhrāmaya nṛtya nṛtya bahurūpe vilāsinibhakte kṛṣṇāṅgī pūraya pūraya veśaya veśaya viśvarūpiṇī raktapaṭṭirudre ājñāpayati hūṃ phaṭ ṭhaḥ ṭhaḥ eṣā svāsthāveṣāvidyā oṃ namo bhagavate pārśvayakṣāya hrīṃ hrīṃ hrūṃ hrūṃ dhenu dhenu kampa kampa purāṇaṃ dṛṣṭvā māveśaya māveśaya svāhā ||  || 25 ||

anantaḥ kulikaścaiva vāsukī śaṅkhapālakaḥ |
takṣakaśca mahāpadmaḥ karkoṭaḥ padma eva ca |
kulanāgāṣṭakaṃ hyete teṣāṃ cihnaṃ śivoditam || 26 ||

śvetapadmamanantasya mūrdhni pṛṣṭhe ca dṛśyate |
śaṅkhaṃ śeṣasya śirasi vāsukeḥ pṛṣṭha utpalam || 27 ||

trinetrāṅkastu karkoṭastakṣakaḥ śaśakāṅkitaḥ |
jvalatriśūlacandrārddhaṃ śaṅkhapālasya mūrddhani || 28 ||

rājavattu samo bindurmahāpadmasya pṛṣṭhataḥ |
padmapṛṣṭhe ca dṛśyante suraktāḥ pañcabindavaḥ || 29 ||

evaṃ yo vetti jātyādīnnāma binduṃ śivoditam |
tasya mantrauṣadhānyeva siddhyante nānyathā punaḥ || 30 ||

dūratastasya sarpādyāḥ patanti garuḍe yathā |
kālākhyā nāmataścihnaṃ śivenoktaṃ yathāpurā || 31 ||

jñeyo daśavidho daṃśo bhujaṅgānāṃ bhiṣagvaraiḥ |
bhītonmattaḥ kṣudhārtaśca ākrānto viṣadarpitaḥ || 32 ||

āhārecchuḥ kṣudhārtaśca svasthānaparirakṣaṇaḥ |
navamo vairisandhāno daśamaḥ kālasaṃjñakaḥ || 33 ||

sthānabhedena daśaphalam 

udyāne jīrṇakūpe ca vaṭaśṛṅgāṭacatvare |
śuṣkevṛkṣe śmaśāne ca plakṣaśleṣmātaśigruke || 34 ||

devatāyatanāgāre tathā ca śākavṛkṣake |
eṣu sthāneṣu ye daṣṭāste na jīvanti mānavā || 35 ||

bhrūmadhye cādhare mūrdhni jaṅghe netre bhruvau tathā |
grīvācibukakaṇṭheṣu karamadhye ca tāluke || 36 ||

stanayoḥ saṃdhayoḥ kukṣau liṅgavṛṣaṇanābhiṣu |
marmasandhiṣu sarvatra sarpadaṣṭo na jīvati || 37 ||

ravau bhaume śanervāre sarpadaṣṭo  na jīvati |
aṣṭamī pañcamī pūrṇā hyamāvāsyā caturdaśī |
aśubhāstithayaḥ proktāssarpadaṣṭo na jīvati || 38 ||

kṛttikā śravaṇaṃ mūlaṃ viśākhā bharaṇī tathā |
pūrvāstisrastathā citrāśleṣābheṣu na jīvati || 39 ||

madhyāhne sandhyayoścaiva hyarddharātre niśātyaye |
kālavelā vāravelā sarpadaṣṭo na jīvati || 40 ||

sarpasya tālumadhye tu yo danto'ṅkuśasannibhaḥ | 
vimuñcati viṣaṃ ghoraṃ tenāyaṃ kālasaṃjñakaḥ || 41 ||

cakrākṛtiśca vā daṃśaḥ pakvajambūphalākṛtiḥ || 42 ||

sunīlaḥ śvetarakto vā tridaśo'pi na jīvati |
sravenmūtrapurīṣaṃ vā hṛcchūlaṃ chardidāhakṛt || 43 ||

sānunāsikayā vākyaṃ sandhibhedamathāpi vā | 
tāmrābhaṃ netrayugalamathavā kākanīlakam |
viyogo devadaṣṭākhyaṃ taṃ vidyātkālapāśagam || 44 ||

secanādudakenāṅge śītalena muhurmuhuḥ |
romāñcā na bhavedyasya taṃ vidyātkālapāśagam || 45 ||

vedanā daṃkṣamūle vā naṣṭadaṃśo'thavā bhavet |
tatkṣaṇāttīvradāhaśca so'pi kālena bhakṣitaḥ || 46 ||

somaṃ sūryaṃ yadā dīptaṃ na paśyati ca tārakam || 47 ||

darpaṇe salile vātha ghṛtatailena vā mukham | 
na paśyedvīkṣyamāṇo'pi kāladaṣṭo na saṃśayaḥ || 48 ||

jñātvā kālamakālaṃ ca paścādbheṣajamācaret |
sarpadaṃśe viṣaṃ nāsti kāladaṣṭo na jīvati || 49 ||

tasya tatrāpi karttavyā cikitsā jīvanāvadhi |
rasadivyauṣadhīnāṃ tu prabhāvātkālajidbhavet || 50 ||

viṣacikitsā 

sūtakaṃ gandhakaṃ tulyaṃ ṭaṅkaṇaṃ rajanīsamam |
devadālyā drarvermathyaṃ dinaṃ niṣkaṃ tu bhakṣayet || 51 ||

kālaśailāśanirnāma rasaḥ sarvaviṣāpahaḥ |
naramūtraṃ pibeccānu kāladaṣṭopi jīvati || 52 ||

śvetāparājitāmūlaṃ devadālīyamūlakam |
vāriṇā peṣitaṃ nasyaṃ kāladaṣṭopi jīvati || 53 ||

dadhimadhunavanītaṃ pippalīśṛṅgaveraṃ 
maricamapi ca kuṣṭhaṃ cāṣṭamaṃ saindhavaṃ syāt ||

yadi daśati saroṣastakṣako vāsukirvā 
samasadanagataḥ syādānayettatkṣaṇena || 54 ||

kuṭakī muśalīmūlaṃ pītvā toyairviṣāpaham |
vṛścikā vīraṇāmūlaṃ lepātsarvaviṣāpaham || 55 ||

vāriṇā ṭaṅkaṇaṃ pītamathavārkasya mūlakam |
saindhavaṃ vā nṛmūtreṇa pratyekaṃ viṣanāśanam || 56 ||

indravāruṇimūlaṃ tu śuklā cātha punarnavā |
vandhyākarkoṭakīmūlaṃ muśalīśikhimūlakam |
taṇḍulodaka pānena pratyekaṃ viṣanāśanam || 57 ||

gaukṣīrai rajanī kuṣṭhaṃ kvāthapānaṃ viṣāpaham || 58 ||

bhṛṅgarājasya mūlaṃ tu triśūlinyāstu mūlakam |
toyairvā taṇḍulīmūlaṃ pratyekaṃ viṣajidbhavet || 59 ||

somarājībījacūrṇaṃ sakṛdgomūtrabhāvitam |
carācaraviṣaghnaṃ tanmṛtasañjīvanaṃ pibet || 60 ||

kaṭutuṃbyudbhavaṃ mūlaṃ sūkṣmaṃ gomūtrapeṣitam |
chāyāśuṣkavaṭī mūtraiḥ pānairlepairviṣāpahā || 61 ||

gomūtrairnaramūtrairvā purāṇena ghṛtena vā |
haridrāpānamātreṇa viṣaṃ hanti carācaram || 62 ||

daśavarṣātparaṃ sarpi purāṇamiti kathyate |
tatpānādañjanādvāpi haratyeva na saṃśayaḥ |
yadi sarpaviṣārtānāṃ sarvasthānagataṃ viṣam || 63 ||

gokṣīrairajanīkvāthaṃ pibetsarvaviṣāpaham |
haridrākuṣṭhamadhvājyaṃ bhuktaṃ sarvaviṣāpaham || 64 ||

mūlaṃ tu śvetaguñjāyā vaktrasthaṃ viṣanāśanam |
puṣyoddhṛtaṃ tasya mūlaṃ nasyena viṣanāśanam || 65 ||

pāṭhādraveṇa tanmūlaṃ pāne syātkālakūṭajit |
arkamūlena saṃlipya daṃśaṃ viṣaharaṃ mahat || 66 ||

rakta citrendragopābhyāṃ tathā viṣavināśakam |
sarpa haritavarṇaṃ ca pucchāgre pāṭayecchiraḥ || 67 ||

śuklaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ pṛthakkāryyaṃ nasyaṃ sarvaviṣāpaham |
śuklaṃ śukle dakṣiṇāṅge kṛṣṇaṃ kṛṣṇe ca vāmake |
mṛtasañjīvanaṃ hyetatkāladaṣṭopi jīvati || 68 ||

tiktakośātakīkvāthaṃ madhvājyasaṃyutaṃ pibet |
tatkṣaṇādvamayedyastu viṣayogādvimuñcati || 69 ||

kuṭakī jambumūlaṃ vā takrāmlairvā pibejjalam |
tatkṣaṇād vamate śīghraṃ viṣayogādvimucyate || 70 ||

rājavṛkṣatvacaṃ grāhyaṃ śuklaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ pṛthak pṛthak 
śuklavṛkṣe tu śuklāntāṃ caturviṃśatibhiḥ saha || 71 ||

maricaiḥ pānaniṣṭhasya kṛṣṇe kṛṣṇatvacaṃ tathā |
pītvā tairnirviṣo daṣṭaḥ kathitaṃ haramekhale || 72 ||

kuṅkumālaktakaṃ lodhraṃ śilā caivātha rocanā |
guṭikālepanāddhanti viṣaṃ sthāvarajaṅgamam || 73 ||

dve haridre śilā tālaṃ kuṅkumaṃ kuṣṭhakaṃ jalaiḥ |
guṭikā lepamātreṇa viṣaṃ hanti mahādbhutam || 74 ||

pūtīkarañjabījasya majjānaṃ kāravellajam |
piṣṭvā pibetsasarpiṣkaṃ viṣaṃ hanti na saṃśayaḥ || 75 ||

pippalīṃ maricaṃ kuṣṭhaṃ gṛhadhūmaṃ manaḥśilām |
tālakaṃ sarṣapāḥ śvetā gavāṃ pittena loḍayet || 76 ||

guṭikāñjana nasyena pānābhyāñjanalepanāt |
takṣakeṇāpi daṣṭasya nirviṣī kurute kṣaṇāt || 77 ||

pathyā kṣaudraṃ maricaṃ ca patraṃ hiṅgu śilā vacā |
jalena guṭikāṃ nasyetkāladaṣṭo'pi jīvati || 78 ||

aśvagandhā meghanādo gomūtraṃ mahiṣākṣakam |
gṛhadhūmena vā lepaḥ śiraḥkaṇṭhaviṣaṃ haret || 79 ||

pañcāṅgamaśvagandhāyāśchāgīmūtreṇa peṣayet |
lepe pāne na sandeho nānāviṣavināśanam || 80 ||

śilā hiṃgu vacā vyoṣamabhayātvak ca patrakam |
nasye vāsukidaṣṭasya nirviṣaṃ śītavāriṇā || 81 ||

putrajīvaphalānmajjāṃ gavāṃkṣīreṇa peṣayet |
lepanāñjana nasyena kāladaṣṭo'pi jīvati || 82 ||

kṛṣṇadhattūramūlasya cūrṇaṃ grāhyaṃ palonmitam |
karañjatailakarṣeṇa vaṭīṃ kṛtvā tu dhārayet |
jambīrasya rasaiḥ pītvā raudrīviṣanivāraṇam || 83 ||

lajjālumūlaṃ nīlyāṃ vā mūlaṃ svacchena vāriṇā |
pītvā raudrīviṣaṃ hanti lepādguñjābalāṃ nataḥ || 84 ||

gṛhadhūmaṃ haridre dve samūlaṃ tandulīyakam |
api vāsukinā daṣṭāḥ pibeddadhighṛtānvitam || 85 ||

tandulīyakamūlaṃ tu pītaṃ tandulavāriṇā |
takṣakeṇāpi daṣṭasya nirviṣaṃ kurute dhruvam || 86 ||

kulikāmūlanasyena kāladaṣṭo'pi jīvati |
oṃ ādityacakṣuṣā dṛṣṭo'haṃ hara viṣaṃ svāhā ||
anena mantreṇoktayogānabhimantrayet || 87 ||

aparājitāmūlaṃ tu ghṛtena tvaggataṃ viṣam |
payasā raktagaṃ hanti māṃsagaṃ kuṣṭhacūrṇataḥ || 88 ||

oṃ ādityacakṣuṣā dṛṣṭaḥ dṛṣṭo'haṃ hara viṣaṃ svāhā |

asthigaṃ rajanīyuktaṃ medogaṃ lāṅgalīyutam |
majjagaṃ pippalīyuktaṃ caṇḍālīmūlasaṃyutam |
śukragaṃ hanti lauhityaṃ tasmāddeyāparājitā || 89 ||

iti bhāvo bhavedyasya ātmarūpamidaṃ jagat |
tatsarvairviṣakīṭādyairbhakṣyamāṇo na bādhyate || 90 ||

sadyaḥ sarpeṇa daṣṭasya vāmanāsikayā kṛtaḥ |
lepaḥ karṇamalenāpi nṛmūtraiḥ secanena vā || 91 ||

stambhate garalaṃ tena norddhvaṃ dhāvati dhātuṣu |
varāhakarṇikāmūlaṃ haste baddhaṃ viṣāpaham || 92 ||

śirīṣapuṣpasvarasaiḥ saptāhaṃ maricaṃ sitam |
bhāvitaṃ sarpadaṣṭānāṃ pāne nasye'ñjane hitam || 93 ||

svacchandabhairavī vidyā kathyate viṣanāśinī |

oṃ namo bhagavatī svacchandabhairavī mahābhairavī kālakūṭaviṣaṃ sphoṭaya sphoṭaya visphāraya visphāraya khādaya khādaya avatāraya avatāraya nāsti viṣahālāhalaviṣakṛtrimaṃ viṣaṃ saṃyogaviṣaṃ hyatyugraviṣaṃ sthāvaraṃ viṣaṃ jaṅgamaṃ viṣaṃ kālacañcuyāyarā(-)iṣṭamantrastaḍadarghāyaṇa ithaya ithaya oṃ kālāya mahākālāya kālamarddadevī amṛtagarbhadevī oṃ oṃ phaṭ phaṭ svāhā | anena mantreṇa mantrayet || saptadhā navadhā jalamabhimantrya tenābhiṣicya tajjalaṃ pāyayecca nirviṣaṃsyādiyaṃ svacchandabhairavī vidyā || oṃ hrūṃ hrūṃ saṃsvaḥ haṃsaḥ || vā oṃ krūṃ krūṃ saṃsvaḥ haṃsaḥ | anena mantreṇābhimaṃtrita pānīyapānanāpi mārjjanena vā nirviṣaḥ syāt ||

oṃ namo bhagavatīsvacchandabhairavī mahābhairavī kālakūṭaviṣaṃ sphoṭaya sphoṭaya visphāraya visphāraya khādaya khādaya avatāraya avatāraya nāsti viṣa hālāhalaviṣaṃ kṛtrimaṃ viṣaṃ saṃyogaviṣaṃ hyatyugraviṣaṃ sthāvaraṃviṣaṃ jaṃgamaṃviṣaṃ kālacañcu yāyarā(-)iṣṭamantrastaḍadarghāyaṇa ithaya ithaya oṃ kālāya mahākālāya kālamardadevī amṛtagarbhadevī oṃ oṃ phaṭ phaṭ svāhā |

oṃ hrūṃ hrūṃ saṃsvaḥ haṃsaḥ | athavā oṃ krūṃ krūṃ saṃsvah haṃsaḥ |

devadāru citrakaṃ ca karavīrārkalāṅgalī |
mūlāni vāriṇā piṣṭvā kāladaṣṭaharaṃ pibet || 94 ||

mantrauṣadhiprayogeṇa yadi daṣṭo na jīvati |
chedayattīkṣṇaśastreṇa daṃśasthānaṃ bhiṣagvaraḥ |
sthāvaraṃ tu viṣaṃ dadyāddaṣṭo daṣṭena hanyate || 95 ||

yastu saṃroṣitaḥ sarpo dhūmaṃ vakrādvimuñcati |
tuṇḍāgre piśitaṃ bhuktvā bahuśastena daṃśitaḥ || 96 ||

aśakyamagadairanyairviṣeṇaiva cikitsayet || 97 ||

kṣīrakṣaudraghṛtairyukta dviguñjaṃ pāyayedviṣam |
viṣeṇa lepayeddaṃśaṃ kāladaṣṭo'pi jīvati || 98 ||

mṛtasañjīvanaṃ khyātaṃ nirguṇḍī tagaraṃ viṣam |
piṇḍītagaramūlaṃ ca puṣyeṇotpāṭya yojitam |
daṃśe deśaṃ mṛtasyāpi daṣṭo jīvati tatkṣaṇāt || 99 ||

sarpadaṣṭo yadā vīrastaṃ sarpa daṃśate svayam |
mukto'sau mriyate sarpaḥ svayaṃ nirviṣatāṃ vrajet || 100 ||

yadvā tadvā phalaṃ dantaissarpabhāvena bhakṣayet || 101 ||

dantairvā daṃśayedbhūmiṃ daṇḍavatpatito naraḥ |
sarpabhāve na sandeho na tasya kramate viṣam || 102 ||

atyantaviṣayogārttaṃ jalamadhye vinikṣipet || 103 ||

mūlaṃ tandulavāriṇā pibati yaḥ pratyaṅgirāsambhavaṃ 
niṣpaṣṭaṃ śuci bhadrayogadivase tasyāhi bhītiḥ kutaḥ |
darpādeva phaṇī yadā daśati taṃ mohānvitaṃ mānavaṃ 
sthāne tatra sa eva yāti niyataṃ cakrī yamasyācirāt || 104 ||

āṣāḍhaśuklapañcamyāṃ kaṭyāṃ śirīṣamūlakam |
tandulodaka pānena sarpadaṃśo na jāyate |
bhramādvā daśate sarpastadā sarpo vinaśyati || 105 ||

puṣye śvetārkamūlaṃ tu śvetavarṣāmbumūlakam || 106 ||

saṃgṛhya peyaṃ tadṛkṣe snātvā tandulavāriṇā |
sarpabhītivināśārthaṃ pratisaṃvatsaraṃ naraiḥ || 107 ||

masūranimbapatrābhyāṃ khādenmeṣagate ravau |
abdamekaṃ na bhītiḥ syādviṣārtasya na saṃśayaḥ |
atiroṣānvitastasya takṣakaḥ kiṃ kariṣyati || 108 ||

kṛkalāsasya dantāṃśca śvetasūtreṇa veṣṭayet |
bāhau baddhvā viṣaṃ hanti viṣaṃ bhuktvā na bādhyate |
sarpavṛścikamūṣāṇāṃ mukhastambhaḥ prajāyate || 109 ||

oṃ śabarī kīrttaya sañjāva sañjāva svāhā |
sahasrajapātsiddhiḥ | anena mantreṇa haste bandhayet || 110 ||

pātālagāruḍīmūlaṃ lambamānaṃ gṛhe sthitam |
dṛṣṭvā gacchanti te dūraṃ sarpādyā viṣakīṭakāḥ || 111 ||

oṃ śabarī kīrtaya sañjāva sañjāva svāhā |

oṃ plaḥ sarpakulāya svāhā ||

iti sarpaviṣanivāraṇam |

atha vṛścikaviṣanivāraṇam 

śirīṣabījaṃ gomedaṃ dāḍimasya tu mūlakam |
arkakṣīrayutaṃ hanti dhūpo vṛścikajaṃ viṣam || 112 ||

mayūrapārāvatakukkuṭānāṃ grāhyaṃ purīṣaṃ saha bhānumūlaiḥ ||

dhūpau nihantyāśu viṣaṃ samastaṃ caturvidhaṃ vṛścika vṛścikasarpajātam || 113 ||

rajanīcūrṇadhūpena viṣaṃ vṛścikajaṃ haret |
vastreṇācchādya gātrāṇi dhūpadhūpaṃ ca pāyayet |
daṃśaṃ ca dhūpayecchīghraṃ sarvadhūpeṣvayaṃ vidhiḥ || 114 ||

toyairvā nāgaraṃ nasyaṃ pibedvā saindhavaṃ ghṛtam |
arkadhattūramūlaṃ vā jalapāne viṣāpaham || 115 ||

putrajīvaphalānmajjāṃ palāśotthāṃ karañjām |
majjāṃ toyaḥ pralepo'yaṃ hanti vṛścikajaṃ viṣam || 116 ||

hiṅgu vā jalalepena vṛścikotthaṃ viṣaṃ haret |
tilamātraṃ viṣaṃ khādellepādvā nāśayedviṣam || 117 ||

ghṛtārkadugdhalepena yaṣṭyā vā dhūpitena vā |
bījapūrakamūlasya lepādvāpi harītakī || 118 ||

sikthakaṃ saptadhā bhāvyaṃ snuhyarkapayasā'tape |
tattaptaṃ vahninā spṛṣṭaṃ daṃśasthāne viṣaṃ haret || 119 ||

lepo jātīguḍābhyāṃ vā haridrālepanena vā | 
vṛścikasya viṣaṃ hanti pratyekaṃ naiva saṃśayaḥ || 120 ||

mātuluṅgasya mūlaṃ tu ravivāre samuddharet |
uttarābhimukhenaiva hrūṃ (krūṃ) mantroccāraṇātspṛśet || 121 ||

vāmāṅge dakṣiṇe daṣṭe vāme daṣṭe ca dakṣiṇe |
mārjanena viṣaṃ hanyātsadaṃśaṃ dṛṣṭapratyayam |
saptadhā mārjanenaiva viṣaṃ vṛścikajaṃ haret || 122 ||

asagandhīyamūlaṃ tu mūlaṃ śvetapunarnavā |
ravivāre samuddhṛtya dvābhyāṃ vṛścikadaṃśanam || 123 ||

mārjanena viṣaṃ hantyātsvadṛśā hyanubhāvitam |
karpāsamūlaṃ carvitvā viṣajitkarṇaphūtkṛte || 124 ||

grāhyaṃ haṃsapadīmūlaṃ prātarādityavāsare |
mukhasthaṃ phūtkṛtaṃ karṇe viṣaṃ vṛścikajaṃ haret || 125 ||

oṃ kṣaḥ phaṭ svāhā |

anenāpomārjayonnirviṣo bhavati | ādityarathavegena viṣṇorbāhubalena ca | garuḍapakṣanipātena bhūmyāṃ gaccha mahāviṣa || 

oṃ ṭhaḥ ṭhaḥ ṭhaḥ jaḥ jaḥ jaḥ | oṃ śrīpakṣayogipādājñā iti mantraḥ | himavatyuttare pārśve kapilo nāma vṛścikaḥ | tenāhaṃ preṣito dūto gaccha gaccha mahāviṣa | klīṃ klīṃ svāhā | ḍākinī svāhā phaṭ iti || athavā śāṃkhā māṃkhā sāhīṃ khauṃhīṃ anena garuḍamantreṇa vṛścikadaṣṭe karavīrakāṣṭhenāpomārjayennirviṣo bhavati ||]

oṃ jhaṃ huṃ kraṅ vaṃ vaṃ laṃ kṣaṃ eṃ aiṃ oṃ auṃ haṃ haḥ iti mantreṇa abhimantryapralepayet | hāṃ hīṃ maṃ caṃ oṃ iti mantreṇa olavṛntamabhimaṃtrya tena mārjanāt vṛścikaviṣanāśo bhavati | ayaṃ śivena bhāṣito yogo nāvahelanīyaḥ ||

bakulatvacabījaṃ vā niṣpīḍya daṃśanasthale |
pralepādavṛścikaviṣaharaṇaṃ cābhimaṃtritāt || 126 ||

iti vṛścikaviṣanivāraṇam |

atha śatapadī viṣanivāraṇam 

dīpakotsṛṣṭatailaṃ tu daṃśasthāne pralepayet |
dhūpaṃ dattvā guggulena arkapatraṃ ca veṣṭayet || 127 ||

atha mūṣakaviṣanivāraṇam 

śilātālakakuṣṭhaṃ ca bhāvyaṃ nirguṇḍikādravaiḥ |
pānaṃ mūṣika daṣṭānāṃ dattaṃ tīvraṃ viṣaṃ haret || 128 ||

gṛhagodhāṃ samādāya piṣṭvā tandulavāriṇā |
lepādākhuviṣaṃ hanti pibedvā kṣīrapācitām || 129 ||

sarṣapaṃ kuṅkumaṃ takraṃ samabhāgaṃ ghṛtaṃ pibet |
viṣaṃ mūṣikadaṣṭānāṃ śamamāpnoti tatkṣaṇāt || 130 ||

ciñcāphalasamāyuktaṃ gṛhadhūmaṃ palārddhakam |
purāṇājyena saptāhaṃ lihedākhuviṣaṃ haret || 131 ||

iti mūṣakaviṣanivāraṇam |

atha śvānaviṣanivāraṇam 

śirīṣasya ca bījaṃ vai snuhīkṣīreṇa gharṣitam |
tallepena varārohe naśyetkukkurajaṃ viṣam || 132 ||

guḍaṃ tailārkadugdhaṃ ca lepācchvānaviṣaṃ haret |
piṣṭvāpāmārgamūlaṃ ca karṣaikaṃ madhunā lihet |
śvānadaṣṭaviṣaṃ hanti lepātkukkuṭaviṣṭhayā || 133 ||

unmattaśvānadaṃṣṭrāṇāṃ kumārīdalasaindhavam |
sukhoṣṇaṃ bandhayetpiṇḍaṃ tridinānte sukhāvaham || 134 ||

oṃ haḍavaḍa kuttā khaḍavaḍa dāṃta kutte kī bāṃdho sātauḍāḍha āvai na lohū pākai na ghāva kuttekā viṣa utara jāva vīrahanumantakī duhā(-)ī rāmalachamanakī duhā(-)ī phuromantra īśvarovāca ||

iti śvānaviṣanivāraṇam |

atha matsyabhekādiviṣanivāraṇam 

śirīṣaphalatvakkṣīraṃ pibedbhekaviṣāpaham |
tryūṣamājyaṃ meghanādo bhekamatsyaviṣāpaham || 135 ||

śṛṅgīmatsyaviṣaṃ svedāghṛtacikkāṃ sapiṇḍitām || 136 ||

gṛhagodhāviṣaṃ hanyātkāśmīrīphalanasyataḥ |
pibenmadhusitāyuktaṃ gṛhagodhāviṣaṃ haret || 137 ||

iti matsyabhekādiviṣanivāraṇam |

atha vyāghraviṣanivāraṇam 

vṛkavyāghraśṛgālākhyabhallūkadvipavājinām |
rudhiraṃ srāvayeddaṃśāddahellohaśalākayā || 138 ||

lepātsarpaviṣaṃ hanti mūlaṃ śvetapunarnavā |
kimatra bahunoktena tatkṣaṇādviṣanāśanam || 139 ||

viḍaṅgasya ca pānena vyāghravyālaviṣaṃ haret |
dhattūrapatratoyena cūrṇaṃ trikaṭusambhavam || 140 ||

udarasthaṃ viṣaṃ hanti vyāghravyālasamudbhavam |
karañjatailalepena jvālāṃ vyāghranakhādbhavām || 141 ||

gojihvāmūlikāṃ piṣṭvā jalena madhunā saha |
lepo hi sarvajantūnāṃ nakhatuṇḍaviṣaṃ haret || 142 ||

tathā nimbavacā caiva śamī vṛkṣatvacaṃ tathā |
uṣṇodakena lepaḥ syānnakhatuṇḍaviṣāpahaḥ || 143 ||

tathā dāruharidrāyā lepo dantaviṣāpahaḥ || 144 ||

iti vyāghraviṣanivāraṇam |

atha kīṭaviṣanivāraṇam 

ājyena tandulīmūlaṃ tulasīmūlikāpi vā |
tandulodakapānena kīṭakotthaṃ viṣaṃ haret || 145 ||

lāṅgalyāḥ kaṭutumbyā vā devadāru niśāpi vā |
mūlaṃ bījaṃ kāñjikena lepaḥ kīṭaviṣāpahaḥ || 146 ||

tilaṃ ca sarṣapaṃ kuṣṭhaṃ bījaṃ karañjasambhavam |
udvarttanātpralepādvā sarvakīṭavikārajit || 147 ||

karañjabījaṃ siddhārthaṃ tilairlepo viṣāpahaḥ |
eraṇḍatailalepo vā sarvakīṭaviṣāpahaḥ || 148 ||

niśā dāruniśā caiva mañjiṣṭhā nāgakeśaram |
eṣāṃ lepo nihantyāśu viṣaṃ lūtādisambhavam || 149 ||

iti kīṭaviṣanivāraṇam |

atha sarvajantūnāṃ viṣanivāraṇam 

putrajīvaphalānmajjāṃ śītatoyena peṣitām |
lepanāñjananasyaistu pānādvā niṣkamātrataḥ || 150 ||

vyāghramūṣakagonāsavṛścikādiviṣaṃ haret |
dussahaṃ yadviṣaṃ cāśu visphoṭaṃ ca vināśayet || 151 ||

bandhyākarkoṭakīkandaṃ jalaiḥ piṣṭvā pralepayet |
sarpamaṣakamārjjāravṛścikādiviṣaṃ haret || 152 ||

iti sarvajantūnāṃ viṣanivāraṇam |

athopaviṣādinivāraṇam 

snuhyarkonmattakaścaiva karavīraśca lāṅgalī |
vajrī jaipālakaḥ kṛṣṇakuṣṭhaṃ guñjā tathaiva ca || 153 ||

mahākālaśca ityādyāḥ smṛtāstūpaviṣā budhaiḥ |
sasindhuṃ kāñjikaṃ pītvā samastopaviṣaṃ haret || 154 ||

karavīraviṣaṃ hanti ghṛtenāpi harītakī |
nimbapatraṃ ghṛtaṃ hanti ghṛtena madhupānataḥ || 155 ||

iti upaviṣādinivāraṇam |

atha kṛtrimaviṣanivāraṇam 

aneka viṣajīvānāṃ cūrṇaṃ hyupaviṣairyutam |
miśritaṃ nakhakeśādyairlohādyaiścūrṇasañcayam || 156 ||

kṛtrimaṃ ca viṣaṃ khyātaṃ pakṣānmāsādvibādhyate |
ālasyaṃ kurute jāḍyaṃ kāsaṃ śvāsaṃ balakṣayam |
raktasrāvo jvaraḥ śoṣaḥ pītacakṣuśca lakṣayet || 157 ||

mṛtaṃ sūtaṃ mṛtaṃ svarṇaṃ śuddhaṃ vai hemamākṣikam |
trayāṇāṃ gandhakaṃ tulyaṃ mardyaṃ kanyādravairdinam || 158 ||

tacca śuṣkaṃ sitākṣaurmāsamekaṃ lihetsadā |
vahnimūlayutaṃ kṣīraṃ manuṣyagaranāśanam || 159 ||

putrajīvaphalānmajjāṃ niṣkamātraṃ gavāṃ payaḥ |
pītvā cogragaraṃ hanyānnānākṛtrimayogajam || 160 ||

śaṭhīpuṣkaramūlasya pānaṃ madyaviṣāpaham || 161 ||

tatpibetkṣīrapānena garatṛṣṇājvarāpaham |
kṣīraṃ mudgayutaṃ pathyaṃ śālyannaṃ paramaṃ hitam || 162 ||

gṛhadhūmaṃ jalaiḥ piṣṭvā tandulīmūlatulyakam |
kalkāccaturguṇaṃ cājyaṃ ghṛtātkṣīraṃ caturguṇam |
ghṛtaśeṣaṃ pacetsarvaṃ pibetsarvaṃgarāpaham || 163 ||

samūlapatrāṃ sarpākṣīṃ jalena kvathitāṃ pibet |
naramūtreścaiva piṣṭāṃ pibetsarvagarāpaham || 164 ||

elātālīśapatrāṇi tryūṣaṇaṃ jīrakaṃ samam |
cūrṇāddvidhā sitā yojyā bhuktvā garaharaṃ bhavet || 165 ||

payasā rajanīkuṣṭhaṃ madhvājyaṃ gṛhadhūmakam |
tandulīmūlasaṃyuktaṃ karṣaṃ garaharaṃ lihet || 166 ||

iti kṛtrimaviṣanivāraṇam |

atha yogajaviṣanivāraṇam 

tailakarpūrajaṃbīrasaṃyogādyogajaṃ viṣam |
samāṃśena tu madhvājyamevaṃ saṃyogajaṃ viṣam || 167 ||

nārikelāmbu karpūraṃ saṃyogādyogajaṃ viṣam |
marīcatumbikāmūlaṃ yogajaṃ viṣameva tat || 168 ||

putraṃ jīvaphalenaiva rajanīmāranālakaiḥ |
devadālīnṛmūtrairvā sarpākṣī cendravāruṇī || 169 ||

girikarṇīyamūlaṃ vā pratyekaṃ viṣajidbhavet |
madhvājyaṃ kākajaṅghāyā dravaiḥ piṣṭvā viṣaṃ haret || 170 ||

girikarṇī nāgapuṣpī muṇḍīpānādviṣaṃ haret || 171 ||

iti yogajaviṣanivāraṇam |

atha bhallātakaviṣanivaraṇam 

bhallātatailasaṃparkātsphoṭaḥ sañjāyate nṛṇām |
navanītaṃ tilaṃ piṣṭvā tallepena tu taṃ jayet || 172 ||

nimbīpatrapralepādvā taṃ jayettatpadena vā |
bhallātakasya mūlasya mṛttikābhiḥ pralepanāt |
tatsañjātavikārāṃśca nāśayatyeva niścitam || 173 ||

[iti śrīnityanāthaviracite kāmaratne viṣanivāraṇaṃ nāma caturdaśopadeśaḥ] || 14 ||



[pañcadaśopadeśaḥ] 

atha yakṣiṇīsādhanam 

sarvāsāṃ yakṣiṇīnāṃ tu dhyānaṃ kuryātsamāhitaḥ |
bhaginīmātṛputrīstrīrūpaṃ tulyaṃ yathopsitam || 1 ||

bhojyaṃ nirāmiṣaṃ cānnaṃ varjyaṃ tāmbūlabhakṣaṇam |
upaviśyājinādau ca prātaḥ snātvā na kaṃ spṛśet || 2 ||

nityakṛtyaṃ tu kṛtvā ca sthāne nirjanake japet |
yāvatpratyakṣatāṃ yānti yakṣiṇyo vāñchitapradāḥ || 3 ||

japellakṣadvayaṃ mantraṃ śmaśāne nirbhayo muniḥ |
daśāṃśaṃ gugguluṃ sājyaṃ hutvā tuṣyati yakṣiṇī || 4 ||

vaṭayakṣiṇīsādhanam (1)

tripathe tu vaṭasthāne rātrau mantraṃ japetsvayam |
lakṣatrayaṃ tataḥ siddhā devī ca vaṭayakṣiṇī || 5 ||

vastrālaṅkārakaṃ divyaṃ rasasiddhirasāyanam |
divyāñjanaṃ tu sā tuṣṭā sādhakāya prayacchati || 6 ||

oṃ hrīṃ vaṭavāsini yakṣakulapatāke vaṭayakṣiṇi ehyehi svāhā |  || 5(-)6 ||

vaṭayakṣiṇīsādhanam (2)

oṃ vaṭavṛkṣaṃ samāruhya lakṣamekaṃ japenmanum |
tatassaptābhimantreṇa kāñjikaiḥ kṣālayenmukham || 7 ||

māsatrayaṃ japedrātrau varaṃ yacchati yakṣiṇī |
rasaṃ rasāyanaṃ divyaṃ kṣudrakarma hyanekadhā |
siddhāni sarvakāryāṇi nānyathā śaṅkaro'bravīt || 8 ||

oṃ namaścandrādyāvākarṇakāraṇa klīṃ svāhā | vā oṃ namo bhagavate rudrāya caṇḍavegine svāhā |

ratipriyāyakṣiṇīsādhanam 

śaṃkhaliptapaṭe devīṃ gauravarṇāṃ dhṛtotpalām |
sarvālaṅkāriṇīṃ divyāṃ samālikhyārcayettataḥ || 9 ||

jātīpuṣpaiḥ sopacāraiḥ sahasraikaṃ tato japet |
trisaṃdhyaṃ saptarātraṃ tu tato rātriṣu nirjapet || 10 ||

arddharātre gate devī samāgatya prayacchati |
pañcaviṃśatidīnārān pratyahaṃ toṣitā satī || 11 ||

oṃ hrīṃ kanakakanaka maithunapriye ratipriye svāhā | athavā 
oṃ hrīṃ ratipriye svāhā |

surasundarīyakṣiṇīsādhanam 

ekaliṅgaṃ mahādevaṃ trisaṃdhyaṃ pūjayetsadā |
dhūpaṃ dattvā japenmantraṃ trisaṃdhyaṃ trisahasrakam || 12 ||

māsamekaṃ tato yāti yakṣiṇī surasundarī |
dattvārghyaṃ praṇamenmantrī brūte sātvaṃ kimicchati || 13 ||

devi dāridryadagdhosmi me tannāśakarī bhava |
tato dadāti sā tuṣṭā vittāyuścirajīvitam || 14 ||

oṃ hrīṃ āgaccha āgaccha surasundari svāhā |

vibhramāyakṣiṇīsādhanam 

vibhramāsādhanaṃ vakṣye prathamaṃ śṛṇu vallabhe |
vibhramāyāṃ prasannāyāṃ vāñchitārthānprayacchati |
pañcāśanmānuṣāṇāṃ ca dadāti bhojanaṃ sadā || 15 ||

oṃ hrīṃ vāṃ vibhramarūpe ehi ehi bhagavati svāhā |

oṃ hrīṃ vibhramarūpe vibhramaṃ kuru ehyehi bhagavati svāhā |

anurāgiṇīyakṣiṇīsādhanam 

kuṅkumena samālikhya bhūrjapatre sulakṣaṇām |
pratipattithimārabhya pūjāṃ kṛtvā japettataḥ || 16 ||

trisandhyaṃ trisahasraṃ tu māsānte pūjayenniśi |
saṃjapedarddharātraṃ tu samāgatya prayacchati |
dīnārāṇāṃ sahasraikaṃ dadāti paritoṣitā || 17 ||

oṃ hrīṃ hrīṃ anurāgiṇi maithunapriye svāhā |

atha viśālāyakṣiṇīsādhanam 

ciñcāvṛkṣatale lakṣaṃ mantramāvarttayecchuciḥ |
śatapuṣpodbhavaiḥ puṣpaiḥ saghṛtairhomamācaret || 18 ||

viśālā ca tatastuṣṭā rasaṃ divyaṃ rasāyanam |
prasannā yacchati tataḥ sarvasiddhirbhaviṣyati || 19 ||

oṃ aiṃ viśāle krīṃ hrīṃ vrīṃ klīṃ krīṃ svāhā |

sāmudrīyakṣiṇīsādhanam 

dhyātvā japettato rātrau sāgarasya taṭe śuciḥ |
lakṣajāpe kṛte siddhirdatte sāgaraceṭakaḥ |
ratnatrayaṃ tathā bhojyaṃ saumyo mantrī sukhī bhavet || 20 ||

oṃ bhagavan samudra dehi ratnāni jalavāso hrīṃ namo'stu te svāhā |

oṃ bhagavan samudra dehi ratnāni jalavāso hrīṃ namo'stu te svāhā |

mahābhayāyakṣiṇīsādhanam 

narāsthinirmitā mālā gale pāṇau ca karṇayoḥ |
dhārayejjapamālāṃ ca tādṛśīṃ tu śmaśānataḥ || 21 ||

lakṣamekaṃ japenmantraṃ sādhako nirbhayaśśuciḥ |
tato mahābhayā yakṣī prayacchati rasāyanam || 22 ||

tasya bhakṣaṇamātreṇa sarvaratnāni cālayet |
valīpalitanirmuktaściraṃjīvī bhavennaraḥ || 23 ||

oṃ hrīṃ trāṃ mahābhaye klīṃ svāhā | athavā oṃ krīṃ mahābhaye klīṃ svāhā |

oṃ hrīṃ trāṃ mahābhaye klīṃ svāhā | athavā oṃ krīṃ mahābhaye klīṃ svāhā |

candrikāyakṣiṇīsādhanam 

śuklapakṣe japettāvadyāvatsaṃdṛśyate vidhuḥ |
pratipatpūrvapūrṇāntaṃ navalakṣamidaṃ japet || 24 ||

amṛtaṃ candrikādattaṃ pītvā jīvo'maro bhavet || 25 ||

oṃ hrīṃ candrike haṃ saḥ krīṃ klīṃ svāhā |

oṃ hrīṃ candrike haṃ saḥ krīṃ klīṃ svāhā |

candradravāyakṣiṇīsādhanam 

raktakambalāyakṣiṇīsādhanam 

japyaṃ māsatrayaṃ raktakambalā sā prasīdati |
mṛtakotthāpanaṃ kuryyātpratimāṃ cālayettathā || 26 ||

oṃ hrīṃ raktakambale mahādevi mṛtakamutthāpaya pratimāṃ cālaya parvatān kampaya kampaya nīlaya nīlaya vihasa vihasa hūṃ hūṃ |

oṃ hrīṃ raktakambale mahādevi mṛtakamutthāpaya pratimāṃ cālaya parvatān kampaya kampaya nīlaya nīlaya vihasa vihasa hūṁ hūṁ |

karṇapiśācinīsādhanam 

pūrvamevāyutaṃ japtvā kṛṣṇa kanyābhimantritaḥ 
hastapādapralepena supte vakti śubhāśubham |
trailokye yādṛśī vārtā tādṛśaṃ kathayetphalam || 27 ||

oṃ hrīṃ saḥ namo bhagavati karṇapiśācini caṇḍavegini vada vada svāhā | (1) 

oṃ krīṃ sanāmaśakti bhagavati karṇapiśācini caṇḍaropiṇi vada vada svāhā | (2) 

vidyujjihvāyakṣiṇīsādhanam 

aṣṭottaraśataṃ japtvā yatkiñcitsvādubhojanam |
tadvalirdīyate tasyai vaṭādho māsamekataḥ || 28 ||

tato devī samāgatya hastādgṛhṇāti bhojanam |
tatraiva sā varaṃ datte nityaṃ sānnidhyakārakam || 29 ||

atītānāgataṃ karma svasthāsvasthaṃ bravīti sā |
pratimāḥ parvatānsarvāścālayatyeva tatkṣaṇāt || 30 ||

oṃkāramukhe vidyujjihve | oṃ hrūṃ ceṭake jaya jaya svāhā |

ciñcipiśācinīsādhanam 

rocanaiḥ kuṃkumaiḥ kṣīraiḥ padmaṃ cāṣṭadalaṃ likhet |
nīrandhre bhūrjapatre ca māyābījaṃ dale dale || 31 ||

likhitvā dhārayenmūrdhni imaṃ mantraṃ tato japet |
pūrvamevāyutaṃ japtvā caivaṃ kuryātprayatnataḥ |
atītānāgataṃ sarvaṃ svapne vadati devatā || 32 ||

oṃ hrīṃ ciñcipiśācini svāhā |

karṇayakṣiṇīsādhanam 

alābumūlikāṃ puṣye tathā sarpākṣimūlikām |
grāhyābhimantritāṃ yatnādraktasūtreṇa veṣṭayet |
mūrdhni baddhvā tathāsuptaṃ vadatyeva śubhāśubham || 33 ||

oṃ namo bhagavatyai rudrāyai karṇayakṣiṇyai svāhā |

oṃ namo bhagavatyai rudrāṇyai karṇayakṣiṇyai svāhā |

vicitrāyakṣiṇīsādhanam 

lakṣamekaṃ japenmantraṃ vaṭavṛkṣatale śuciḥ |
bandhūkakusumaiḥ paścānmadhvājyaiḥ kṣīramiśritaiḥ || 34 ||

datte dhūpe daśāṃśena juhuyātpūrṇayānvitam |
tataḥ siddhā bhaveddevī vicitrā vāñchitapradā || 35 ||

oṃ vicitre vicitrarūpe siddhiṃ kuru kuru svāhā |

svapnāvatīyakṣiṇīsādhanam 

mṛdgomayairlipedbhūmiṃ kuśāṃstatra samāstaret |
pañcopacāranaivedyairdevadevīṃ prapūjayet || 36 ||

akṣasūtraṃ kare dhṛtvā pūrvamevāyutaṃ japet |
sūryakoṭisamāṃ dhyātvā rātrau pāṇitale japet |
arddharātre gate devī vārttāṃ vakti śubhāśubhām || 37 ||

oṃ hrīṃ āgaccha āgaccha cāmuṇḍe śrīṃ svāhā |

haṃsīyakṣiṇīsādhanam 

praviśya nagarasyāntaṃ lakṣasaṃkhyaṃ japecchuciḥ |
padmapatraiḥ kṛto homo ghṛtopetairdaśāṃśataḥ || 38 ||

prayacchatyañjanaṃ  haṃsī yena paśyati bhūnidhim |
sukhena taṃ ca gṛhṇāti na vighnaiḥ paribhūyate || 39 ||

oṃ haṃsi haṃsijane hrīṃ klīṃ svāhā | oṃ namo haṃsini haṃsagate māṃ svāhā iti vā |

kālakarṇīyakṣiṇīsādhanam 

lakṣasaṃkhyaṃ japenmantraṃ palāśatarujendhanaiḥ |
madhunājyaiḥ kṛte home kālakarṇī prasīdati || 40 ||

sainyadhārāstrabandhaṃ ca gatistambhakarī bhavet |
satataṃ tāṃ smarenmaṃtrī vividhaiśvaryakāriṇīm || 41 ||

oṃ hrīṃ klīṃ kālakarṇike ṭhaḥ ṭhaḥ svāhā |

madanāyakṣiṇīsādhanam 

lakṣa saṃkhyaṃ japenmatraṃ rājadvāre śuciḥ sthiraḥ |
sakṣīrairmālatīpuṣpairghṛtahomo daṃśāṃśataḥ || 42 ||

madanā yakṣiṇī siddhiṃ guṭikāṃ saṃprayacchati |
tayā mukhasthayā'dṛśyaścirasthāyī bhavennaraḥ || 43 ||

oṃ aiṃ madane madanavidrāvaṇe anaṅgasaṅgamaṃ dehi dehi krīṃ krīṃ svāhā |

lakṣmīyakṣiṇīsādhanam 

svagṛhe saṃsthito raktaiḥ karavīraprasūnakaiḥ |
lakṣamāvarttayenmantraṃ homaṃ kuryāddaśāṃśataḥ || 44 ||

home kṛte bhavetsiddhirlakṣmīnāmnī ca yakṣiṇī |
rasaṃ rasāyanaṃ divyaṃ vidhānaṃ ca prayacchati || 45 ||

oṃ aiṃ lakṣmīṃ śrīṃ kamaladhāriṇīṃ kalahaṃsī svāhā |

oṃ aiṃ lakṣmīṃ śrīṃ kamaladhāriṇīṃ kalahaṃsī svāhā |

śobhanāyakṣiṇīsādhanam 

raktamālyāmbaro mantraṃ caturdaśidine japet |
tataḥ siddhā bhaveddevī śobhanā bhogadāyinī || 46 ||

oṃ aśoka pallavākāra karatale śobhanīṃ śrīṃ kṣaḥ svāhā |

oṃ aśoka pallavākāra karatale śobhanīṃ śrīm kṣaḥ svāhā |

naṭīyakṣiṇīsādhanam 

puṣyāśokatalaṃ gatvā candanena sumaṇḍalam |
kṛtvā devīṃ samabhyarcya dhūpaṃ dattvā sahasrakam || 47 ||

mantramāvartayenmāsaṃ naktabhojī narastadā |
rātrau pūjāṃ śubhāṃ kṛtvā japenmatraṃ niśārddhake || 48 ||

naṭī devī samāgatya nidhānaṃ rasamañjanam |
dadāti mantriṇemantraṃ divyayogaṃ ca niścitam || 49 ||

oṃ hrīṃ naṭi mahānaṭi svarūpavati svāhā |

padminīyakṣiṇīsādhanam 

sraksugandhigṛhasthāne candanena sumaṇḍalam |
kṛtvā hastapramāṇena pūjayettatra padminīm || 50 ||

dhūpaṃ sagugguluṃ dattvā japenmantraṃ sahasrakam |
māsamekaṃ tataḥ pūjāṃ kṛtvā rātrau punarjjapet || 51 ||

arddharātre gate devī samāgatya prayacchati |
nidhānaṃ divyayogaṃ ca tasmānmantrī sukhī bhavet || 52 ||

oṃ hrīṃ (vā krīṃ) padminī svāhā | 

oṃ hrīṃ padminī svāhā | oṃ krīṃ padminī svāhā |


mahāyakṣiṇī 

oṃ hrīṃ klīṃ aiṃ śrīmahāyakṣiṇyai sarvaiśvaryapradātryai namaḥ |

kāmeśvarīyakṣiṇī 

oṃ namo āgaccha kāmeśvari svāhā |

jayāyakṣiṇī 

oṃ aiṃ jaye yakṣiṇī sarvakāryaṃ sādhanaṃ kuru kuru svāhā |

kalāvatīyakṣiṇī 

oṃ hrīṃ kalāvati maithunapriye āgaccha svāhā |


[iti śrīnityanāthaviracite kāmaratne yakṣiṇīsādhanaṃ nāma pañcadaśopadeśaḥ] || 15 ||



[ṣoḍaśopadeśaḥ] 

atha bājīkaraṇādiprayogasiddhayerasādiśodhanam 

atha rasaśodhanam 

palānnyūnaṃ na karttavyaṃ rasasaṃskāramuttamam |
aghoreṇaiva mantreṇa rasarājasya pūjanam || 1 ||

oṃ aghorebhyotha ghorebhyo ghoraghoratarebhyaḥ |
sarvataḥ sarvasarvebhyo namaste'stu rudrarūpebhyaḥ || 2 ||

aghorebhyotha ghorebhyo ghoraghoratarebhyaḥ |

sarvataḥ sarvasarvebhyo namaste'stu rudrarūpebhyaḥ || 1-2 ||

kumāryāścaniśācūṇairdinaṃ sūtaṃ vimardayet |
pātayetpātanāyantre samyakśuddho bhavedrasaḥ || 3 ||

athavā hiṅgulātsūtaṃ grāhayettannigadyate |
pāribhadrarasaiḥ peṣyaṃ hiṃgulaṃ yāmamātrakam || 47 ||

jambīrāṇāṃ dravairvātha pātyaṃ pātālayantrake |
taṃ sūtaṃ yojayedyoge saptakañcukavarjitam || 5 ||

sūtasya daśamāṃśaṃ tu gandhaṃ dattvā vimardayet |
jambīrotthadravairyāmaṃ pātyaṃ pātālayantrake || 6 ||

punarmardyaṃ punaḥ pātyaṃ saptavāraṃ viśuddhaye |
ityevaṃ śuddhayaḥ khyātāstāsāmekāṃ tu kārayet || 7 ||

uparyāpo hyadho vahnirmadhye ca rasapiṣṭikā |
kramādagnirvidadhyāttatpātālaṃ yantramucyate || 8 ||

iti rasaśodhanam |

atha rasamāraṇam 

uktaṃ sarvasya sūtasya tapta khalve vimardanam |
ajāśakṛttuṣāgniṃ tu bhūgarte tritayaṃ kṣipet || 9 ||

tasyopari sthitaṃ khalvaṃ taptakhalvamidaṃ bhavet |
khalvaṃ lohamayaṃ śastaṃ pāṣāṇoṣṇamathāvi vā || 10 ||

ajīrṇaṃ cāpyabījaṃ vā yaḥ sūtaṃ ghātayennaraḥ |
brahmahā sudurācāro mantradrohī maheśvari || 11 ||

rāmaṭhaṃ pañcalavaṇaṃ tathā kṣāracatuṣṭayam |
trikaṭuṃ śṛṅgaveraṃ ca mātuluṅgaṃ rasāplutam || 12 ||

piṇḍamadhye rasaṃ dattvā svedayetsaptavāsarān |
sāranāle tu mṛdbhāṇḍe grāsārthī jāyate dhruvam || 13 ||

etadeva rasaṃ yatnājjambīradravasaṃyutam |
dinaikaṃ dhārayedgharme mṛtpātre vā mṛto bhavet || 14 ||

grāsaṃ tatraiva dātavyaṃ svarṇaśuddhiḥ śanaiḥ śanaiḥ |
catuṣṣaṣṭyādi tulyāṃśaṃ deyaṃ jīrṇañca cālayet || 15 ||

catuṣṣaṣṭyaṃśakaṃ cādau dvātriṃśattadanantaram |
punarviṃśatimaṃ grāhyaṃ dviraṣṭadvādaśaṃ kramāt || 16 ||

aṣṭamāṃśaṃ caturthaṃ vāpyarddhaṃ caiva samāṃśakam |
pratigrāse taptakhalve dinamamlena mardayet || 17 ||

taṃ kṣipeccāraṇāyaṃtre jambīranīrasaṃyutam |
tadyantraṃ dhārayedgharme dinaṃ syājjārito rasaḥ || 18 ||

taṃ chāgakṣīragomūtrasnukkṣīrāmlai pralepite |
dṛḍhavastre bahirbaddhvā mṛdghaṭe svedayedbudhaḥ || 19 ||

kāñjikākṣāramūtrairvā dolāyantre tvaharniśam |
tamuddhataṃ rasaṃ devi khalve saṃśodhayetkṣaṇāt || 20 ||

saṃmardya pūrvavatkhalve yantre liptapuṭe punaḥ |
krameṇānena deveśi tribhirgrāsaiḥ prajīryate || 21 ||

yāvattena yadā tasmāttāvattena vimardayet |
pratigrāse taptakhalve yathāśaktyā ca jārayet || 22 ||

taṃ jīrṇaṃ mārayetsūtaṃ māraṇaṃ kathyate dravaiḥ |
taṃ hi sarvarasopetaṃ piṣṭvā khalve vimardayet || 23 ||

sūtaṃ gandhakasaṃyuktaṃ dinānte tannirodhayet |
puṭayedbhūdhare yantre dinānte tatsamuddharet || 24 ||

kṛṣṇadhattūrataile sūto mardyo dviyāmakam |
dinaikaṃ tatpacedyantre kacchapākhye na saṃśayaḥ || 25 ||

mṛtaḥ sūto bhavetsadyaḥ sarvarogeṣu yojayet |
rasagandhaṃ samaṃ mardya dinaṃ nirguṇḍikādravaiḥ |
cakramūṣānvite dhmāte bhasma sūtaṃ bhavenmalam || 26 ||

ṭaṅkaṇaṃ madhu lākṣā ca kurṇāguñjāyuto rasaḥ || 27 ||

mardayedbhṛṅgajadrāvairdinaikaṃ cāndhayetpunaḥ |
dhmāto bhasmatvamāpnoti śuddhaḥ sphaṭikasannibhaḥ || 28 ||

dvipalaṃ sūtarājasya palaikaṃ gandhakasya ca |
mardayenmārkavadrāvairdinamekaṃ nirantaram || 29 ||

ruddhvā tadbhūdhare yantre dinaikaṃ mārayetpuṭāt |
ityevaṃ jārite sūte māraṇaṃ parikīrtitam || 30 ||

athavā grāsayogyaṃ tu nihanyātsānvitaṃ rasam |
sūtakaṃ ghanasattvaṃ ca mardayetkaṃguṇīdravaiḥ || 31 ||

dinaikaṃ golakaṃ taṃ ca śoṣayedātape khare |
garbhayaṃtragataṃ pacyāt tridinaṃ hi tuṣāgninā || 32 ||

karīṣāgnau divārātrau pacetadbhasmatāṃ nayet |
sūtaṃ svarṇaṃ vyoma śaṅkhaṃ samaṃ rambhādravairdinam || 33 ||

mardayedvījasaṃyuktaṃ carṣicāraṇayantrake |
sarvakairmūlikādrāvādanamekaṃ tu mardayet || 34 ||

garbhayantragataṃ pācyaṃ mriyate pūrvavadrasaḥ |
brahmadaṇḍī meghanādo citrakaṃ kaṭutumbikā || 35 ||

vajravallī balā kanyā trikuṭārke snuhīpayaḥ |
kandorambhā ca nirguṇḍī lajjā jātī jayantikā || 36 ||

viṣṇukrāntā hastiśuṇḍī dadrughno bhṛṅgarāṭ paṭuḥ |
guḍūcī lāṅgalī nīrakaṇā kālī mahoragā || 37 ||

kākamācī ca dantī ca etāḥ pāradamārakāḥ |
vyastāḥ samastā vā sarvā deyā hyaṣṭadaśādhikāḥ |
uktasthāne prayoktavyā rasarājasya siddhaye || 38 ||

atha garbhayantraprakāraḥ 

caturaṅguladīrghā tu mṛnmayī dṛḍhamūṣikā |
tryaṅgulīmadhyavitāre vartulaṃ kārayenmukham || 39 ||

lonasya viṃśatirbhāgā eko bhāgastu gugguloḥ |
suślakṣṇaṃ peṣayitvā tu toyaṃ dattvā punaḥ punaḥ || 40 ||

mukhalepaṃ tataḥ kuryādrasaṃ tatra vinikṣipet |
andhayitvā puṭaṃ deyaṃ garbhayantramidaṃ bhavet || 41 ||

iti rasamāraṇam |

atha hiṃgulaśuddhiḥ 

meṣīkṣīrāmlavargābhyāṃ daradaṃ dharmabhāvitam |
saptavāraṃ prayatnena śuddhimāyāti niścitam || 42 ||

iti hiṃgulaśuddhiḥ |

atha gandhakaśuddhiḥ 

śuklapakṣasamacchāyo navanītasamaprabhaḥ |
masṛṇaḥ kaṭhinaḥ snigdhaḥ śreṣṭho gandhaka ucyate || 43 ||

ghṛtaṃ bhāṇḍe payaḥ kṣiptvā mukhaṃ vastreṇa veṣṭayet |
tatpūrvaṃ cūrṇitaṃ gandhaṃ kṣiptvā tasyopari nyaset || 44 ||

kapālamekamuttānaṃ gandhakasyāviyogi tat |
dugdhabhāṇḍaṃ nyasya bhūmau deyamūrdhvapuṭaṃ laghu || 45 ||

tataḥ kṣīre drutaṃ gandhaṃ śuddhayogeṣu yojayet |
gandhaṃ ghṛte vipaktavyaṃ yāvattailanibhaṃ bhavet || 46 ||

vastreṇāntaritaṃ kṛtvā cālayestriphalāmbhasi |
evaṃ gandhakaśuddhiḥ syāttatto yogeṣu yojayet || 47 ||

iti gandhakaśuddhi |

atha abhrakaśuddhiḥ 

kṛṣṇaḥ pītaḥ śvetarakto yojyo yogarasāyane |
pinākaṃ darduraṃ nāgaṃ vajraṃ ceti caturvidham || 48 ||

pinākādyāstrayo varjyā vajraṃ yatnātsamāharet |
muñcatyagnau ca nikṣiptaḥ pināko dalasañcayam || 49 ||

ajñānādbhakṣaṇāttasya mahāduḥkhaprado bhavet |
darduro'gnau vinikṣiptaḥ kurute darduradhvanim || 50 ||

tacca bhakṣaṇamātreṇa nānārogān prayacchati |
nāgaścāgnisthitaḥ sadyaḥ phūtkāraṃ ca vimuñcati || 51 ||

sa ca dehagato nityaṃ vyādhiṃ kuryādbhagandaram |
vajrābhrakaṃ tu vahnau ca na kiñcidvikriyāṃ vrajet || 52 ||

tasmādvajrābhrakaṃ yojyaṃ vyādhivārdhakyamṛtyujit |
dhamedvajrābhrakaṃ vahnau yāvadagninibhaṃ bhavet || 53 ||

gokṣīre ca tataḥ secyaṃ gokṣīre ca punaḥ punaḥ |
bhinna pātre ca tatkṛtvā meghanādadravāmbunā |
bhāvayedaṣṭayāmaṃ ca jāyate doṣavarjitam || 54 ||

athavābhrasya bhāgau dvau mustā caikaṃ jalaissaha |
tridinaṃ sthāpayetpātre tataḥ sūkṣmaṃ papeṣayet || 55 ||

etadabhrakacūrṇaṃ tu nistuṣaṃ vrīhisaṃyutam |
vastreṇa baddhvā sāranāle bhāṇḍamadhye vimardayet || 56 ||

hastābhyāṃ svayamāyāti yāvadamlaṃ tu reṇutām |
adoṣābhragataṃ śuddhaṃ śuṣkaṃ dhānyābhrakaṃ bhavet || 57 ||

dhānyābhrakaṃ ravikṣīre ravimūladravaiśca vā |
dinamardhapuṭe pacyātsaptadhainaṃ mṛtaṃ bhavet || 58 ||

dhānyābhrakasya bhāgaikaṃ dvau bhāgau ṭaṅkaṇasya tu || 59 ||

piṣṭvā tadandhamūṣāyāṃ ruddhvā tīvrāgninā dhamet |
svabhāvaśītalaṃ cūrṇaṃ sarvayogeṣu yojayet || 60 ||

iti abhrakaśuddhiḥ |

atha amṛtīkaraṇam 

sarveṣāṃ ghātitānāṃ tu hyamṛtīkaraṇaṃ śuṛṇu |
triphalotthakaṣāyasya palānyādāya ṣoḍaśa || 61 ||

goghṛtasya palānyaṣṭau mṛtābhrasya palān daśa |
ekīkṛtvā lohapātre pācayenmṛduvahninā || 62 ||

dravairjīrṇaṃ samādāya sarvayogeṣu yojayet || 63 ||

iti amṛtīkaraṇam |

atha abhrakasattvapātanam 

cūrṇīkṛtaṃ gaganapatramathāranāle dhṛtvā dinaikamatha śoṣya ca sūraṇasya |
bhāvyaṃ rasastadanu mūlarase kadalyā vedāṃśaṭaṅkaṇayutaṃ śapharīsametam || 64 ||

piṇḍīkṛtaṃ tu bahudhā mahiṣīmalena saṃśoṣya koṣṭhagatamāśu dhamedyatāgnau |
bhastrīdvayena ca tato vamate hi sattvaṃ pāṣāṇadhātugatamātramasaṃśayosti || 65 ||

iti abhrakasattvapātanam |

atha manaḥśilāśuddhiḥ 

jayantībhṛṅgarājotthaṃ raktāgastirasaiḥ śilām || 66 ||

dolāyantre pacedyāmaṃ yāmaṃ chāgotthamūtrakaiḥ |
kṣālayedāranālena sarvayogeṣu yojayet || 67 ||

iti manaḥśilāśuddhiḥ |

atha haratālaśuddhiḥ 

tālakaṃ poṭalīṃ baddhvā sacūrṇaṃ kāñjike kṣipet |
dolāyantreṇa yāmaikaṃ tataḥ kūṣmāṇḍaje rase || 68 ||

tilataile pacedyāmaṃ yāmaṃ ca triphalājalaiḥ |
evaṃ yantre caturyāmaṃ pācyaṃ śuddhyati tālakam || 69 ||


iti haratālaśuddhiḥ |

atha tutthaśuddhiḥ 

viṣṭhayā madayattutthaṃ mārjārakakapotayoḥ |
daśāṃśaṃ ṭaṅkaṇaṃ dattvā pācyaṃ mṛdupuṭe tu tat |
puṭaṃ dadhnā puṭaṃ kṣaudre deyaṃ tutthaṃ viśuddhaye || 70 ||

iti tutthaśuddhiḥ |

atha kāśīśaśaṃkhanābhiśuddhiḥ 

gharme śuddhyati kāśīśaṃ dinaṃ jambīrabhāvitam || 71 ||

śaṅkhanābhaṃ ca saṃdagdhvā bhāvyamamlena saptadhā |
prakṣālyaṃ grāhayettāvacchuddhimāyāti nānyathā || 72 ||

iti kāśīśaśaṃkhanābhiśuddhiḥ |

atha śātakumbhādidhātuśodhanam 

mṛttikāmātuluṅgāmlaiḥ pañcavāsarabhāvitā |
sabhasmalavaṇairhema śodhayetpuṭapākataḥ || 73 ||

valmīkamṛttikā dhūmaṃ gairikaṃ ceṣṭikā paṭuḥ |
ityetā mṛttikāḥ pañca jambīrairāranālakaiḥ || 74 ||

piṣṭvā liptasvarṇapatraṃ puṭena pariśuddhyati |
nāgena ṭaṅkaṇenaiva dhūme śuddhyati raupyakam || 75 ||

khaṭikā lavaṇaṃ takrairāranālaiśca peṣayet |
tena liptaṃ tāmrapatraṃ taptaṃ taptaṃ niṣecayet || 76 ||

khadirāranālatakrāntanirguṇḍī na viśuddhaye || 77 ||

rohaṇaṃ rājavaṃ caivaṃ tṛtīyaṃ ca puṭīrakam |
iti tīkṣṇaṃ tridhā taṃ ca śodhayedyogasiddhaye || 78 ||

iti śātakumbhādidhātuśodhanam |

atha tutthaṭaṅkaṇakācalauhaśodhanam 

śaśaraktena saṃliptaṃ trivāraṃ cāgnipācitam |
tutthaṭaṅkaṇakācairvā dhāmitaṃ śuddhamṛcchati || 79 ||

athavā lohacūrṇaṃ tu gomūtraiḥ ṣaḍguṇaiḥ pacet |
prakṣālayedāranālaiḥ śoṣyaṃ śuddhimavāpnuyāt || 80 ||

sarveṣāṃmate māraṇam 

śuddhasūtaṃ samaṃ svarṇaṃ khalve kuryācca golakam |
adhorddhaṃ gandhakaṃ dattvā sarvatulyaṃ nirudhya ca || 81 ||

viṃśadvanopalairdeyaṃ puṭānyeva caturdaśa |
nirutthaṃ jāyate bhasma gandhaṃ deyaṃ punaḥ punaḥ || 82 ||

raupyaṃ patraṃ caturbhāgaṃ gandhaṃ bhāgena lepayet |
jambīrīnarapiṣṭena pañcaviṃśadvanopalaiḥ || 83 ||

baddhvā tribhiḥ puṭe pacyādgandhaṃ deyaṃ punaḥ punaḥ ||
mriyate nātra sandehastattatkarmaṇi yojayet || 84 ||

tāmratulyena gandhena hyamlapiṣṭena lepayet |
kaṇṭavedhīkṛtaṃ patramandhayitvā puṭe pacet || 85 ||

uddhṛtya cūrṇayettasmin pādāṃśaṃ gandhakaṃ kṣipet |
jambīrairāranālairvā piṣṭvā baddhvā puṭe pacet || 86 ||

evaṃ catuḥ puṭaiḥ pācyaṃ gandhaṃ deyaṃ punaḥ punaḥ |
mātuluṅgadravaiḥ piṣṭvā puṭamekaṃ pradāpayet || 87 ||

athāsya doṣaharaṇam 

sitaśarkarayāpyaikaṃ puṭaṃ deyaṃ mṛtaṃ bhavet || 88 ||

mṛtaṃ tāmraṃ tu jambīrairyāmaṃ khalve vimardayet |
tadgolaṃ sūraṇe kṣiptvā ruddhvā sarvaṃ ca lepayet |
śuṣkaṃ gajapuṭe pācyaṃ nirdoṣaṃ sarvarogahṛt || 89 ||

atha lauhamāraṇam 

nāyaṃ pacet pañcapalādarvāgūrddhva trayodaśa |
ādau mantrastathā karma kartavyaṃ mantra ucyate || 90 ||

oṃ amṛtodbhavāya svāhā(-) iti mardanamantraḥ | anena mantreṇa lohasya tatsādhakasya rakṣā kartavyā | oṃ namaścaṇḍacakrapāṇaye svāhā yakṣasenādhipataye suragurumahāvidyābalāya svāhā | anena mantreṇa baliṃ dattvā tataḥ karmaṃ kuryāt ||

dantīpatradravaṃ tasyāṃ lohacūrṇaṃ dinodaye |
gharme dhārya dinaṃ kāṃsye dravaṃ deyaṃ punaḥ punaḥ || 91 ||

ruddhvā rātrau puṭe pācyaṃ prātardravaiśca bhāvayet |
evamaṣṭadinaṃ kuryāttrividhaṃ mriyate tu yaḥ || 92 ||

mṛtasya lakṣaṇam 

madhvājyaṃ mṛtalohaṃ ca raupyaṃ sampuṭake kṣipet |
ruddhvā dhmāte tu saṃgrāhyaṃ raupyaṃ cetpūrvamānakam |
tadā lohaṃ mṛtaṃ vidyādanyathā sādhayetpunaḥ || 93 ||

atha śodhanam 

gandhakaṃ tulyakaṃ lohaṃ tulyaṃ khalve vimardayet || 94 ||

dinaikaṃ kanyakādrāvai ruddhvā gajapuṭe pacet |
ityevaṃ sarvalohānāṃ kartavyaṃ syānnirutthanam || 95 ||

athāsyāmṛtīkaraṇam 

ghṛtatulyaṃ mṛtaṃ lohaṃ lohapātragataṃ pacet |
jīrṇe ghṛte samādāya yogavāheṣu yojayet || 96 ||

atha bhūnāgasattvam 

sadyo bhūnāgamādāya kṣālayecchithilaṃ budhaḥ |
athavā kukkuṭaṃ vīraṃ kṛtvā mandiramāśritam || 97 ||

malamūtraṃ gṛhītena sadambu prathamāṃśakam |
āloḍya ṭaṅkaṃ madhvājyaigharme sarvārthamādarāt |
muñcettu tāmravatsattvametad bhūnāgasattvakam || 98 ||

iti nāgavargaḥ |

atha lavaṇapañcakam 

sāmudraṃ saindhavaṃ kācaṃ cullikā ca suvarcalam |
mūlikā navakṣāraśca jñeyaṃ lavaṇapañcakam || 99 ||

iti lavaṇapañcakam |

atha kṣārāḥ 

trikṣāraṣṭaṅkaṇakṣāro yavakṣāraśca svarjikā || 100 ||

iti kṣārāḥ |

atha vṛkṣakṣāraḥ 

tilāpāmārgakadalīpalāśaḥ śigrupauṇḍrakauḥ 
mūlakārdrakacitrāśca sarvamantaḥ puṭepacet || 101 ||

samāloḍya jalairbaddhvā vastre grāhyamadhojalam |
śodhayetpācayedagnaumṛdbhāṇḍena tu tajjalam |
grāhyaṃ kṣārāvaśeṣaṃ tu vṛkṣakṣāramidaṃ smṛtam || 102 ||

iti vṛkṣakṣārāḥ |

atha viḍaḥ 

mūlikārdrakavahnīnāṃ kṣāraṃ gomūtrayojitam |
vastraṃ pūtaṃ jalaṃ grāhyaṃ gandhakaṃ tena bhāvayet || 103 ||

saptavāraṃ khare gharme bījo'yaṃ hemajāraṇe |
kanyāhayāridhattūradravairbhāvyaṃ tu gandhakam || 104 ||

śatavāraṃ khare dharme bījo'yaṃ hemajāraṇe |
gandhakaṃ śaṅkhacūrṇaṃ vā gomūtraiḥ śatabhāvitam |
bījo'yaṃ jāraṇe śreṣṭho bījānāṃ drāvaṇe hitaḥ || 105 ||

atha amlavargaḥ 

jambīraṃ nāgaraṅgaśca mātuluṅgāmbavetasam |
cāṅgerī caṇaśukraśca amlavargaḥ prakīrtitaḥ || 106 ||

iti amlavargaḥ |

atha vajramūṣā 

valmīkamṛttikābhāgaṃ gavāsthituṣabhasmanoḥ |
bhāgaṃ rasaṃ samādāyaṃ vajramūṣā viracya te || 107 ||

[iti śrīnityanāthaviracite kāmaratne rasādiśodhanaṃ nāma ṣoḍaśopadeśaḥ] || 16 ||

(###########   END OF FILE  #######)
